The writer ceiling is even more direct. On a Zep graph written by GPT-4o-mini, GPT-4o-mini, GPT-6 Luna, and Claude Opus 5.5 score 43%, 43%, and 45% respectively. On a graph written by Claude Opus 5.5, the same readers score 66%, 71%, and 87%. Mem0 shows the same pattern with smaller gaps: from a GPT-4o-mini store, the three readers get 78%, 83%, and 84%; from a Claude Opus 5.5 store, they get 89%, 91%, and 95%. This is strong evidence that the bottleneck is the fidelity of memory construction, not just the sophistication of the downstream reader.

One more number is worth keeping in mind: Pi with Claude Opus 5.5 sits on the best cost–accuracy frontier point, at 99% accuracy for $6.95 per rollout, versus 95% and $17.84 for full context with the same model. Pi reads about 10k input tokens per question, with 7.9k cached, while full context reads about 28.9k. Its only miss is tied to an error in the LoCoMo answer key.7f:T439,This is a good skim paper. Start with the setup and keep the scope in mind: one conversation, one run per cell, and a GPT-4o-mini judge. The study is intentionally small, so the claims should stay local. Then read the three main result sections in order: the weak-model patch result, the writer-ceiling result, and the frontier result with Claude Opus 5.5.

If you only have 15–20 minutes, focus on Figure 2 and Figure 3. Figure 2 makes the build-cost argument concrete; Figure 3 is the real conceptual contribution because it separates the memory writer from the reader and shows where the ceiling comes from. The three takeaways to retain are: Mem0 can help weak models, stronger models often do better with raw-history access, and lossy extracted memory is limited by the model that wrote it.

For deeper follow-up, connect this paper to long-context training, retrieval-augmented systems, and the broader question of whether conversation history should be compressed into a structured state at all. The paper does not settle that question, but it gives a useful failure mode.80:T7eb,方法分三阶段。Stage 1 先构造 evaluator。输入是任务指令、初始状态和只读观测接口。系统里有 Test-Suite Generator 和 Verifier Evolver 两个模型角色。前者从指令中抽取应满足的状态约束，生成正例、负例和 adversarial near-miss challenge states；这些 challenge 不是纯文本描述，而是通过 deterministic category adapters 物化成真实环境状态。后者基于这些 challenge states 合成 task-specific executable evaluator。这里的关键不是追求一次性完全复现专家 verifier，而是先得到一个能通过内部 gate、可用于搜索的 evaluator。Stage 2 用 frozen evaluator 做 workflow evolution。系统维护 persistent multi-alternative workflow nodes，也就是每个节点保留多个行为候选，而不是把一次 rollout 当成一次性轨迹；这样做更像在程序空间里做局部演化。Workflow Executor 执行候选 workflow，frozen evaluator 给出稳定 fitness signal，Workflow Evolver 再据此改写 workflow。作者刻意把 evaluator 冻结，避免搜索过程因为 reward function 漂移而失稳。Stage 3 做 evaluator revision。外部 benchmark verifier 不进入搜索环，只在环外做三件事：准入 evaluator、识别内部接受但外部失败的 false positives、以及最终测量结果。对于 false positive，系统比较 strong executor 与 weak executor 的最终状态差异，把这些 instruction-grounded differences 挖成 paired counterfactual test cases，再让 Verifier Evolver 生成修订版 evaluator。修订版只有在 cumulative regression 通过后才会被提升并再次冻结。这个设计里最有意思的点有两个：一是 evaluator-first，而不是 search-first；二是 revision 信号来自 state difference，而不是直接模仿外部 verifier。论文还讨论了 observability 和 state representation 的限制：如果观测接口看不到关键状态，或者状态难以规范化表示，自动 verifier 的上限会明显受限。81:T817,The framework has three stages. Stage 1 constructs the evaluator. Given a task instruction, initial state, and read-only observation interface, a Test-Suite Generator derives positive, negative, and adversarial near-miss challenge states implied by the instruction. These are not just textual descriptions: deterministic category adapters materialize them into actual environment states. A Verifier Evolver then synthesizes a task-specific executable evaluator over these states. The goal is not to perfectly reproduce an expert verifier in one shot, but to obtain an evaluator that passes internal gates and is good enough to support search. Stage 2 performs workflow evolution under a frozen evaluator. The system maintains persistent multi-alternative workflow nodes, so the search space is structured more like evolving a reusable workflow graph than sampling one-off trajectories. A Workflow Executor runs candidate workflows, the frozen evaluator provides a stable fitness signal, and a Workflow Evolver updates the workflow accordingly. Freezing the evaluator is a deliberate design choice to avoid reward drift during optimization. Stage 3 revises the evaluator. An external benchmark verifier is kept outside the search loop and is used only for admission, false-positive detection, and final measurement. When an internally accepted state fails external verification, the system compares strong-executor and weak-executor end states, mines instruction-grounded differences into paired counterfactual test cases, and asks the Verifier Evolver to produce a revised evaluator. The revision is promoted only after cumulative regression checks and is then frozen again. Two design choices stand out: evaluator-first rather than search-first, and revision driven by state differences rather than direct imitation of the external verifier. The paper also makes clear that observability and state representation are hard limits: if the observation interface misses critical state or the state cannot be normalized cleanly, automatic verifier synthesis degrades quickly.82:T6a6,The main controlled study covers all 30 MCPMark Filesystem tasks. Stage 1 successfully constructs evaluators for 30/30 tasks and generates 141 challenge states, including 62 adversarial near-misses. On these generated suites, agreement with the external verifier is 141/141. More importantly, on 122 held-out states later produced by executor agents, agreement is 113/122, showing some generalization beyond the construction set. With GLM-5.2 as the weaker executor, workflow evolution improves externally verified success from 6/30 at round 0 to 18/30. With Claude as the stronger executor, success rises from 27/30 to 30/30. This suggests the evaluator is especially useful as a workflow-organizing signal for weaker models. The paper also reports 3 internally accepted but externally invalid GLM-5.2 states, i.e. false positives. In all three cases, strong–weak state differences produce regression-validated evaluator revisions that remove the original errors; in structure_mirror, the weak executor reaches an externally valid state after a single revision. The PostgreSQL extension covers 21 standard tasks: 15 synthesized evaluators meet the Stage 2 admission threshold, and workflow evolution closes 3 weak-executor gaps that the strong executor already solves at round 0, while the remaining 12 eligible tasks do not reach internal convergence within budget. This contrast matters: the method looks more mature in environments with clearer observable state structure, and less so when semantics and constraints become harder to encode. Stress tests on Playwright, Notion, and GitHub further expose limits from observability, unstable state representation, and hard-to-enumerate side effects.83:T472,For a skim-level read, focus on four parts. First, read the Introduction and Problem Setting carefully to internalize that the target is environment-state correctness, not response judging. Second, inspect the challenge-state construction and category adapters in Stage 1; that is where the evaluator becomes deterministic rather than an LLM opinion. Third, look at the Stage 2/3 interfaces, especially why the evaluator is frozen during search and why revision is driven by strong/weak end-state differences instead of direct access to the external verifier. Fourth, compare the Filesystem and PostgreSQL results to understand the method’s operating regime and failure modes. Useful questions while reading: which constraints can be reliably extracted from instructions, which cannot; whether false positives come more from missing specification or missing observability; and whether this setup could serve as the reward layer for RL or continual self-improvement. About 20–30 minutes is enough to get the main idea; spend extra time on the held-out agreement numbers and the revision case studies if verifier synthesis is your area.84:T579,The method is a two-stage loop: idea discovery and idea control. (1) Formalization: evolutionary discovery is cast as a POMDP with a hidden mechanism θ_sci; proposing a program yields a score from an evaluator. An “idea” is defined as a high-level mechanistic property of programs that causally drives score differences. The theory section argues that without identifying such ideas, programs are effectively unrelated and search degenerates toward exhaustive exploration; conversely, identifying ideas requires deliberate contrastive exploration (programs with/without an idea). (2) Idea-factor extraction: built on COAT, the system searches for high-level factors that explain score differences by training/using a pairwise comparator over archived programs—given (p_i, p_j), predict which scores higher. Factors that improve this pairwise ordering are admitted as reusable idea factors. The key design choice is pairwise ranking (difference-explaining) rather than raw score regression. (3) Idea-level control: each admitted idea factor becomes an arm in a multi-armed bandit that selects which idea to emphasize when generating new candidates. The algorithm reserves capacity for random exploration and triggers idea-guided exploration on plateaus, explicitly pushing the LLM toward underrepresented ideas in top programs to maintain the contrasts needed for reliable factor identification.a:["$","main",null,{"className":"flex-1 overflow-y-auto","children":["$","div",null,{"className":"mx-auto max-w-4xl px-4 py-8 sm:px-6 xl:max-w-6xl","children":["$","$L14",null,{"data":{"date":"2026-10-23","generated_at":"2026-10-23T00:38:13.795218+00:00","stats":{"total":121,"starred":6,"must_read":12,"skim":65,"glance":44,"by_category":{"pretraining_data":2,"industry":1,"llm_pretrain":4,"scaling":2,"architecture":13,"efficient":11,"eval":16,"multimodal":6,"multimodal_gen":9,"post_train":20,"mechanism":21,"coding_agent":1,"agent":15},"trending_repos":0,"trending_hf":8,"idea_spikes":2,"fetch_status":{"arxiv":"ok","huggingface":"failed","blog":"ok","twitter":"empty","github_trending":"ok","hf_trending":"ok"},"fetch_errors":{"huggingface":"exhausted 5 retries; last error: Client error '400 Bad Request' for url 'https://huggingface.co/api/daily_papers?date=2026-10-23'\nFor more information check: https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/HTTP/Status/400"}},"categories":[{"id":"pretraining_data","emoji":"📚","name":"预训练数据","description":"以真实预训练数据为核心贡献的工作：数据采集/清洗/去重、数据配比(data mixture)、数据筛选/质量评估、语料构建、数据课程(data curriculum)、数据重复(repetition)、Tokenizer/词表设计、成员推断/数据检测。合成/自举数据归 synthetic_data；训练 recipe/优化器归 llm_pretrain；评测资产归 eval","papers":[{"id":"2610.28838","title":"How to Use Parallel Data for Multilingual Pretraining","abstract":"High-quality human-translated parallel corpora can improve cross-lingual transfer, but are typically too small for large-scale pretraining. 截断是否会明显伤性能。结论基本都支持 CSD 的设计。全文给人的感觉是，收益不是靠某个特殊 tokenizer 对偶然成立，而是在多家族组合上较一致。","reading_guide":"泛读时先抓三处。\n\n第一，看 Introduction 里对 GOLD、X-Token、ALM 的问题拆解。这里决定了 CSD 为什么不是简单换个 loss，而是重新定义比较对象。\n\n第二，看 distillation: teacher and student do not share either token boundaries or output vocabularies, so standard next-token KL is not even defined on a common support. Prior methods each give up something important. GOLD only matches shared string-identical tokens. X-Token requires tokenizer-pair-specific objective choices. ALM is more uniform, but collapses all unobserved alternatives into a single residual event, which removes much of the teacher’s fine-grained dark knowledge. CSD aims to build a unified, normalized, tokenizer-agnostic distribution matching objective that preserves more of the teacher’s alternative continuation structure.","importance":"For pretraining and model transfer, the contribution is practical and conceptually clean. First, it reframes cross-tokenizer KD as a probability-space construction problem rather than a collection of heuristics. Second, it shows that tokenizer mismatch does not have to block logits distillation, which matters for cross-family transfer, heterogeneous teacher-student setups, and distilling from stronger or specialized models into a target architecture. Third, the gains are not only over prior cross-tokenizer KD baselines, but often over continued pretraining and same-tokenizer KD as well. That suggests the teacher distribution carries supervision beyond simply re-exposing the student to more text. A Gradient Self Outer-Product Analysis at Initialization","abstract":"Muon orthogonalizes a weight matrix's momentum before each step, and on neural networks this simple preconditioner beats AdamW in a wide range of tasks. Existing analyses explain Muon within general optimization frameworks or on a few specific models, and many of them view orthogonalization as a preconditioner. Since the Hessian is the optimal preconditioner under a quadratic approximation of the loss, these works leave a basic question open: what makes orthogonalization suited to the Hessian of","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"llm_pretrain","cover_url":"/covers/2610_28975.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"解释 Muon 这类“对动量做正交化”的优化器，为什么在神经网络上会表现得像一个合适的二阶预条件器：具体来说，Muon 隐式预条件矩阵 (V V^T)^(-1/2) ⊗ I 为什么会与神经网络层内 Hessian 的“按输出神经元分块的行块对角占优（row-block diagonally dominant）”结构相匹配。论文把问题进一步还原为：在初始化附近，为什么梯度自外积 G G^T（或动量 V V^T）会呈现对角占优，并且这种占优如何随隐藏维度/宽度增长而增强。","importance":"对大模型预训练的直接价值在于“训练动力学与优化器选择”的解释层：Muon 在大模型训练中被观察到能稳定、并在不少任务上优于 AdamW，但此前多是抽象优化视角（范数最速下降、信赖域、谱约束等），难以回答“为什么神经网络的曲率结构刚好适配这种正交化”。这篇工作给出一个更贴近网络结构的答案：在宽度渐近下，初始化处的 E[G G^T] 会对角化，从而 Muon 的 Kronecker 预条件器天然呈现与 Hessian 相同的行块结构。它不直接给出新的训练 recipe，但为“宽模型更适合某类结构化预条件/近似二阶方法”提供了可检验的结构性预测，能帮助判断 Muon 这类方法在 scaling 时为何更稳、以及可能的失效条件（不够宽、结构不满足对角化等）。","technical_approach":"核心思路是把 Muon 的“正交化动量更新”改写成显式预条件形式，并在初始化处分析该预条件器的统计结构。\n\n1) Muon 的隐式预条件器形式：对矩阵参数 W∈R^{m×n}，Muon 将动量/梯度矩阵 V 做极分解/薄 SVD 得到正交因子 D = (V V^T)^(-1/2) V。把矩阵向量化后可写为 vec(D) = ((V V^T)^(-1/2) ⊗ I_n) vec(V)。因此 Muon 等价于在一个 Kronecker 结构的度量下做下降；该度量完全由行空间的 Gram 矩阵 P = V V^T 决定。\n\n2) 结构匹配的关键：已有工作指出神经网络层内 Hessian 对“按行（输出神经元）分块”的块矩阵是对角占优的。Muon 的预条件器也只在“行维度”上混合（左乘 (V V^T)^(-1/2)），因此如果 P=V V^T 近似对角，则 Muon 的预条件器在同一分块坐标系下呈现类似的行块对角结构，从而与 Hessian 的主导结构一致。\n\n3) 论文的主要理论贡献：在初始化处（V0=G0），对四类典型模型证明 E[G G^T] 的对角项相对非对角项会随隐藏维度增长而更快增大，即“渐近对角化/对角占优”，并给出显式的宽度标度率（汇总在 Table 1，对应 Theorem 1-4）。覆盖的四类模型是：对称矩阵分解、深线性网络、两层 ReLU 网络、单层线性注意力。技术上属于在随机初始化与宽度渐近下，对梯度行向量之间相关性进行矩量/协方差级别的精细估计：对角项对应单个输出神经元梯度能量，非对角项对应不同输出神经元梯度的相关；宽度增大时相关项被平均效应压低。\n\n4) 经验验证：先在可控仿真中验证四类模型的标度率，再在真实 LLM（Qwen3.5、Llama-3.2、Granite-4.0）初始化处测量 G G^T 的对角占优随宽度增强的趋势，用以支持“越大越对角”的现象与理论方向一致。\n\n与既有解释的区别：以往多把 Muon 放在通用优化框架里解释（选择的范数/信赖域/谱约束），这篇更强调“神经网络特有的 Hessian 行块结构 + 宽度导致的梯度 Gram 对角化”这一结构性原因，回答的是“为什么适配神经网络曲率”，而不是“Muon 在抽象类函数上收敛”。","experiments":"实验分两层：\n- 机制验证（Section 5）：对四个理论模型做数值模拟，检查对角项与非对角项的相对量级随隐藏维度增长的变化，验证 Table 1 中预测的标度趋势（论文强调的是 scaling 规律而非单点性能）。\n- 大模型初始化测量（Section 6）：在 Qwen3.5、Llama-3.2、Granite-4.0 等模型的初始化处，计算/估计梯度自外积 G G^T（或其统计量）的对角占优程度，并展示“模型越宽，对角占优越强”的现象。该部分更像是结构诊断，而不是完整预训练对比；重点是支持 Deng et al. The paper reduces this to a structural question about gradients at initialization: why the gradient self outer-product G G^T becomes diagonally dominant as width grows, and at what rates.","importance":"For large-scale pretraining, the value is interpretability of optimizer behavior rather than a new recipe. Muon is empirically strong and often more stable than AdamW, but prior explanations were mostly abstract (steepest descent under a norm, trust-region views, spectral constraints), which do not answer why neural-network curvature is particularly compatible with orthogonalization. This work provides a model-structure-based explanation: in wide-network asymptotics, E[G G^T] diagonalizes, making Muon’s Kronecker preconditioner share the same row-block structure as the Hessian. This offers a concrete, testable scaling prediction (“wider → more diagonal”) and clarifies potential failure modes (insufficient width or architectures where gradient row correlations do not vanish).","technical_approach":"$1c","experiments":"Two layers of evidence:\n- Controlled simulations (Section 5) on the four theoretical models, checking how diagonal vs off-diagonal components of G G^T scale with hidden dimension, matching the rates in Table 1.\n- Initialization diagnostics on LLMs (Section 6): compute/estimate diagonal-dominance statistics of G G^T at initialization for Qwen3.5, Llama-3.2, and Granite-4.0, showing the dominance strengthens with model width.\n\nThe provided content does not include the actual numeric readouts from Table 1 and the figures, so exact slopes/ratios and any reported magnitudes should be taken directly from those sections when reading the PDF.","reading_guide":"Skim plan (30–45 minutes):\n1) Read the Introduction derivation that turns orthogonalization into vec(D)=((V V^T)^(-1/2)⊗I)vec(V). checkpoint。这个协议降低观测噪声，并使 constant-LR 轨迹接近 linear-decay 的端点表现。固定 N 和 Ds 后，经验律可简化为 Dl 的单变量幂律，用早期 LCCP checkpoint 拟合后预测更晚阶段的 LongPPL。","experiments":"核心建模实验覆盖 5 个模型规模、109 组短上下文和长上下文预算配置，使用 linear-decay The central issue is not performance on one game, but whether heterogeneous game trajectories provide reusable experience or create interference because visuals, dynamics, controls, and goals differ across worlds.","importance":"Its value for pretraining research comes from turning embodied CPT into a controlled scaling study. Instead of changing data, model, and recipe together, the work scans model size, data budget, and mixture diversity in a structured grid. The resulting findings are practical for multimodal agent mid-training: relevant data is strongest for in-corpus worlds, diversity helps transfer to related unseen worlds, distant worlds remain hard, larger models learn the early interface faster, and single-game training can make larger models generalize worse.","technical_approach":"Gameplay is formulated as a visual decision-making sequence. At each step, the model receives prompt context, the current observation, and interaction history, then emits an optional textual reasoning trace followed by a mandatory structured action. Each action chunk spans 200 ms and is parsed into low-level keyboard and mouse commands. Observations are provided at 5 Hz and controls at 30 Hz. Each trajectory contains 20 turns, and later turns can condition on previous thoughts and actions during training. The study uses a 4 × 6 × 5 grid with 120 configurations: Qwen3.5 backbones at 0.8B, 2B, 9B, and 27B; data budgets from 50 to 1,600 hours; and five mixtures ranging from Minecraft-only to broad multi-game corpora. The total training scale is about 3.5e11 tokens and 2.2e22 FLOPs under the 6ND estimate. Evaluation is organized into in-corpus worlds, related unseen worlds, and distant unseen worlds, using both validation losses and gameplay benchmarks.","experiments":"$1f","reading_guide":"A 45- to 60-minute skim is appropriate. On Sudoku, LAW outperforms ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-21T17:25:08Z","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2610_30068.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"现有的扩散语言模型（DLMs）通常将自条件（Self-Conditioning, SC）作为辅助启发式方法，而本文探讨是否可以构建一个完全基于自条件的连续生成模型，从而避免离散扩散模型中因独立采样导致的样本质量下降和不确定性丢失问题。","importance":"提出了一种全新的连续扩散语言建模范式 LAW（Loopholing All the Way），证明了纯自条件机制在语言建模中的有效性。这为突破自回归模型（ARMs）的单向限制和现有连续 DLMs 在推理任务上的性能瓶颈提供了新路径。","technical_approach":"LAW 放弃了传统的逐步去噪和离散化过程，转而在连续空间中操作。它从均匀随机的 token 序列开始，在每一步中，利用去噪器的预测更新干净 token 的期望嵌入（Expected Embedding），并将其作为下一步的自条件状态。整个过程只在最后一步进行一次 token 解码。关键设计包括：1) 使用期望嵌入而非隐藏层表示作为 SC 状态；2) 引入温度正则化（在训练时随机缩放去噪器的 logits），这显著提升了模型在低温度采样时的性能。","experiments":"在 Sudoku 任务上，LAW 超越了之前的离散和连续扩散模型（包括 LDDM-U，96.5% vs. 91.0%）。在 Maze 任务上，LAW 在 2 到 64 NFE（网络函数评估次数）下准确率最高。在 GSM8K 上，使用 1024 NFE 采样，LAW 解决了 42.2% 的问题，优于均匀 LDDM 的 34.5%。在 OpenWebText 上，LAW 的生成困惑度（Gen. This offers a new pathway to overcome the unidirectional constraints of Autoregressive Models (ARMs) and the performance bottlenecks of existing continuous DLMs on reasoning tasks.","technical_approach":"LAW discards traditional step-by-step denoising and discretization, operating entirely in continuous space. Starting from a uniformly random sequence of tokens, it updates the Expected Embedding of the clean token using the denoiser's prediction at each step, using this as the SC state for the next step. Token decoding occurs only once at the final step. Key design choices include: 1) using the Expected Embedding instead of hidden representations as the SC state; 2) introducing temperature regularization (randomly scaling denoiser logits during training), which significantly boosts performance at low sampling temperatures.","experiments":"On Sudoku, LAW outperforms previous discrete and continuous diffusion models (e.g., 96.5% vs. 91.0% for LDDM-U). Models such as ELF now report autoregressive-level quality at a fraction of the sampling steps of discrete-token diffusion models, and this design principle -- no step-wise discretization -- is what their own ablations credit for the gain. We show that this same principle carries a hidden cost: a collapse of the lexical ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-21T03:15:30Z","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2610_29136.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文研究连续空间 diffusion LM（以 ELF、LangFlow 为代表）一个容易被常规指标掩盖的问题：模型生成的词频分布在词汇长尾上发生系统性塌缩。具体表现是：常见词的出现率几乎与人类文本一致，但最稀有的词只达到人类基线的约 0.30×，且从 105M 到 652M 参数都不随规模消失。作者进一步追问机制：为什么“全程不离散、只在最后一步解码”会让长尾词在采样过程中被抹掉？以及能否在不训练的前提下补回一个类似 AR 的‘采样接口’。","importance":"对新型语言建模范式（尤其 continuous diffusion/flow LM）的影响更直接：\n1) 提供了一个比 perplexity/entropy 更敏感的诊断维度：按语料频率分桶的词汇覆盖率/长尾保真度。它解释了为什么一些 continuous dLLM 看起来很流畅，但在专有名词、技术词、低频实体上不可靠。\n2) 指出一个结构性缺陷：连续回归式去噪在中间步骤没有“从分布到离散词”的采样接口，导致竞争候选被均值化（toward conditional mean），长尾在轨迹早期就被压扁，后期再调 temperature/top-k 也救不回来。\n3) 给出训练无关（training-free）的推断期修复思路 GRAFT：通过中途读出词分布、挑出正在被压制的稀有词并“重启生成+固定约束”，把离散选择提前注入轨迹。即使不采用 GRAFT，论文的机制分析也会影响后续 continuous dLLM 的建模/训练设计（例如在中间步骤引入离散化或可采样的接口）。","technical_approach":"对象是连续 dLLM：整个扩散/flow 轨迹都在 token embedding 空间里演化，去噪头用回归（MSE）预测干净 embedding（x-prediction），最后一步才通过 decoding head 把 embedding 映射到词表。\n\n作者的机制论证分三步：\n1) 现象刻画：用“与同分布 held-out 人类文本相比”的词频对齐指标，按语料频率定义 rarity（而不是按模型概率），统计各频段词的生成占比。发现 common words 对齐良好，tail 严重不足。\n2) 原因定位：连续回归在每一步都趋向条件均值。若某位置存在 rare word 与 common competitor 的多峰分布，回归会把 embedding 拉向更可能的 common word 区域，相当于把多峰平均成单峰。作者用“effective candidates”刻画最终解码前每个位置还剩多少可选词，报告只有约 1.02–1.05，意味着几乎已经确定，后续任何采样策略都缺乏操作空间。\n3) 推断期修复 GRAFT：核心是把 AR 里的“先得到词分布，再采样/截断”这一接口，插回到扩散轨迹的中段。\n- 过程是 read-and-restart：在生成中途用 decoding head 读出词分布，找出那些‘仍有不小概率但正在输给更常见词’的稀有词；把这些词作为硬约束固定下来，然后从噪声重新开始生成，让剩余位置围绕这些已提交词重新协调。\n- 多次重复，逐步累积一组 committed rare words，最后再完整生成一次得到输出。\n- 为了减少硬约束带来的副作用，加入 sub-word 级约束处理，以及 anti-repetition 修正（引用 Zhang et al., 2026）。\n\n值得注意的是，GRAFT 不改模型参数，属于纯采样/解码层面的补丁；它的意义更多在于证明“长尾概率并非模型学不到，而是采样接口缺失导致无法兑现为离散选择”。","experiments":"论文给出的关键数字与结论：\n- 长尾塌缩：在 ELF 的 held-out 同分布文本上，最稀有词的生成率只有人类的约 0.30×；该差距从 105M 到 652M 参数不消失，且通过 sampler tuning 无法弥合。\n- 指标对比：GPT-2 generative perplexity 可能偏好回避稀有词的文本；unigram entropy 太粗，难以发现“只在尾部缺失”的问题。\n- 机制证据：当 rare word 被替换时，通常被更常见词或伪词替代；文中给了摘要例子把人名生成成虚构名（如“New Barcelona president Neyll ...”）。并且 competitor 的概率能强预测哪些 rare words 会被压制（全文 §5）。\n- GRAFT 效果：在三种 ELF 规模和独立实现 LangFlow 上，GRAFT 把 rare-word share 提升到人类的 0.82–1.10×；把整体词频分布拉近人类基线，距离改善在 ELF-B 上约 2.7×，在 LangFlow 上约 12×；并让 ELF-L 的 ζ 指标与人类差距缩小到 0.010 以内（具体 ζ 定义在正文对应小节）。\n\n这些结果共同支持：问题主要出在生成过程的“均值化+过早确定”，而不是模型容量不足。","reading_guide":"泛读建议：\n1) 先看 §4 的现象与度量定义：他们如何按语料频率分桶、如何定义 human rate、以及为什么常规指标会漏检。\n2) 再看 §5 的机制分析：尤其是“competitor probability 预测 rare suppression”和“effective candidates≈1.02–1.05”的证据，这是把问题从‘经验现象’变成‘可解释缺陷’的关键。\n3) 最后看 §7 的 GRAFT：重点理解 read-and-restart 的触发时机、如何选 rare words、约束如何施加、以及 anti-repetition/sub-word 处理。\n预计阅读时间：40–60 分钟可以抓住主线；如果你在做 continuous dLLM 采样器，建议把 GRAFT 的实现细节也读完。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper identifies a systematic lexical long-tail collapse in continuous-space diffusion/flow language models (e.g., ELF, LangFlow): common-word rates match human text, but the rarest words appear at only ~0.30× the human rate on in-distribution held-out text, and this does not vanish from 105M to 652M parameters. It then asks why the “no intermediate discretization, decode only at the end” design causes this tail loss, and whether the missing distribution-to-token sampling interface can be restored at inference time without retraining.","importance":"For diffusion/flow LMs, this is a concrete failure mode with a clear mechanism and a practical mitigation:\n- It introduces a diagnostic that standard metrics can miss: frequency-bucketed lexical fidelity against a same-distribution human baseline.\n- It argues the issue is structural: continuous regression denoising averages competing modes, collapsing rare-word alternatives early; by the final decode, there is almost nothing left for temperature/top-k/nucleus to act on.\n- It provides GRAFT, a training-free inference procedure that re-injects discrete commitment mid-trajectory, showing that tail probability may exist inside the model but is not realized as discrete choices under the default sampling pipeline.\nEven if one does not adopt GRAFT, the analysis pressures future continuous dLLMs to expose an intermediate sampling interface or otherwise avoid mean-seeking collapse.","technical_approach":"$25","experiments":"Key reported numbers:\n- Tail collapse: on ELF’s in-distribution held-out text, the rarest words appear at ~0.30× the human rate; the gap persists from 105M to 652M parameters and cannot be closed by exposed sampler settings.\n- Metric caveat: GPT-2-based generative perplexity can reward avoiding rare words; unigram entropy is too coarse to detect tail-specific deficits.\n- Qualitative failure: rare entities are replaced by common words or fabricated non-words (e.g., invented names), remaining fluent but lexically unfaithful.\n- GRAFT improvements: across three ELF scales and an independent LangFlow implementation, GRAFT raises rare-word share to 0.82–1.10× the human rate; it moves the overall frequency distribution closer to human by ~2.7× on ELF-B and ~12× on LangFlow; it brings ELF-L’s ζ metric within 0.010 of the human value.\nThese results support the claim that the bottleneck is the sampling pipeline (premature collapse), not simply model capacity.","reading_guide":"Skim plan:\n- Read the measurement section first (the frequency-bucket evaluation and why common metrics miss the issue).\n- Then read the mechanism section: competitor-probability predictability and the “effective candidates ≈ 1.02–1.05” evidence are the crux.\n- Finally, read the GRAFT algorithm details (when to read distributions, how to select rare tokens, how constraints are applied, and how repetition/subword artifacts are handled).\nEstimated time: ~45–60 minutes for a solid skim; longer if you want to re-implement the sampler.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"扩散LM别只盯任务分：这篇用“稀有词率对齐”戳破连续扩散的一个硬伤，建议复现其统计口径并加进自家评测面板。","en":"Don’t judge diffusion LMs by task scores alone—this paper’s rare-word-rate mismatch is a concrete failure mode worth reproducing and adding to your eval dashboard."},"metadata":{"institutions":["University of Electronic Science and Technology of China","Affiliation:University of Electronic Science and Technology of China"],"authors":["Haoning Yang","Lili Pan"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["diffusion-lm","lexical-long-tail","distribution-matching","sampling","evaluation"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29136","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.29136","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2610.29255","title":"Hierarchical Masked Diffusion Language Models","abstract":"Masked diffusion language models have emerged as an alternative to autoregressive models, enabling parallel token generation. However, standard masked diffusion language models use a shared noise schedule across positions without explicitly encoding which positions should be resolved first. Many tasks exhibit a natural priority among positions: document titles and section headings can guide the generation of body text, while Sudoku cells determined directly from the initial clues can help resolv","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-21T06:06:01Z","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2610_29255.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"Masked diffusion LM 在训练与采样时通常对所有位置使用同一噪声/解码日程，默认“位置对称”。但很多生成任务存在天然的“先解哪些位置更合理”的层级结构（标题→正文、易确定的数独格→难格）。论文要解决的是：如何把这种“位置优先级/层级”显式纳入离散 masked diffusion 的前向扩散与反向去噪，使模型能先确定高层/关键位置，再补全细节位置。","importance":"对非 AR 语言建模范式（masked diffusion / any-order）有直接方法贡献：\n- 把“解码顺序”从启发式采样策略，变成训练目标里可学习、可控制的变量（hierarchy level），并允许不同层级使用不同噪声日程。\n- 相比 anchor-token 方法（把重要性绑在 token 身份上），这里把层级绑定到 position，更适合结构化任务（数独）和文档结构（标题/小节）。\n- 对预训练研究的启发点在于：如果未来 diffusion LM 要在长文本/结构化生成上竞争，必须有类似“结构先行”的 inductive bias；HMDLM 给了一个相对干净的概率分解与训练推导入口。","technical_approach":"HMDLM 在标准 masked diffusion（逐步把 token 替换成 [MASK]）上增加一个离散的层级变量 h_i（每个位置一个 hierarchy level，来自训练数据或可零/少量标注获得）。关键是把反向过程分解成“两阶段”：先预测层级，再在层级条件下预测 token。\n- 前向过程：不仅对 token x 扩散到 mask 状态，也对层级状态扩散到“未知/掩蔽”的状态，使得在高噪声步模型需要先恢复层级结构。\n- 反向过程（去噪）：\n  1) p(h | x_t)：对被 mask 的位置预测其层级分布；\n  2) p(x_0 | x_t, h)：在给定（预测的）层级后预测 token 分布。\n  这种 factorization 让模型学习“哪些位置属于高层级、应更早解码”。\n- 分层噪声日程：不同 hierarchy level 使用不同的 unmask 速度/噪声 schedule，从机制上实现“先高层后低层”。这点是对称 diffusion 的关键改动。\n- 训练目标：作者推导了包含层级变量的 NELBO（negative ELBO）作为训练目标，对应离散扩散的变分下界形式，但把层级的扩散与反向预测纳入同一目标。\n- 与相关工作的区别：\n  - ADLM/anchor 类：重要性依赖 token 身份（anchor tokens），不适合“位置先验”强但 token 未知的场景。\n  - planning-aware decoding：多在推理时加规划或重排；HMDLM 把层级作为生成过程的一部分，训练时就学习。","experiments":"作者报告了三类域的验证：\n- LM1B：从零训练，与 MDLM、SEDD、ADLM、DiffusionBERT 等 diffusion/掩码基线比较 perplexity 与生成质量；结论是 HMDLM 在 PPL 与质量上优于这些 diffusion baseline（摘要与引言未给出具体数值）。\n- WikiHow：在预训练 checkpoint 上微调，与 ADLM/MDLM 比较 perplexity 与定性生成，显示层级建模对“结构化步骤文本”更友好。\n- Sudoku：从零训练，与 MGDM、MDLM 等比较解题准确率；作者声称更可靠地产生正确解。\n（注：提供的全文片段未包含结果表格与具体提升幅度；若需要精确数字，需要对照论文实验章节的表/图。）","reading_guide":"泛读建议（约 25–35 分钟）：\n1) 先看方法章节里对 forward/backward 的两阶段分解，以及“每层不同 schedule”的定义（引言提到在 §4.3.2）。这是论文真正的新东西。\n2) 看 NELBO 推导部分：重点不是公式细节，而是弄清楚层级变量如何进入变分目标、训练时到底监督了什么。\n3) 实验部分优先看 Sudoku 与 WikiHow：它们更能体现“位置层级”而非纯语言困惑度的收益。\n4) 带着一个问题读：hierarchy level 在真实预训练里如何获得？如果需要标注，成本与泛化如何？论文声称可零/少量标注，但需要核对其具体设定与敏感性分析。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Standard masked diffusion LMs treat token positions symmetrically with a shared noise/unmasking schedule, lacking an explicit notion of which positions should be resolved earlier. Many domains have a natural positional hierarchy (titles/headings before body text; easy Sudoku cells before hard ones). The paper addresses how to incorporate position-level priority into discrete masked diffusion so generation can recover high-level structure first and fill details later.","importance":"A concrete modeling contribution to non-autoregressive / diffusion-style language modeling:\n- Turns “decoding order” from a heuristic sampling trick into a learnable variable (a hierarchy level per position) embedded in the generative process.\n- Unlike anchor-token approaches that tie importance to token identity, HMDLM ties hierarchy to positions, which better matches structured generation where positional roles matter.\n- For pretraining research, it suggests that diffusion LMs likely need explicit structure-first inductive bias to compete on long-form or structured text; the paper provides a clean probabilistic factorization and an ELBO-style training objective to build on.","technical_approach":"$26","experiments":"Reported evaluations span:\n- LM1B (trained from scratch): compares against MDLM, SEDD, ADLM, DiffusionBERT on perplexity and generation quality; However, not much is known about how these abilities are influenced by larger-scale pretraining. In this paper, we train a family of MPIXEL models along three axes of variation: more languages, more scripts, and larger model capacity under fixed per-language compute. In evaluations on five tasks covering 83 languages, we find that scaling acts locally: e","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-21T12:57:41Z","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2610_29681.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作研究的是 pixel language model 在多语言预训练中的 scaling 规律。与常规 subword LM 不同，pixel LM 直接把文本渲染成图像 patch 序列，不依赖固定词表，因此天然适合长尾书写系统和未见脚本迁移。问题在于，这种优势是否会随着预训练语言数、脚本数和模型容量的增加而继续扩大，还是只在少量多语设置下成立。作者试图把三个变量拆开：增加同一脚本内的语言多样性、增加新的书写系统、以及增大模型容量，并观察它们分别如何影响 seen language、unseen language、unseen script 的迁移。","importance":"从基座预训练视角看，这篇工作的价值不在于提出新架构，而在于给一种非 tokenizer 驱动的语言建模路线补上了 scaling 证据。很多关于 tokenizer、词表覆盖和跨语言迁移的讨论默认建立在离散 token 上，而 pixel LM 走的是另一条路：把书写系统的差异交给视觉编码器处理。作者的结论比较克制但有用：多语言扩展的收益主要是局部的，新增语言和新增脚本主要改善被覆盖到的那部分；对未见脚本的迁移，一旦有了多语训练就会出现，但继续扩大规模并不会线性增强。这说明 pixel LM 的跨脚本泛化并不是“越大越通用”的简单故事，而是受到表示瓶颈和视觉共享结构的约束。对做 tokenizer-free、byte/char/pixel 统一建模的人，这是一份很有参考价值的经验图谱。","technical_approach":"方法上，这是一篇控制变量做得比较认真的实证研究。作者从一个 86M 参数的四语言四脚本模型出发，构造了五个预训练变体。mpixel 4s 是基线，覆盖英语、印地语、繁体中文、乌克兰语。mpixel 8l 在保持脚本已见的前提下，增加四种拉丁脚本语言，用来测试“语言多样性增加但脚本不变”的效果。mpixel 8s 则增加四种新脚本语言，用来测试“脚本多样性增加”的效果。mpixel base 同时沿两条轴扩展到 16 种语言。mpixel large 把容量从 86M 提到 307M，用来观察容量是否缓解 multilinguality 带来的负迁移。\n\n预训练数据来自 FineWeb 和 multilingual FineWeb2。一个关键设计是每种语言分配相同数量的 patches，而不是按文档数或 token 数配比，这样更适合 pixel LM 的输入单位，也让不同语言的 compute 更可比。每个样本最多 512 patches，总体上每种语言约 7.5B image patches。另一个很重要的贡献是 multilingual rendering strategy。作者没有沿用单一的字符 bigram 渲染，而是按脚本特性做自适应：默认使用 grapheme-aware bigrams，避免把附加符号和基字符拆开；CJK 因为字符宽、bigram 常跨多个 patch，改成每 patch 一个字符；阿拉伯文和希伯来文因为连写和视觉顺序问题，采用按词渲染并保持逻辑顺序。这部分虽然更像工程设计，但对 pixel LM 很关键，因为输入冗余和视觉破坏会直接影响有效上下文长度与跨语言共享。\n\n分析层面，作者不仅看下游任务，还做了表示探针。一个方向是 ABX discrimination，用来测跨语言表示对齐；另一个方向是 probing，不同层上看句子级和词级语言信息如何分布。正文的一个有意思发现是，大模型并不是简单把所有层都变成更强的语言层，而是“保留更多层做视觉处理”，说明 pixel LM 的容量扩展会先补视觉前端的表征需求，而不是立刻转化成更强的跨语义对齐。","experiments":"实验覆盖五类任务、83 种语言，包括句子分类、依存句法、命名实体识别、自然语言推断，以及英语 GLUE 作为高资源对照。评测集被分成三类：预训练语言、预训练脚本内但未见语言、以及未见脚本语言。作者特别强调低资源和类型学距离较远的语言，这使结果更能反映 long-tail transfer，而不是只看高资源语种。\n\n主要结论有四个。第一，scaling 的收益是局部的。增加同脚本语言主要提升这些新增语言及同脚本语言；增加新脚本主要提升这些新增脚本及其邻近语言。第二，对未见脚本的迁移在“有多语预训练”时就已经出现，但继续增加语言数、脚本数或模型容量，并没有带来同等幅度的进一步提升，说明 unseen-script transfer 更像一种由视觉共享诱发的阈值效应，而不是持续的 scaling law。第三，pixel LM 也会遭遇 curse of multilinguality：随着预训练语言增加，共享的四个基线语言性能会下降；把模型从 86M 扩到 307M 可以部分缓解这种下降。第四，在一些低资源、且与 XLM-R 预训练语言类型学距离较远的语言上，mpixel 优于 subword 和 character baselines，这支持了 pixel 路线在长尾书写系统上的独特优势。\n\n从表示分析看，随着语言数增加，cross-lingual alignment 会变弱，但这种弱化并不意味着完全失去泛化，因为共享脚本仍提供了迁移通道。作者还观察到 307M 模型把更多层用于视觉处理，这与“容量先补前端瓶颈”一致。摘要没有给出完整分数表，但全文的实验组织比较清楚，重点不是追求单一 benchmark 的高分，而是回答四个 scaling 问题。","reading_guide":"这篇更适合带着问题去读。先看 Introduction 和 Table 1，把五个模型变体与四个研究问题对齐，否则后面容易把语言扩展和脚本扩展混在一起。然后直接看 pretraining setup 中的 rendering strategy，这部分决定了实验是否公平，也决定 pixel LM multilinguality 分析，因为这两处最接近基座训练决策。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies scaling behavior in multilingual pixel language models. Unlike subword LMs, pixel LMs read rendered text images as patch sequences and therefore avoid a fixed vocabulary. That makes them attractive for long-tail writing systems and cross-script transfer, but it is unclear whether those advantages improve systematically with more pretraining languages, more scripts, or larger model size. The paper isolates these three axes and asks a clean question: where does scaling actually help, and where do gains saturate?","importance":"For pretraining research, the contribution is not a new architecture but a useful scaling map for a tokenizer-free modeling route. Many multilingual scaling discussions are framed around vocabulary design, token coverage, and segmentation quality. Pixel LMs move the problem into visual representation learning instead. The main conclusion is measured but important: scaling is mostly local. Adding languages or scripts helps the newly covered regions much more than it improves universal transfer, and transfer to unseen scripts appears once multilingual training is present but does not keep improving with further scaling. That is a meaningful constraint on the common intuition that tokenizer-free models should simply get better at everything as scale increases.","technical_approach":"$27","experiments":"$28","reading_guide":"Read this one with the experimental design in mind. During training, UDLMs must denoise from a context that may itself be unreliable, whereas masked diffusion language models (MDLMs) always condition on a clean context. This challenging training objective makes UDLMs more FLOP-intensive to train than MDLMs, but it yields stronger downstream reasoning performance. Recently","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-21T17:24:35Z","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2610_30066.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"Uniform Diffusion Language Models (UDLMs) 在训练时需要从可能被破坏的上下文中预测干净的 token，这比 Masked Diffusion Language Models (MDLMs) 更困难且更耗费算力。本文旨在探究在不改变训练目标和采样器的情况下，引入循环机制（looping）能否提升 UDLMs 的训练效率和推理性能。","importance":"本文首次将循环机制（looping）引入 UDLMs，证明了在保持计算量不变（iso-FLOP）的情况下，循环机制能显著提升 UDLMs 的训练效率和生成质量，为构建更高效的离散扩散语言模型提供了新的思路。","technical_approach":"本文在 Duo++ 框架下，将循环机制集成到 UDLMs 中。在训练阶段，除了均匀采样循环次数外，还探索了 FLOP 比例采样（使不同循环次数的预期 FLOPs 相同）和噪声比例采样（噪声越大，循环次数越多）。在推理阶段，除了固定循环次数外，还引入了自适应循环（当相邻两次迭代的隐藏状态变化小于阈值时停止循环）。","experiments":"在 OpenWebText 和 FineWeb-Edu 数据集上，带有循环机制的 UDLMs 达到相同验证 NLL 所需的训练 FLOPs 分别减少了 2.8 倍和 3.3 倍。在推理阶段，循环机制使生成困惑度降低了 9.8%，在 7 个域外数据集上的零样本困惑度平均降低了 11.5%。自适应循环在 10 个零样本多项选择任务中，在减少 26.0%（均匀采样）或 34.8%（FLOP 比例采样）循环次数的情况下，达到了与固定 12 次循环相同的准确率。分析表明，循环主要改善了被替换 token 的预测，尤其是在中等噪声水平下。","reading_guide":"建议重点关注 Introduction 中对 UDLMs 和 MDLMs 差异的分析，以及 Methodology 中关于不同循环采样策略（FLOP-proportional, noise-proportional）和自适应循环的介绍。实验部分重点看训练效率提升（Figure 1/2）和自适应循环的效果（Table 2）。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Uniform Diffusion Language Models (UDLMs) face a harder denoising task than Masked Diffusion Language Models (MDLMs) because they must predict clean tokens from a context that may itself be corrupted, making them more FLOP-intensive to train. This paper investigates whether introducing a looping mechanism can improve the training efficiency and inference performance of UDLMs without changing the training objective or sampler.","importance":"This work is the first to integrate looping into UDLMs, demonstrating that looping significantly improves training efficiency and generation quality under an iso-FLOP budget. It provides a new perspective for building more efficient discrete diffusion language models.","technical_approach":"The authors integrate looping into UDLMs using the Duo++ framework. During training, they explore FLOP-proportional sampling (equalizing expected FLOPs across loop counts) and noise-proportional sampling (assigning larger loop counts to noisier inputs), in addition to uniform sampling. During inference, they introduce adaptive looping, which halts when the relative change in hidden states between consecutive iterations falls below a threshold, alongside fixed looping.","experiments":"On OpenWebText and FineWeb-Edu, looped UDLMs achieve the same validation NLL with up to 2.8x and 3.3x fewer training FLOPs, respectively. However, router-based approaches remain vulnerable to routin","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2610_29355.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","stat.ML"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是稀疏 MoE 里一个很具体、也很顽固的问题：专家虽然在路由上被分开了，但训练后常常学成相似函数，出现 expert collapse 或 specialization 不足。已有方法大多从 router 端下手，或者加额外正则去惩罚专家相似性；前者只能间接影响专家学习，后者又会把“让专家分开”和“把任务做好”混在同一个 loss 里，容易产生优化冲突。GaP-MoE 想做的是绕开这两类问题，直接在反向传播阶段改写共路由专家的输出梯度，让它们沿不同方向更新，从而显式推动专家分工。","importance":"对预训练研究的启发主要有两点。第一，它把 MoE specialization 问题从“路由设计”转成了“梯度几何”问题，这个视角更接近训练动力学本身，而不是只改 token assignment。第二，它不引入新的辅助目标，而是在已有 task gradient 上做投影，尽量不改原始训练目标。这一点对大规模预训练比较现实，因为很多 auxiliary loss 在小模型上有效，放到大规模训练里常常会和主目标争预算、争梯度、争稳定性。量级上，这更像一个训练算法层面的增量改进，而不是改变 MoE 范式本身；但如果它在大规模语言预训练中也能稳定成立，会比单纯 router trick 更有可迁移性。","technical_approach":"方法核心有三步。第一步是动态 leader 选择。对每个 token，在 Top-K 选中的专家里，不是固定把最高分专家当主专家，而是按归一化 routing score 采样一个 leader。这样做的目的，是让“谁保留原始梯度、谁做去重”这件事带一点随机性，避免长期由同一个高分专家垄断主导地位。第二步是梯度累积。每个专家维护一个最近输出梯度的正交基，文中用 Gram-Schmidt 做在线正交化，把近期梯度方向压成一个子空间记忆。第三步是梯度投影。leader 保留原始 task gradient；其余 follower 专家把自己的输出梯度在 leader 梯度子空间上的分量减掉，只保留与 leader 历史更新方向正交的部分。这样，共路由专家即便处理同一 token，也不会沿高度重合的方向一起更新。\n\n这个设计和已有方法的区别比较清楚。它不改 router 结构，不要求额外 forward/backward，也不通过 auxiliary loss 在参数空间或表示空间上显式拉开专家。它操作的是已经算出来的 expert output gradient，因此更像一个 backward-time intervention。论文强调这一点：不是再造一个优化目标，而是对冗余梯度分量做后处理。代价上，主要新增的是为每个专家维护梯度基以及投影计算。方法描述主要以 Top-2 routing 展开，但思路可以推广到一般 Top-K：选一个 leader，其余专家对 leader 的梯度子空间做去重。","experiments":"从给出的全文片段看，实验主线应当围绕三类指标展开：任务性能、专家专门化程度、以及与现有 specialization 方法的兼容性。论文在引言里明确把对比对象分成 router-based、aux-loss-based 和 gradient-regulation-based 三类，因此实验上大概率会覆盖这些基线，并展示 GaP-MoE 在不增加额外前后向的前提下改善 expert diversity，同时不损伤甚至提升主任务指标。\n\n就目前可见内容，能确定的关键实验结论是：作者声称该方法通过显式正交化共路由专家的梯度方向，减少表示塌缩，并且可以与现有 router 架构和 auxiliary loss 兼容叠加。方法层面的关键消融应当包括：是否使用动态 leader 采样、梯度记忆窗口或基向量数量的影响、只做累积不做投影或只对当前梯度投影的效果、以及不同 Top-K 设置下 specialization 指标的变化。全文片段里没有给出具体参数规模、数据规模和性能数字，因此无法可靠复述具体提升幅度；这部分需要回到实验章节和附录核对表格后再下结论。","reading_guide":"泛读时先抓住两个问题：它到底在改哪里，以及为什么不直接加一个正交损失。建议先看方法总图和 Algorithm 1，把 leader / follower、gradient accumulation、projection 三个步骤串起来。然后重点看方法章节里关于“投影发生在 expert output gradient 而不是参数梯度”的说明，这决定了它和 PCGrad、正交正则、router balancing 这些方法的关系。\n\n实验部分建议优先看三类图表：一是主任务性能是否稳定提升，二是专家相似度或利用率是否真的改善，三是额外开销有多大。如果时间有限，先看主表、specialization 可视化和关键消融，预计 20–30 分钟可以建立基本判断。若要深挖，值得看的点是：梯度子空间记忆是否会过时、leader 采样是否引入训练噪声、以及这种方法在大规模语言 MoE 预训练里是否会和 load balancing 或 capacity factor 发生新的耦合。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a specific failure mode in sparse MoE training: experts are routed separately but still converge to similar functions, leading to weak specialization or outright expert collapse. Prior approaches mostly act on the router or add auxiliary regularizers that penalize expert similarity. Router-side fixes only influence specialization indirectly, while auxiliary losses mix “make experts different” with the original task objective and can create optimization conflict. GaP-MoE instead intervenes directly on the backward pass: it modifies co-routed experts’ output gradients so they update in different directions.","importance":"For pretraining research, the main value is the shift in viewpoint. The second useful point is that it avoids introducing a new optimization target. Instead of adding another loss term, it projects already-computed task gradients. That matters in large-scale pretraining, where auxiliary objectives often compete with the main loss for gradient budget and stability. Prior stochastic latent approaches try to restore uncertainty, but often suffer from unstable optimization and weak empirical performance. PLRM aims to keep three properties at once: parallel training, dense supervision, and probabilistic modeling of diverse reasoning trajectories.","importance":"Its relevance to pretraining-style research is not that it immediately replaces autoregressive LMs, but that it offers a concrete latent-variable alternative to token-level reasoning. Because such accumulation is monotonic, these mechanisms cannot selectively erase intermediate context while preserving older, structurally relevant tokens. We propose First Passage Attention (FPA), which shifts from path summation to path extrema by lowering a key's logit proportionally to the deepest dip of a learned scalar field below that key's level over the intervening ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-15T22:31:45Z","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2610_22714.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"现有的带有遗忘门的注意力机制（如 FoX）依赖于沿键-查询路径的单调累加，无法在保留较旧、结构相关的 token 的同时，选择性地擦除中间的上下文。","importance":"提出 First Passage Attention (FPA)，通过将路径累加改为路径极值（最小值），实现了一种类似栈的注意力机制，允许模型选择性地遗忘中间信息，对长上下文建模和长度外推有启发。","technical_approach":"FPA 为每个位置计算一个标量场（field），为每个键计算一个标量层级（level）。如果后续的标量场低于某个键的层级，该键的 logit 就会根据最深下降幅度成比例降低。当场和层级共享一个学习到的随机游走时，存活的键自然形成一个栈结构。","experiments":"在单层探测实验中，FPA 成功学习了理论目标，而基线方法失败。训练的 110M 参数 FPA 语言模型在困惑度上匹配 FoX，并在 4 倍和 8 倍训练长度下实现了 100% 的 passkey 检索，且仅需约四分之一的 KV cache。","reading_guide":"重点理解 FPA 的核心公式（基于路径最小值的偏置计算）以及它如何实现类似栈的结构。对比 FPA 与基于累加的遗忘机制（如 FoX）的根本区别。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Existing attention mechanisms with forget gates (like FoX) rely on monotonic accumulation along the key-query path, failing to selectively erase intermediate context while preserving older, structurally relevant tokens.","importance":"Proposes First Passage Attention (FPA), which shifts from path accumulation to path extrema (minimum), enabling a stack-like attention mechanism that selectively forgets intermediate information, offering insights for long-context modeling and length extrapolation.","technical_approach":"FPA computes a scalar field at each position and a scalar level for each key. A key's logit is lowered proportionally to the deepest dip of the subsequent field below its level. When the field and levels share a learned walk, the alive keys naturally form a stack.","experiments":"In single-layer probes, FPA successfully learns theoretical targets where baselines fail. Semi-Structured Sparsity versus Quantization in LLM Inference","abstract":"NVIDIA tensor cores have supported 2:4 semi-structured sparsity since Ampere, and the pattern composes with quantization. When the two are combined, the practical question is which step to take next: from a given precision, add 2:4 sparsity or drop to the next lower precision? We compare the two at equal bits, with sparsity metadata counted, and at equal nominal tensor-core tiers, over all six GEMM operands of a transformer layer, with kernels measured on three GPU generations and online pruning","starred":true,"priority":"p1","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-20T21:48:45Z","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2610_28947.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.PF"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇文章回答的是一个很具体、但在 LLM 推理压缩里一直没有被讲透的问题：在 NVIDIA 支持 2:4 半结构化稀疏的前提下，从某个已有精度出发，下一步到底应该加 2:4 sparsity，还是直接降到更低 bit 的稠密量化？作者认为过去很多比较都不公平，因为没有把稀疏元数据算进存储成本，也没有把在线 pruning/quantization 的运行时开销算进去，更没有按硬件真实允许的稀疏轴来比较。全文的核心结论很明确：只要硬件上还有更低的 dense precision 可用，继续降精度通常比加 2:4 更划算，既更准，也更快；2:4 真正有价值的场景主要出现在已经到达 quantization floor 之后。","importance":"这篇工作虽然属于推理效率，但对基座研究和训练后压缩接口都很有启发。第一，它把“稀疏 vs 量化”的讨论从抽象压缩率拉回到真实硬件和真实 bit accounting，结论足够清楚，能直接影响模型发布和 serving 方案选择。第二，它给出一个很有用的误差视角：2:4 沿硬件强制轴丢掉的是固定的 tensor energy，这个损失不会因为保留下来的值用更多 bit 表示而消失；而量化误差会随 bit 增加而系统下降。这个分析对预训练阶段考虑可压缩性、对中后训练阶段做稀疏恢复、以及对 future architecture 设计是否要迎合 2:4，都有实际参考。第三，文章提醒一个常见误判：用 perplexity 判断 retrained sparse model 是否恢复得差不多，可能会高估 reasoning 能力的恢复程度。","technical_approach":"方法设计很扎实，重点在“公平比较”。作者从两个维度比较 2:4 与 dense quantization。其一是 equal bits：把值、索引、scale、zero-point 全部计入每个原始元素的实际 bit 成本。这样 2:4 并不是简单地把存储减半，因为每个保留值还要带位置索引。文中给出清晰换算：2:4 + INT8 的真实成本约等于 dense INT5，2:4 + INT4 约等于 dense INT3，2:4 + FP16 甚至比 FP8 更贵。其二是 equal tensor-core tiers：两者在 datasheet 上都可能承诺约 2 倍吞吐，但作者实际测量真实 kernel latency，而不是只看理论峰值。更关键的是，比较对象覆盖 transformer layer 的六类 GEMM operand，而不是只看静态权重。因为 activations、Q/K/V、attention probabilities 这些动态张量需要在线剪枝和压缩，真实系统里这部分开销不能忽略。作者还严格遵守硬件约束，只沿 tensor core 允许的 reduction axis 做 2:4，这一点很重要，因为很多张量统计量如果不按这个轴看，会误判稀疏的可行性。\n\n在误差分析上，文章提出 2:4 energy loss 这个量：对每个硬件规定的 4 元组，2:4 会直接丢掉其中两项，损失的能量份额可以从张量本身计算出来。作者发现这个量与实际精度退化高度相关，而且解释了为什么 Hadamard rotation 往往会让 2:4 更差：旋转会把能量打散，使每个 4 元组里被迫丢掉的有效信息更多。这个视角比只看全局 kurtosis 更贴近硬件实际，因为 2:4 的约束是局部、定轴、定分组的。\n\n在 quantization floor 场景，作者进一步区分 one-shot pruning 和 retraining。结论是：到了 floor 之后，2:4 的确能带来真实速度收益，但前提通常是重新训练恢复；一次性剪枝基本不可用。文章还测了 Blackwell 的 sparse FP4，指出它采用 pairwise 4:8 模式，kernel 速度有提升，但丢失的信息更多，而且不能直接复用为 2:4 训练过的模型。","experiments":"实验覆盖两类 8B 模型的所有层，并在 Ada、Hopper、Blackwell 三代 GPU 上测量，范围比常见只测单 kernel 的工作更完整。结果可以分三层看。\n\n第一层是等 bit 比较。作者发现，在相同存储预算下，降一档 dense precision 始终比加 2:4 更准确。原因一部分来自真实 bit 成本：2:4 的 metadata 让它实际竞争对象不是直觉里的 INT4，而是更低 bit 的 dense 格式。更关键的是误差性质不同：2:4 丢掉的能量不会因保留值精度提高而恢复，而量化误差大致随 bit 呈系统下降。\n\n第二层是等 tensor-core tier 比较。即便从理论吞吐看，2:4 和更低精度 dense 都像是在争取同一档硬件加速，但实测下来，在每一个 tier 上都能找到某种 dense format 同时更快且更准。作者把在线 pruning 和 quantization 都算进端到端时间后，这个结论更稳，因为动态 operand 的在线稀疏化成本不低。\n\n第三层是到达 quantization floor 后的结果。这里 2:4 开始体现价值。叠加在 FP8 上时，线性层可获得约 1.3–1.7× 的加速；一个经过 retraining 的 2:4 模型在 vLLM 中服务时，无论 prefill 还是 decode 都快于 dense FP8，同时 GSM8K 准确率大致持平。作者还做了一个关键对照：如果把同样的稀疏权重用 dense kernel 跑，速度收益会消失，说明增益确实来自 sparse tensor core，而不是别的实现细节。\n\n恢复训练部分也给出很有价值的数字。perplexity 可以在约 2.5 亿 token 内较快恢复，但 GSM8K 这类 reasoning 指标恢复明显更慢，远低于 Sparse-Llama 那种训练更久、数据也不同的方案。这个结果说明“PPL 回来了”不等于“能力回来了”。对于 Blackwell sparse FP4，作者测到 kernel 大约比 dense NVFP4 快 1.4×，但在其预算下 reasoning accuracy 恢复不到可用水平。","reading_guide":"这篇值得精读。先看 Introduction 和 Figure 1，把作者的比较框架吃透：equal bits 与 equal tensor-core tiers 是全文的骨架。然后重点读 Methodology，尤其是 bit accounting 的公式和六类 operand 的定义，这决定了后面结论为什么比很多旧工作更可信。实验部分建议按三条线读：一是 accuracy-vs-bits，二是 real latency 而不是理论吞吐，三是 floor 场景下 retraining 的恢复曲线。误差分析部分要认真看 2:4 energy loss 的定义及其与 measured gap 的对应关系，这是全文最有解释力的地方之一；Hadamard rotation 为什么会扩大差距，也值得细看。最后看 serving 结果和 Blackwell sparse FP4 的讨论，理解作者为什么说 2:4 不是普遍无用，而是“只有在没有更低 dense precision 可降时才开始成立”。如果你做模型压缩、训练后量化、稀疏训练，或者在设计可部署的预训练 recipe，这篇都值得完整读一遍。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks a practical question that has been poorly answered in LLM inference: given NVIDIA support for 2:4 semi-structured sparsity, what is the better next step from a current precision point—add 2:4 sparsity, or move to the next lower dense precision? The paper argues that most prior comparisons are not on equal terms because they ignore sparsity metadata, omit online pruning and quantization costs for dynamic tensors, and often do not respect the hardware-constrained sparsity axis. The main conclusion is sharp: as long as a lower dense precision is still available on the target GPU, dropping precision is usually the better move, both in accuracy and in speed. 2:4 becomes attractive mainly at the quantization floor.","importance":"This is an inference paper, but it has clear implications for foundation-model research and the pretrain-to-deploy interface. First, it replaces vague compression-rate arguments with hardware-faithful accounting and end-to-end measurements, which makes the conclusion directly actionable for model release and serving decisions. Second, it offers a useful error model: 2:4 removes a fixed share of tensor energy along the hardware-mandated grouping axis, and that loss does not vanish by storing the remaining values at higher precision, whereas quantization error decreases systematically with more bits. That perspective matters for compression-aware training, sparse recovery, and even architecture choices. Third, the paper shows that perplexity can recover much faster than reasoning accuracy during sparse retraining, which is an important warning for anyone using language-model loss as the main recovery metric.","technical_approach":"$31","experiments":"$32","reading_guide":"This one is worth a full read. 即可。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies a practical failure mode in low-bit reasoning models: even after standard QAT and teacher distillation, quantized LLMs still lose a large amount of long-horizon reasoning ability, generate unnecessarily long responses, and often fail to terminate within a token budget that is sufficient for the full-precision model. The full paper argues that this gap comes from two distinct issues. First, trajectory divergence: fixed-response distillation only supervises prefixes from reference trajectories, while the quantized student quickly conditions on its own generated prefixes at inference time, and this mismatch grows with response length. Second, distributional misalignment: on those student-generated prefixes, the quantized model simultaneously underestimates some next-token candidates and overestimates others. A single KL direction does not correct both sides equally well. The result is not just lower accuracy, but distorted generation dynamics under quantization.","importance":"Its main value is methodological rather than paradigm-shifting. The paper makes three useful points for foundation-model training. First, it shows that fixed-trajectory distillation breaks down systematically on long reasoning chains; this is closely related to exposure bias in AR training and to on-policy correction in post-training, with quantization making the mismatch easier to observe. Second, it gives a concrete diagnosis of student errors on self-generated prefixes: they are two-sided, so forward KL or reverse KL alone is structurally incomplete. Third, it reframes low-bit reasoning recovery as a generation-process problem, not just a static accuracy problem. Termination behavior, repetition, and overthinking become first-class metrics. For pretraining researchers, the broader lesson is about training on the states the model will actually visit.","technical_approach":"$34","experiments":"$35","reading_guide":"For a skim, focus on three parts. recurrent state 要为每个并发序列各存一份，显存占用会随并发数线性增长。已有状态量化方法通常在每个生成 token 后都把状态重新量化一次，四比特下误差会持续注入并沿递推累积，所以精度掉得明显，往往需要升到 8-bit 或做额外微调。PACE 的核心问题不是“如何发明一种更复杂的量化器”，而是“如何改变状态被量化的时机和分组方式，让 4-bit 在不训练的前提下也能可用”。","importance":"对基座研究的意义主要在两层。第一层是 serving 侧：线性注意力模型常被讨论为替代 KV-cache Transformer 的长上下文方案，但如果 recurrent state 仍然吃掉大量显存，并发优势会被削弱。PACE 说明，状态量化的关键不只在量化公式，还在递推系统里的误差传播路径；把量化频率从每 token 一次改成每若干 token 一次，能显著改善 4-bit 的可用性。第二层是方法论层面：这篇工作给出一个很清楚的结论——在有状态递推模型里，量化误差是动态系统问题，不是静态张量压缩问题。这个视角对后续做 SSM、线性 RNN、Mamba 类模型的低比特推理都很有启发。","technical_approach":"$37","experiments":"实验覆盖四个 hybrid linear-attention backbones，参数规模从 4B 到 35B。文中反复强调两个变量：一是精度损失，二是总内存占用，而且总内存包含 decode buffer。先看规律性结果：在所有低于 8-bit 的设置里，把 decode rounding period 设为 8 个 token，精度损失都比 period=1 更小，这验证了理论分析。再看方法对比：PACE 与 DAMP、Q-Mamba 等已有后训练状态量化方法比较，在 4-bit 条件下通常能更好保持困惑度或下游准确率；PACE-4/8 进一步缩小与高精度基线的差距。摘要截断没有给出完整表格数值，但正文信息足以确认，作者的主结论不是微小改进，而是把“4-bit recurrent state 不太可用”改成“在合适的写回周期和分组方式下，4-bit 可以接近可用区间”。\n\n消融部分比较扎实。其一，period 消融显示收益主要来自 decode 阶段减少量化次数，而不是 prefill 端设置变化；prefill chunk 长度固定为 256。其二，grouping 消融显示按列分组优于按行分组，而且 group width 越大，这个差距越明显，因为错误的分组方向会放大 shared-scale 带来的动态范围浪费。其三，head precision 分配实验说明少量 8-bit head 就能覆盖大部分脆弱 head。其四，速度实验用 Triton kernel 验证 periodic decoding 仍保留 decode buffer 带来的吞吐优势，没有因为额外 pack/unpack 把收益吃掉。引言里给出一个很有用的部署量级：在 64 路并发下，fp32 recurrent states 可占权重加状态总内存的 10% 到 41%，这解释了为什么这类优化值得做。","reading_guide":"泛读建议是先抓三处。第一看 Section 3 的误差传播分析，重点不是公式推导细节，而是记住两个控制量：head decay 和 rounding period。只要理解 retained error 为什么随 dP 增大而下降，后面的设计就顺了。第二看 Section 4，尤其 Figure 3 一类展示 state 列结构的图，这是全文最有信息量的观察之一；如果只看方法不看这个现象，会误以为 channel-aligned grouping 只是经验技巧。第三看 Section 7 的消融和 kernel 实验，确认作者有没有把 buffer 成本、速度和精度一起算清楚。\n\n预计阅读时间 25 到 35 分钟。若只想判断是否值得跟进，实现和部署相关的人应重点看 5.1 的 memory accounting、7.2 的主结果、7.5 的速度结果；做模型结构的人则更值得看 3 和 4，因为这里面有对 gated-delta state 统计结构的直接分析。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a deployment bottleneck in linear-attention language models: each concurrent sequence needs its own recurrent state, so memory still scales with batch concurrency even though there is no Transformer-style KV cache. Prior post-hoc state quantizers typically re-quantize the state after every generated token. At 4 bits, that repeated writeback injects quantization noise at every step, and the recurrent dynamics keep part of that noise alive, which leads to noticeable quality loss unless one moves to 8-bit or fine-tunes the model. PACE asks a narrower and more useful question: can 4-bit state quantization work without training if we change when the state is rounded and how entries are grouped?","importance":"Its value is mostly on the inference side, but the underlying lesson is broader. For linear-attention and recurrent LM families, memory efficiency is not determined only by asymptotic state size; it also depends on whether that state can be stored cheaply at realistic concurrency. PACE shows that the main failure mode of 4-bit state quantization is not simply insufficient precision in a static tensor sense. It is repeated error injection inside a dynamical system. That framing matters for Mamba-like models, SSM-based decoders, and any recurrent LM where state compression interacts with temporal propagation.","technical_approach":"$38","experiments":"$39","reading_guide":"For a skim-level read, focus on three parts. Then read Section 4 and the figures showing column structure in the recurrent state. That observation is the real reason the grouping design makes sense. Finally, read the main results and ablations in Section 7, especially the memory accounting and speed measurements, to check whether the method still works once buffer cost is included.\n\nA practical reading path is 25 to 35 minutes. Expert pruning reduces this footprint, but most methods focus on which experts to remove and prune every layer equally. We propose SPARE (Sparsity Allocation via Routing Entropy), a training-free, selector-agnostic method that decides how many experts to remove from each layer using routing entropy, the entropy of tokens' normalized Top-K gate weigh","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-21T12:40:00Z","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2610_29662.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文解决的是 MoE 专家剪枝里的“层间预算分配”问题：在给定总剪枝比例时，应该每一层剪掉多少专家，而不是只讨论每层内部该剪哪些专家。已有方法多用专家使用频率、路由统计或重构误差来给专家排序，但这些分数跨层不可直接比较，容易把敏感层剪得过重。","importance":"对基座研究的启发主要在 MoE 路由动态和压缩策略上。SPARE 的价值不在于提出新的 MoE 训练方法，而在于把专家剪枝拆成两个问题：层间分配和层内选择。这个拆分很实用，也更符合大规模 MoE 的工程现实。它说明路由分布的“尖锐程度”可以作为层级敏感性的信号，比直接跨层比较专家重要性更稳。对预训练研究员来说，这类分析也有助于理解不同层的专家专化程度、路由冗余和压缩鲁棒性。","technical_approach":"SPARE 使用 routing entropy 作为层间剪枝预算信号。对每个 token，先取 router 的 Top-K 专家及其归一化 gate 权重，然后计算这些权重的 Shannon entropy，再在校准 token 上求平均。高熵表示一个 token 的 gate 权重在活跃专家之间分得较均匀，删掉其中一个活跃专家时丢失的路由质量较小，因此这一层可以剪得更激进。低熵表示路由更集中，说明该层存在更强的输入条件化专化，剪枝应更保守。方法本身不决定剪哪个专家，而是决定每层剪几个专家；具体专家选择仍交给 REAP、EASY-EP 等层内排序方法。论文还给出随机剪枝下的扰动界，把输出扰动与 routing entropy gap 联系起来，用理论形式解释为什么高熵层更耐剪。这个设计的关键区别是避免把不同层的专家重要性分数硬做全局排序，而是先用层级统计量分配预算，再在层内比较专家。","experiments":"实验覆盖 Mixtral、Qwen3 和 DeepSeek 系列 MoE。最有信息量的是 Qwen3-30B-A3B 的分析：模型有 48 个 MoE 层，论文按 routing entropy 相比均匀分配更稳，尤其在高剪枝率下更明显。Qwen3-30B-A3B 在 50% expert pruning 时，八任务平均从均匀分配的 57.2% 提升到 63.8%。DeepSeek-R1 上，math、code、science 平均从 80.1 提升到 82.8。论文还指出，专家身份会随 web text、code、math、science 等校准域变化，但层级 entropy 的方式。实验部分重点看 Qwen3-30B-A3B 的 entropy tercile 干预实验，以及 50% 剪枝比例下 SPARE 对 uniform allocation 的提升。若关心部署压缩，可以进一步看它如何与 REAP、EASY-EP 组合，以及在高剪枝率下是否有层被分配到极端预算。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets the inter-layer allocation problem in MoE expert pruning: given a global pruning budget, how many experts should be removed from each layer. Existing methods mostly decide which experts to prune within a layer, using usage, routing statistics, or reconstruction scores. Those scores are not well calibrated across depth, so a global expert ranking can over-prune sensitive layers.","importance":"The main value for foundation-model research is its treatment of MoE routing as a layer-level sensitivity signal. SPARE is not a new MoE training recipe; it is a clean decomposition of expert pruning into allocation across layers and selection within each layer. That decomposition is useful for large-scale MoE compression, and it also gives a practical lens for studying routing redundancy, expert specialization, and depth-wise robustness in pretrained MoEs.","technical_approach":"SPARE computes routing entropy from the normalized Top-K gate weights for each token and averages it over calibration data. If the active expert weights are diffuse, removing one active expert loses only a small share of the token’s routing mass, so the layer is treated as more pruning-tolerant. If the weights are concentrated, the layer is treated as more specialized and receives a smaller pruning budget. SPARE only allocates how many experts to prune per layer; the within-layer choice of experts can be handled by existing selectors such as REAP or EASY-EP. The paper also derives a perturbation bound under random pruning, where the bound depends on the routing entropy gap, giving a formal justification for using entropy as a layer-level signal. The key distinction from prior pruning criteria is that it avoids comparing expert-importance scores across layers and instead uses a layer statistic to assign budgets.","experiments":"The experiments cover Mixtral, Qwen3, and DeepSeek MoE models. A useful diagnostic is reported on Qwen3-30B-A3B with 48 layers: layers are split into high-, medium-, and low-entropy terciles, and 50% of experts are pruned within one tercile at a time using REAP, removing about 16.7% of all routed experts. Starting from an unpruned eight-task average of 72.1%, pruning the high-entropy tercile gives 68.2%, the medium-entropy tercile gives 67.2%, and the low-entropy tercile gives 66.4%. This supports entropy as a pruning-tolerance signal. Under the same selector and pruning budget, SPARE improves over uniform allocation, especially at high pruning ratios. At 50% expert pruning, Qwen3-30B-A3B improves from 57.2% to 63.8% on the eight-task average. On DeepSeek-R1, the math/code/science average improves from 80.1 to 82.8. The paper also reports that expert identities vary across calibration domains such as web text, code, math, and science, while layer-wise entropy rankings are more stable.","reading_guide":"A 20–30 minute skim is enough for most readers. understanding. Video-LLMs prefill incoming frames into KV caches while no query is pending, but they must cap the per-frame cost with sliding windows or cache compression. The cached KV states are therefore built under a context that may differ from the context needed when a user query arrives. Some relevant history may be missing, while irrelevant history may already be encoded in the cached states.","importance":"The contribution is mainly on inference-time systems for video-LLMs rather than pretraining. Still, it is useful for researchers thinking about long-context representations and multimodal serving. The key lesson is that KV caches are not neutral memory. They encode the context under which they were created. In an online setting with unknown future queries, larger prefill context can inject irrelevant information into the representation. A shorter local cache plus selective query-time recomputation can be a better operating point than simply increasing the prefill window.","technical_approach":"FrameBlend is a training-free plug-in method. During idle-time processing, the model caches KVs for incoming video tokens using a bounded local context. When a query arrives, FrameBlend selects a subset of query-relevant tokens and recomputes their KVs under the query-time context, then overwrites the stale cached states. The selection module, QTS, is described as a lightweight frequency-based token-level mechanism guided by query relevance. This avoids recomputing all cached tokens and avoids query-agnostic selection. The design deliberately shifts part of the contextualization burden from idle-time prefill to query-time correction: prefill uses shorter windows to save compute and reduce irrelevant context injection, while recomputation repairs the tokens that matter for the current query.","experiments":"The method is evaluated on three streaming video understanding frameworks, ReKV, HERMES, and LiveVLM, with two streaming benchmarks, RVS and OVO-Bench, plus three offline video understanding benchmarks. FrameBlend matches or improves aggregate accuracy for every framework-benchmark pair. Reported gains reach 3.2 percentage points on streaming benchmarks and 3.9 percentage points on offline benchmarks. On RVS, for ReKV and LiveVLM, it reduces per-frame prefill FLOPs by 22.2% to 26.0% and prefill latency by 12.6% to 60.5%. After including query-time recomputation, total FLOPs still drop by 19.0% to 23.2%, and total latency by 14.1% to 58.7%. The ablations around window length and recomputation are the most important evidence, since they support the claim that shorter prefill windows can improve both cost and accuracy.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 20 to 30 minutes. LLM Serving 里的一个新缓存决策问题：在 prefill 和 decode 被拆到不同节点后，KV cache 的“保留”不再只是本地显存里的替换问题，而变成了 admission + eviction 的联合优化。保留一个前缀块，需要在生成时额外把 KV 传到共享池；不保留，则在下次命中失败时要重新做 prefill，再跨节点传输到 decode 端。两边都有真实成本，而且成本随前缀长度、模型宽度、网络带宽、模态类型而变。传统 LRU/LFU 默认 miss 成本近似恒定、admit 成本近似忽略，这个前提在 disaggregated serving 下失效。论文要解决的核心就是：如何在在线、低开销条件下，预测某个 prefix 值不值得留下，并把这个预测和系统成本模型结合起来，做出比统一阈值或 recency/frequency 规则更合理的 keep/evict 决策。","importance":"对基座研究员来说，这不是 pretrain 论文，但它对大模型系统侧有直接启发，尤其是长上下文、多模态、prefix-heavy workload 的推理基础设施。价值主要有三点。第一，它把 KV cache 从“命中率优化”改写成“端到端时延/带宽/池容量联合优化”，这个问题定义是清楚的，也更贴近真实部署。第二，它说明多模态前缀会显著放大缓存策略的收益差异，因为视觉 token 前缀又长又贵，错误保留和错误丢弃都很伤。第三，它给出一个很实用的思路：不要只做 hand-crafted policy，而是在线学习 reuse probability，再用闭式成本模型把概率映射成保留决策。这个范式对未来 unified multimodal serving、prefix sharing、session-aware routing 都有参考意义。局限也很明确：它更偏 serving policy，不直接改变模型训练或建模范式；对 pretrain 的启发主要在“系统成本如何反过来约束模型与 tokenizer 设计”这一层。","technical_approach":"方法由三个部件组成。第一是 PRP，Prefix Reuse Predictor。它在线学习某个 prefix 在给定时间窗内是否会被再次请求，输入特征分成结构特征和时间特征两类。结构特征包括 prefix 长度、模态类型、在 radix-tree 中的位置、会话元数据等；时间特征包括 recency、frequency、访问间隔、时间段模式等。论文强调不依赖离线 profiling，而是在服务过程中持续更新，因此模型必须轻量。第二是 CARS，Cost-Aware Retention Score。它不是直接拿 reuse probability 排序，而是把“保留的期望收益”和“保留的即时成本”显式写成闭式公式。miss 代价包含重新 prefill 的算力成本和 P-node 到 D-node 的传输成本；admit 代价包含首次把 KV 放入共享池的传输和占用池内存的机会成本。于是 keep 决策不再是‘预测会不会复用’，而是‘预测复用概率是否足以覆盖保留成本’。第三是 ATC，Adaptive Threshold Controller。由于池容量、网络拥塞、工作负载分布会变，固定阈值会失效，所以它动态调节保留阈值，使策略适配当前资源状态。\n\n论文的关键点不在于用了多复杂的学习器，而在于把学习目标和系统目标对齐。LRU/LFU 只编码最近/频繁访问，无法表达“长前缀 miss 更贵、短前缀 admit 不划算”这种异质成本。作者还在理论部分形式化了最优 admission threshold 随 prefix length 变化的条件，说明统一阈值在 q>1 的理想化设定下就已经不是最优。这一点把经验观察上升成了问题结构本身。","experiments":"实验覆盖语言和多模态工作负载，并与 No-Cache、LRU-Pool、Mooncake-LRU 等基线比较。最核心的结果是端到端 TTFT 和跨节点传输量。论文报告，相比 No-Cache，KVLearn 最多可把 TTFT 降低 56%；相比 LRU-Pool 降低 38%；相比 Mooncake-LRU 降低 33%。在网络开销上，相比 LRU-Pool，inter-node transfer volume 最多减少 53%。在 MM-Session 这类更接近真实多模态会话的场景下，在现实池容量约束下，吞吐接近 Oracle。这个结果说明收益不只是来自更高命中率，而是来自更少的无效 admit 和更少的高代价 miss。\n\n从文中给出的背景数字也能看出问题规模：13B 模型、bfloat16 下，一个高分辨率视觉前缀的 KV 占用可到 128–512 MB；在 A100 上重算这类前缀大约要 100–400 ms。也就是说，多模态前缀上的一次错误决策，代价已经足够大，简单 recency policy 很难稳住。\n\n值得注意的消融点通常有三类。其一，去掉 cost-aware scoring，只保留 reuse predictor，效果会明显下降，说明‘会不会复用’和‘值不值得保留’不是一回事。其二，去掉在线学习或只用静态规则，面对 workload shift 时性能会退化。其三，多模态和长前缀场景的收益通常显著高于纯短文本场景，这和论文的问题设定一致：异质成本越强，学习式策略越有优势。","reading_guide":"泛读建议是先看 Introduction 和 §3 的问题形式化，再看 §4 的三个模块，最后直接看 §5 的主结果图。阅读重点有三个。第一，先确认作者到底优化什么指标：不是单纯 cache hit rate，而是 TTFT、throughput、transfer volume 这些端到端系统指标。第二，重点看 CARS 的成本分解，理解为什么 disaggregated setting 下 admit 和 miss 都有代价，这一步决定了整篇工作的必要性。第三，看多模态实验，因为那里最能体现策略差异。\n\n如果时间有限，建议 25–35 分钟。优先看：问题定义图、成本模型公式、主表格里 TTFT/transfer 的对比、以及 ablation。若要深挖，可以想两个延伸问题：一是 predictor 是否会被 workload shift 或新模态打穿；二是这个策略能否和 prefix-aware routing、speculative decoding、KV 压缩联合优化。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"This paper studies a new cache decision problem in disaggregated LLM serving. Once prefill and decode are separated onto different node pools, KV retention is no longer a simple on-device replacement problem. Keeping a prefix block incurs an upfront transfer into the shared KV pool; not keeping it means that a future miss triggers both recomputation on a prefill node and another transfer to the decode node. Both sides have real and heterogeneous costs, depending on prefix length, model size, bandwidth, and modality. Classical LRU/LFU assumes roughly uniform miss cost and negligible admission cost, which breaks in this setting. The paper asks: can we predict, online and cheaply, whether a prefix is worth retaining, and combine that prediction with an explicit cost model to make better keep/evict decisions than recency- or frequency-based heuristics?","importance":"This is a serving paper rather than a pretraining paper, but it is relevant to foundation-model researchers working on long-context and multimodal systems. Its main contribution is not a new cache heuristic in isolation, but a cleaner formulation of the objective: optimize end-to-end latency, bandwidth, and pool capacity jointly, instead of optimizing hit rate alone. That matters more in multimodal workloads, where visual prefixes are long and expensive, so both false keeps and false evictions are costly. The broader lesson is useful: once system costs become asymmetric, hand-designed cache policies tied to recency/frequency are often misaligned with the actual objective. A lightweight learned reuse model plus an explicit systems cost model is a practical alternative. The direct impact is on inference infrastructure, but it also feeds back into how one thinks about tokenizer choices, multimodal prefix length, and deployment-aware model design.","technical_approach":"$3a","experiments":"$3b","reading_guide":"$3c","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"分离式推理是未来的重要方向，本文将网络传输成本显式建模进 KV Cache 驱逐策略，对设计大规模 Serving 系统有直接参考价值。","en":"Disaggregated serving is a key future direction; explicitly modeling network transfer costs into KV cache eviction policies offers direct reference value for designing large-scale serving systems."},"metadata":{"institutions":["University of California","Columbia University"],"authors":["Dong Liu","Yanxuan Yu","Eric Jiang","Shu Wang","Ying Nian Wu"],"notable_authors":["Ying Nian Wu"],"tags":["Disaggregated Serving","KV Cache","Cost-Aware Caching","LLM Inference"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28637","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.28637","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2610.29013","title":"Target Regeneration Shrinks the Gains of Draft-Training Objectives in Speculative Decoding","abstract":"Speculative decoding accelerates large language models by verifying drafted tokens in parallel. The framework integrates advances from two areas: (1) it leverages the meta-cognitive capabilities of LLMs to a","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-20T18:06:47Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2610_28767.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文要解决的是 LLM benchmark 评测过度依赖平均分的问题。平均分会把题目难度、技能覆盖、模型长短板都压成一个标量，无法回答一个更有用的问题：模型到底在哪些可解释技能上强或弱。","importance":"对预训练研究的直接贡献不是训练方法，而是评测粒度。它把 psychometrics 里的 cognitive diagnosis model 引入 LLM 评测，用技能向量替代单一分数。这个方向对 pretrain eval、数据配比诊断、benchmark 结构分析都有参考价值。尤其是它能把 benchmark item 映射到可解释技能，再估计不同模型的 skill mastery，这比只看总分更接近预训练数据和能力形成之间的接口。","technical_approach":"SkillEval 包含两个核心模块。第一步用 LLM 的元认知能力从题目中自动发现技能，并构造 item-skill 的 Q-matrix，也就是每道题关联哪些潜在技能。第二步使用 neural cognitive diagnosis model，根据模型在题目上的对错矩阵估计每个模型在每个技能上的能力。它与 multidimensional IRT 的区别在于，MIRT 虽然也学习能力向量，但维度往往不可解释且存在可辨识性问题；SkillEval 先显式发现技能，再用 CDM 建模，因此输出更稳定，也更容易用于分析和路由。","experiments":"实验规模较大：覆盖 3,811 个 LLM、9,523 道题、约 3,600 万条 model-item response，benchmark 包括 MATH、BBH、GPQA、MuSR、IFEval。SkillEval 在 item-level 预测上达到 AUC 81.73，在 benchmark-level 分数预测上 Pearson r 为 0.989。用于模型路由时，在 τ=0.80 下可以用 25% 成本达到最强模型 99.9% 的准确率；在 τ=0.95 下用 37% 成本超过最强模型 3.7 个点。对新模型 profiling 时，20 个自适应选择的回答可达到接近 100 个随机回答的 benchmark-score 误差。论文还展示了技能覆盖、技能共掌握模式、base 与 instruction-tuned 模型技能差异等分析。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，约 25–35 分钟。重点看方法部分中 Q-matrix 构造和 CDM 建模方式，再看与 MIRT 类方法的稳定性、可解释性对比。实验部分可重点看 item-level/benchmark-level 预测、adaptive profiling、routing 三组结果。对预训练研究员更有用的是 dataset structure 和 model behavior 分析，因为它可能帮助判断 benchmark 是否真的覆盖了某类预训练能力，而不是只给出一个排名。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses a common weakness in LLM evaluation: benchmark scores are usually reduced to a single average, which hides the skill composition of the benchmark and the model’s uneven capability profile.","importance":"The main value is in evaluation rather than training. SkillEval provides a way to turn benchmark responses into interpretable skill-level ability estimates. For pretraining researchers, this is useful for diagnosing model families, auditing benchmark coverage, and connecting evaluation failures back to data or training choices. It is more informative than scalar accuracy when two models have similar aggregate scores but different skill profiles.","technical_approach":"SkillEval has two stages. It first uses LLMs to discover human-readable skills from benchmark items and builds a binary Q-matrix that maps each item to the skills it requires. It then fits a neural cognitive diagnosis model over the model-item response matrix to estimate per-model proficiency over these skills. Compared with multidimensional IRT, the skill axes are explicitly named and anchored to items, which improves interpretability and run-to-run stability.","experiments":"The study uses about 36M model-item response records from 3,811 LLMs on 9,523 items across MATH, BBH, GPQA, MuSR, and IFEval. In model routing, it reaches 99.9% of the strongest model’s accuracy at 25% of the cost when τ=0.80, and exceeds the strongest model by 3.7 points at 37% of the cost when τ=0.95. We introduce MOSAIC, a benchmark of 923 questions over persona-grounded histories of 11-24 sessions spanning text, image, video, and audio. MOSAIC constructs targeted spatial, temporal, and prosodic si","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-21T05:55:20Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2610_29249.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"如何把“全模态、多会话、长时记忆”从一个端到端分数拆成可诊断的系统问题：证据到底是在写入（表示/存储）、检索、投递（打包进上下文）、还是阅读（跨模态推理）阶段丢失的？现有 benchmark 多停留在图文或单轮，且评测耦合了系统组件，难以定位失败来源。MOSAIC 试图用可控构造的跨模态证据链与匹配干预协议，把这条链路拆开测。","importance":"对预训练本身不是“新目标函数/新架构”，但对做多模态基座与长期记忆系统的研究很实用：\n1) 把 long-term memory 的失败模式拆成 write/retrieve/deliver/read 四段，并用冻结 reader/prompt/budget/judge 的 matched protocol 做因果式归因，减少“换个检索器/换个提示词”导致的不可比。\n2) 数据构造强调“模态缺口（modality gap）”：非文本模态携带的关键信息在 caption/ASR 文本化后会系统性丢失，这对统一 tokenization、跨模态压缩表示、以及是否该在 pretrain 阶段学习保真表示都有直接启发。\n3) 评测把 packing（媒体上限导致的投递丢失）从 retrieval 里剥离出来，这一点对真实产品预算约束下的系统设计很关键，也能反向影响训练时的上下文预算/检索增强策略。","technical_approach":"核心是“数据构造 + 分阶段干预评测协议”。\n- 任务形态：每个样本是 persona-grounded 的多会话历史（11–24 sessions），问题嵌在时间顺序的 haystack 里；覆盖 6 类问题（单会话抽取、跨会话选择/桥接、知识更新、时间计划扰动、以及证据移除后的拒答等）。\n- 证据链设计：每题需要 1–3 个事实，按 Narrower/Selector/Answer 三种角色组成确定性检索路径（NAR* SEL* ANS）。删除测试保证每个 required fact 都是必要条件：移除任一事实即不可回答。\n- 模态缺口（modality gap）：要求至少一个关键事实只能靠图像/视频/音频的“原生关系”保留，且通过对话约束避免文本泄漏。\n  - 图像：背景细节、细粒度属性、空间配置。\n  - 视频：动作顺序、状态转移、重复次数（例如固定机位重复动作、对话只说“几次”，caption 会丢 selector）。\n  - 音频：用“语义中性文本 + prosody 区分（happy/unhappy）+ ≥2 候选”构造选择器；作者明确指出这类事实在 Caption-Text/ASR 写入路径下会“按设计丢失”，用于暴露 pipeline，而不是考察感知。\n- 四阶段诊断：\n  1) Write：不同写入表示（例如文本化/符号化等）是否保留空间/时间/韵律细节。\n  2) Retrieve：是否能找全 required units。\n  3) Deliver（packing）：在固定预算/媒体上限下，top-ranked 证据是否被截断或挤掉；与 retrieve 分开计量。\n  4) Read：在 gold delivered chain 下，reader 是否能跨模态跨会话整合推理。\n  另有 Abstain 控制：证据缺失时是否只在该缺失条件下拒答。\n- 生成流水线：config 驱动的自动化生成（LLM 先设计实体与问题，再 validate-and-repair），包含 12 类失败模式的程序化检查与 per-fact deletion test；媒体由图像/视频/语音生成模型合成（文中给出 FLUX-2-pro、Kling–Seedance、Qwen3-TTS），并有人审抽检（100 items，94.3% well-posed）与“探针假阴性”修复。","experiments":"论文给出的 headline 结果是按 matched protocol 对阶段损失做配对差分：\n- 文本化（textualization）在空间信息占比高的设置下带来约 9 分下降（作者用“∼9 points”表述）。\n- 检索带来约 8 分下降；packing/投递在生产预算下约 4 分下降，但在更紧的媒体上限下会更严重。\n- 即便给到 gold symbolic facts（更干净、更结构化的证据交付），仍有约 15 分差距，指向 reader 的跨模态/跨会话整合能力不足，而不只是检索或表示问题。\n- 音频 prosody 的 gap 在 Caption-Text/ASR 写入路径下会系统性消失，因此相关对比更像“写入表示的暴露测试”；空间信息的因果验证最强，prosody 有 selector-level 的额外验证。\n- 数据规模与构成：923 个问题；11–24 sessions 的历史；包含 139 个 refusal controls；音频 prosody 标注策略出现频繁（文中给出 450 facts / 399 of 784 answerable items）。\n（注：全文片段未包含所有表格的具体模型榜单分数；这里优先总结作者在引言与方法部分明确给出的定量结论与数据统计。）","reading_guide":"泛读建议（约 30–45 分钟抓住方法）：\n1) 先看 Figure 2（四阶段 write/retrieve/deliver/read）和 Table 1（与既有 benchmark carrier/阶段。\n4) 如果做系统落地，重点看 deliver/packing 的设置：媒体上限如何导致 top-ranked artefact 被丢弃，这部分往往比检索算法本身更接近真实瓶颈。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"How to turn omni-modal, multi-session “long-term memory” from a single end-to-end score into a diagnosable pipeline problem: when an agent fails, is evidence lost at write/representation time, during retrieval, during delivery/packing under budget constraints, or during reading (cross-modal, cross-session reasoning)? Prior benchmarks largely conflate these components and rarely cover text+image+video+audio in long histories.","importance":"Not a new pretraining objective, but a useful evaluation asset for foundation-model researchers building multimodal assistants and memory stacks:\n1) A matched, stage-wise intervention protocol (write/retrieve/deliver/read) with frozen reader/prompt/budget/judge makes failures attributable rather than anecdotal.\n2) The “modality gap” construction explicitly targets information that is systematically destroyed by captioning/ASR textualization, which feeds back into questions about unified tokenization, multimodal compression, and what fidelity should be learned during pretraining.\n3) Separating retrieval from delivery/packing highlights a practical bottleneck: even correctly retrieved artifacts can be dropped under media caps—an issue that often dominates real deployments and should influence training-time context budgeting and RAG design.","technical_approach":"$43","experiments":"Headline, stage-attributed drops reported by the authors under the matched protocol:\n- Textualization costs ~9 points on spatial-heavy items.\n- Retrieval costs ~8 points;
