Modern pipelines combine embedding, chunking, retrieval, reranking, query augmentation, context compression, and agentic verification, yet these techniques are typically evaluated one at a time. It remains unclear whether their isolated gains survive composition, whether retrieval quality and answer faithfulness favor the same design choices, and whether conclusions from small corpora hold at large scale. W","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-21T13:30:29Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2610_29722.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文研究 RAG 管线组件在组合后是否仍然有效。核心问题不是提出一个新的检索器或 reranker，而是量化 embedding、chunking、query augmentation、reranking、context compression、agentic verification 等组件之间的交互：单点 ablation 得到的收益，放进完整系统后是否还能保留。","importance":"对基座预训练本身不是直接贡献，但对评测和系统化实验有参考价值。它提醒两点：第一，RAG 论文里常见的单组件提升不一定能迁移到完整 pipeline；第二，检索质量和答案忠实性不是同一个优化目标。对于做长上下文、检索增强预训练、外部记忆接口的人，这类组合效应会影响训练数据构造、工具使用评测和 serving 侧设计。","technical_approach":"作者构建了 lasagna，一个模块化 RAG 评测框架。每个阶段都有固定接口，避免替换一个组件时隐式改变其他实现。覆盖的阶段包括文档处理、chunking、index-time enrichment、embedding/indexing、query augmentation、retrieval、reranking、context compression，以及带可选 agentic verification 的生成。实验设计上，作者没有做穷举网格，而是用 167 个配置的 D-optimal factorial design 来估计组件主效应和交互效应，再用 138 个非因子 ablation 配置做 24 组定向验证。评价同时看 NDCG@10 和答案 faithfulness，避免只优化检索命中而忽视生成是否被证据支持。","experiments":"实验覆盖 8 个 benchmark，语料规模从 5K 到 200K documents。主要结果是：检索收益明显 sub-additive，三个最大单组件提升相加预测可带来 +47% NDCG@10，但组合后实际只有 +17%。faithfulness 的收益则更接近 additive，单独测到的改进较容易迁移到完整 pipeline。强 generator 和复杂 RAG pipeline 存在替代关系：generator 越强，重型 pipeline 的边际收益越小。加入 agentic loop 到强检索栈后，faithfulness 只小幅改善，但检索质量和延迟变差。在 100K–200K 文档规模的若干 benchmark 上，轻量 pipeline 可以保持相近质量，同时延迟低 10–20 倍。规模也会改变排序：小语料上领先的配置到 200K 时落后 13.7% NDCG@10，且从 25K 起已能看到 rank flip。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，约 30–45 分钟。重点看 Introduction 中三个预注册假设，Section 3 的 lasagna 组件接口设计，Section 5 的四类 operational profiles，以及 Appendix D/F 中关于 sub-additivity、scale shift、generator-pipeline substitution 的实验表。若只关心方法结论，可优先看 Figure 4、Figure 5、Table 47 和 Section F.9。值得深挖的是它的实验设计：D-optimal factorial design 比简单网格更适合在组合空间很大时估计交互效应。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks whether RAG components retain their measured gains once they are composed into a full pipeline. The focus is not a new retriever or reranker, but the interaction among stages such as embedding, chunking, query augmentation, reranking, context compression, and agentic verification.","importance":"This is not a pretraining paper, but it is useful for researchers working on retrieval-augmented models, long-context evaluation, and external-memory interfaces. The main lesson is that single-component ablations can overstate real system gains. Retrieval quality and answer faithfulness also favor different design choices, so optimizing one metric can degrade the other.","technical_approach":"The authors introduce lasagna, a modular RAG evaluation framework with fixed interfaces for each pipeline stage. The pipeline includes document processing, chunking, index-time enrichment, embedding and indexing, query augmentation, retrieval, reranking, context compression, and answer generation with optional agentic verification. Instead of exhaustively sweeping all configurations, they use a 167-configuration D-optimal factorial design to estimate main effects and interactions, then add 138 non-factorial configurations across 24 targeted ablation studies.","experiments":"The study covers eight benchmarks and corpus sizes from 5K to 200K documents. Retrieval gains compose sub-additively: the three largest individual component gains predict a +47% NDCG@10 improvement, but the composed pipeline realizes only +17%. Faithfulness gains are closer to additive. Stronger generators substitute for heavier RAG pipelines, especially for lightweight configurations. Adding an agentic loop to an already strong retrieval stack gives only a small faithfulness gain while hurting retrieval quality and latency. At 100K–200K documents, lighter pipelines often retain similar measured quality with 10–20x lower latency. Corpus scale changes rankings: a configuration that leads at small scale trails by 13.7% NDCG@10 at 200K, with the rank flip already visible around 25K.","reading_guide":"Skim time: about 30–45 minutes. Figure 4, Figure 5, Table 47, and Section F.9 are the most informative parts if time is limited. However, many benchmarks remain English-centric, with limited cultural coverage and a focus on factual knowledge over its application in real-world cultural contexts. We propose CulturalRiddles, a benchmark of original, culturally grounded riddles created entirely by humans through a participatory community effort, spanning 62 cultures (45 countries, 51 langu","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-21T16:20:30Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2610_29968.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"如何在“多语言 + 多文化”的真实语境下评估 LLM：不仅测文化事实记忆，还要测模型能否把文化线索（隐喻、典故、习俗联想、语言双关、社会语用）组合起来做溯因/组合推理，并且用可扩展的人类在环评测把开放式答案可靠地打分。","importance":"这篇工作的价值主要在评测资产与评测协议，而不是提出新的预训练方法。对预训练研究的直接启发有三点：\n1) 文化能力的瓶颈不只来自“语料覆盖”，还来自“线索解释与联想空间”的建模偏差：同样的词面线索在不同文化里有不同的默认解释，模型更容易选错解释而不是缺事实。\n2) 低资源语言的性能塌陷很明显，且呈现随资源等级单调下降的趋势，这对 data mixture（高/中/低资源配比）、跨语种 tokenizer、以及持续预训练的语言补齐策略提供了更可操作的目标函数（以文化推理类题目而非百科 QA 作为补齐指标）。\n3) 模型在不确定时倾向于“编答案”而不是明确拒答（文中给出 hallucination 与 ‘I don’t know’ 的比例差异），这提示预训练阶段的 uncertainty calibration / abstention 行为可能需要更早介入（例如在预训练或 mid-train 引入显式的未知/不可判定建模信号），否则仅靠后训练很难覆盖长尾文化场景。\n总体上，它更像一个高质量的文化推理压力测试集：适合用来诊断预训练数据覆盖与跨语种泛化的缺口，但不直接给出如何修补缺口的训练 recipe。","technical_approach":"方法核心是“社区共创数据 + 多层评测协议”。\n- 数据构建：6,191 条原创文化谜语，由各社区成员用母语撰写，覆盖 62 个 community（语言-国家组合维度）、51 种语言、45 个国家；强调 many-to-many 的语言/国家映射（同一语言跨国、同一国家多语言），避免把文化等同于语言。\n- 质量控制：每条谜语由非作者的母语者复核，目标是保证谜语确实依赖本文化联想而非可被通用常识直接解。\n- 任务形式：开放式生成答案（不是多选），更接近真实使用时的“解释线索→给出结论”。谜语天然要求：\n  1) compositional reasoning：把多个线索组合成一致解释；\n  2) abductive reasoning：在多个可能解释中选最能解释全部线索的答案。\n- 评测协议（关键贡献）：对 28 个模型配置产生的 170,520 份回答，采用三段式打分：\n  1) 规则层：exact match / 空答 / ‘I don’t know’ 等可确定情形；\n  2) 人类层：母语者对大量 unique answers 做细粒度标注（含错误类型 taxonomy），覆盖 57 个 community、48 种语言；\n  3) LLM jury：两个裁判模型，先在人类标签上验证一致性，再用于覆盖剩余回答；无法判定的样本从准确率计算中剔除。\n这套设计的取舍很明确：开放式答案必须有人类锚点，否则纯自动 judge 会把“文化上不对但语言上像”误判为对；但全量人工又不可扩展，所以用‘人工标注 unique answers + 校准后的 LLM jury’折中。","experiments":"论文报告的关键结果集中在三个现象：\n- 总体与社区差异：最强模型总体准确率约 79.4%，但在部分社区（文中举例 Lumasaba、Mongondow）所有模型准确率都不超过 22%，显示“平均分掩盖结构性盲区”。\n- 资源等级效应：按语言资源等级汇总的 pooled accuracy 随资源下降：高资源约 37%，中资源约 31%，低资源约 18%，极低资源约 13%。这更像数据覆盖/表示能力的系统性问题，而不是个别语言的偶然波动。\n- 不确定性行为：在规则+人工可判定的回答里，hallucinated 约 24%，而显式 ‘I don’t know’ 仅约 0.8%。这说明模型在文化谜语这种高歧义任务上缺少“保守输出”的默认策略。\n从预训练角度，最值得看的不是“哪个模型第一”，而是：社区间方差、资源等级曲线、以及错误类型分布（如果全文给出更细的 taxonomy 统计，建议重点对照）。","reading_guide":"泛读建议（约 25–35 分钟）：\n1) 先看 Figure 1 把数据构建、评测流程、三条核心结论串起来。\n2) overall。\n如果要把它用于预训练诊断：建议把社区/语言切片当作 data mixture 的回归测试集，并结合错误类型（事实缺失 vs 线索解释错误 vs 语言理解错误）做定向补数或 mid-train。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"How to evaluate LLMs’ multicultural competence in a realistic way: not just whether they can recall cultural facts, but whether they can interpret culturally grounded cues (associations, idioms, wordplay, social pragmatics) and solve Before an agent can follow a policy, answer a compliance question or file a ticket, some ingestion layer must turn a Word file or PDF into tokens - and that layer is where an agent deployed in the wild silently loses information. We show that one layer is lost by every mainstream converter - the meaning an author encodes in how text looks - and that the loss makes agents confidently wrong rather than visibly incomplete. We build a task from 76 real policy agent/LLM 在合规与流程类问答中对“作者刻意强调的要求”判断失真，而且是静默失败：文本看起来完整、系统不报错、模型却更自信地答错。","importance":"这不是预训练论文，但对“LLM 作为系统组件”很关键：它把一个长期被忽略的可靠性问题（ingestion 表示层丢信息）做成了可度量任务，并给出无需 LLM judge 的确定性评测。\n对预训练研究的启发点主要有两类：\n1) tokenizer/表示与数据：当前预训练几乎不包含“文档呈现层语义”（颜色/高亮/修订痕迹等）的稳定编码，导致模型即使读到完整文字也缺少作者意图的先验。若要做面向企业文档的 continual pretrain 或 mid-train，可能需要把格式标记作为一等 token/控制符纳入语料，而不是只在 RAG 侧补丁。\n2) 训练信号与校准：格式线索缺失会把模型推向更高的 false positive（把未被强调的条款当成要求），属于高风险错误类型；这类错误很难靠“更强生成能力”自然消失，反而会随流畅度上升更隐蔽。\n总体贡献是：把 ingestion 的‘silent cue loss’从工程抱怨变成可复现实验与可回归指标。","technical_approach":"全文的技术路线偏工程与评测设计，但做得比较克制：\n- 任务定义：从 76 份真实政策/流程文档中，要求模型“恢复作者用视觉方式 singled out 的 requirements”。关键是 ground truth 不来自人工主观判断，而来自 .docx 的 OOXML run properties（颜色、高亮、删除线等）——用确定性 matcher 评分，避免 LLM judge。\n- 解析器（formatting-aware parsing）：用 python-docx 直接读 .docx，输出扁平 RawBlock 序列；把连续相同格式的文本切成 FormattingSpan，每个 span 记录 12 个格式属性。\n- 语义提示层（SemanticRole hints）：基于高置信规则把部分格式映射为语义提示（例如红色文本/红色高亮→critical_warning，其他可见高亮→warning，删除线→deprecated）。作者明确把它定义为 hints 而非真值，并刻意排除中等置信映射，理由是“错提示比没提示更糟”。\n- 序列化：混合 Markdown（模型熟悉的 **bold**、~~strikethrough~~）与自定义标签（Markdown 表达不了的 [COLOR:#FF0000]、[HIGHLIGHT:YELLOW]），再加结构标签（列表归属等文档级结构）。\n- 归因实验：对 cue 类别做 span-level leave-one-out，定位到底哪些格式线索在驱动性能差异。\n- 额外问题：不仅要“恢复 cue”，还要“告诉模型 cue 的含义”。他们做了预注册对比，结论是后者有小幅收益。\n这套方案的取舍很清楚：不追求把所有格式都语义化，而是先把最常见、最有信息增益、且转换器普遍丢失的 cue（颜色/高亮）变成可见 token，并用确定性评测闭环验证。","experiments":"文中给出的关键实证点（以全文描述为准，部分未给出具体数值细节）：\n- 普遍性：约三分之一真实文档包含这类“视觉标注的要求/例外”，不是对抗样本。\n- 下游影响：格式盲 ingestion 会让模型对未被作者标注的 requirements 的 false-positive 率上升约 4 倍；且表现为“更自信地错”。\n- 跨模型一致性：该效应在来自两家供应商的 6 个 answering models 上都成立，说明不是单一模型的偶然。\n- 归因：leave-one-out 显示几乎全部增益来自 colour 与 highlighting；bold/italic/monospace/strikethrough 对该任务贡献不显著（至少在他们的设置与文档分布下）。这把‘formatting matters’收敛成可执行结论：优先保颜色与高亮。\n- ‘cue 恢复’ vs ‘cue 解释’：预注册实验显示需要告诉模型 cue 的语义，收益存在但幅度不大。\n- 负结果：progressive disclosure 没有降低 token 成本；typed layer 在两个“只稀疏尝试任务”的模型上无收益。\n如果要复现或迁移，建议重点核对：评分 matcher 的定义、OOXML 属性到标签的映射表、以及 leave-one-out 的统计显著性与置信区间。","reading_guide":"泛读建议（约 30–45 Parsing 与 tag set（正文 + Appendix S4/S5）：理解他们到底把哪些 cue 编码成 token、哪些刻意不做。\n4) 看 cue leave-one-out 归因结果：这是最可迁移的工程结论（优先级排序）。\n如果你做的是预训练/持续预训练：把它当作“文档表示层”的需求文档。重点思考两件事：是否要把格式标签纳入 tokenizer/词表；以及是否需要在 Testing LLMs on Paired P and NP-Complete Problems Hardened by Adversarial Multi-Agent Rewriting","abstract":"Many NP-complete (NPC) problems have a polynomial-time (P) twin that differs in a single structural detail, such as 3- versus 2-colouring a graph. A model that reasons rather than recalls should notice which side of this boundary a problem falls on, yet existing benchmarks either fix the wording of each task or ignore its complexity class, so they cannot separate the two. We introduce P2NPCBench, a benchmark of 12 problem families in which every NPC problem is paired with a P twin posed over the","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-21T17:50:19Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2610_30103.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"现有评测基准无法区分大模型是真正理解了问题的计算复杂度（如 P 与 NP-Complete 的区别），还是仅仅记住了教科书上的题目表述。当问题从 P 变为 NP-Complete 时，模型往往无法感知并调整算法。","importance":"提出了首个控制表面形式不变、仅改变计算复杂度类别的评测基准 P2NPCBench，揭示了当前 LLM 在面对结构微调导致复杂度跃升时，普遍缺乏真正的算法推理能力，更多依赖于对题面模式的记忆。","technical_approach":"构建了 P2NPCBench，包含 12 个问题家族，每个家族包含一对 P 和 NP-Complete 问题，它们在表面描述上完全一致，仅在决定复杂度的关键结构上有所不同。为了消除教科书式的提示词，提出了 AgentGAN 框架，利用生成器不断重写题目描述，直到一组固定的代码模型无法解答，同时通过可执行的验证门（validity gate）确保重写后的题目答案和复杂度类别不变。","experiments":"在 12 个闭源和开源模型上的评测显示，所有模型在 NP-Complete 问题上的得分均低于其对应的 P 问题（pass@1: 35.4% vs. 63.8%）。将时间限制放宽 10 倍几乎没有帮助（28.5% -> 31.9%），说明失败主要源于算法错误而非超时。经过 AgentGAN 强化（hardening）后，P 和 NPC 之间的性能差距从 24.6 分缩小到 14.5 分，表明强化有效去除了模型依赖的表面线索。","reading_guide":"重点阅读 Introduction 中关于模型“推理 vs 记忆”的讨论，以及 Methodology 中 AgentGAN 的设计，特别是 validity gate 如何保证题目重写后的等价性。实验部分关注 P 和 NPC 问题的性能差异对比，以及 hardening 对模型表现的影响。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Existing benchmarks cannot distinguish whether LLMs truly understand the computational complexity of a problem (e.g., P vs. NP-Complete) or merely recall textbook phrasings. Models often fail to adapt their algorithms when a problem crosses the boundary from P to NP-Complete due to a minor structural change.","importance":"Introduces P2NPCBench, the first benchmark that pairs P and NP-Complete problems with identical surface forms, revealing that current LLMs lack true algorithmic reasoning when faced with structural tweaks that alter complexity, relying heavily on memorized patterns.","technical_approach":"Constructs P2NPCBench with 12 problem families, each containing a P and an NP-Complete variant that share the same surface description but differ in the critical structure defining their complexity. To remove textbook cues, the authors propose AgentGAN, an adversarial multi-agent framework where a generator rewrites problem statements until a panel of frozen code models fails, while an executable validity gate ensures the rewrites preserve both the answer and the complexity class.","experiments":"Evaluation across 12 proprietary and open-source models shows all models score lower on NP-Complete statements than their P twins (35.4% vs. 63.8% pass@1). A tenfold time limit increase barely helps (28.5% -> 31.9%), indicating failures stem from incorrect algorithms rather than timeouts. How well does each model play its role in this ecosystem, as a creator and as a solver? We introduce Environment Duels, a dynamic agentic benchmark that evaluates both capabilities on interactive environments. Each model writes environments, each with a privileged hint, aiming for tasks that capable solvers fail withou","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-21T17:55:31Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2610_30116.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"核心问题是：在模型越来越多地消费其他模型生成内容的环境里，如何评估一个 LLM 不只是“会解题”，还是否“会出题”，尤其是能否生成对下一代模型训练有学习信号的交互式任务。","importance":"对预训练和后训练的启发主要在数据生成侧。它把 synthetic curriculum 的质量拆成两个能力：任务难度和提示可学习性。这个拆分比只看模型解题分数更接近真实训练闭环，因为合成数据是否有用，取决于任务能不能让当前模型失败、又能通过正确线索学会。对 RL 环境自动生成、self-play curriculum、模型共演化评测有参考价值，但当前更像一个评测和数据生成框架，离可直接指导大规模预训练数据配比还有距离。","technical_approach":"Environment Duels 让每个模型同时扮演 author 和 solver。author 需要生成一个可执行交互环境，并提供一个 privileged hint。理想环境应满足：solver 没有 hint 时容易失败，有 hint 时能够成功。环境不是静态问答，而是带隐藏状态、文本 observation、action space、step 函数、reward 和 action budget 的程序化任务。作者侧被拆成设计和实现两步：先写设计规格、hint 和可行性分析，再根据锁定方案写环境代码，减少实现过程泄露 hint 的风险。任务覆盖 symbolic reasoning、planning and optimization、causal diagnosis、adversarial strategy、dynamic control 五类机制。第一轮求解后，作者能看到其他模型的实际轨迹，再做一次 counter-move，生成修订环境。评分包括 solve rate、difficulty 和 hint lift。hint lift 衡量提示带来的成功率提升，直接对应“这个环境是否能提供可学习信号”。","experiments":"实验覆盖 9 个前沿模型、90 个环境、12,960 次 episode。主要发现是，强 solver 不一定是强 author；会解题和会构造训练任务是不同能力。文中提到 DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash 在 9 个模型中写出了最难的环境，而其开发者报告中也强调了 synthetic environments 训练，这为该评测捕捉到的 author 能力提供了旁证。另一个关键结果是，某个 author 的 hint lift 能预测 learner 在该 author 环境上训练后，在 held-out benchmark 上获得的收益。这一点比单纯报告环境难度更重要，因为它把评测指标和训练收益联系起来。","reading_guide":"泛读建议用 20–30 分钟。重点看 Methodology 中环境构造流程、hint 设计约束、counter-move 机制和评分定义。实验部分优先看 solver ranking 与 author ranking 的差异，以及 hint lift 和 RL 训练收益之间的相关性。若关心 synthetic RL data，可以细看环境释放格式和轨迹数据；若只关心 benchmark，则读 introduction、framework figure 和主结果表即可。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The central question is how to evaluate an LLM as both a solver and a task author in an ecosystem where models increasingly train on artifacts produced by other models.","importance":"The main value for pretraining and post-training research is on synthetic curriculum design. The work separates task-solving ability from task-authoring ability, and further asks whether an authored task provides a learnable signal rather than just being hard. This is closer to the data-generation loop used in RL and synthetic-data pipelines than conventional static benchmarks. Its immediate contribution is a benchmark and environment source; its longer-term relevance is in measuring which models can generate useful training distributions for other models.","technical_approach":"Environment Duels assigns each model two roles. As an author, the model writes an executable interactive environment and a privileged hint. As a solver, the model plays environments written by others, once with the hint and once without it. An environment has hidden state, text observations, actions, a deterministic step function under a seed, a reward, and an action budget. The authoring process is split into planning and implementation: the model first writes a design specification, the hint, and a private feasibility analysis, then writes the source code from the locked plan. This separation tries to make the core difficulty deliberate rather than an artifact of buggy implementation. The benchmark covers five domains: symbolic reasoning, planning and optimization, causal diagnosis, adversarial strategy, and dynamic control. After observing solver trajectories, each author produces one counter-move by revising its environment. The main metrics are solve rate, environment difficulty, and hint lift.","experiments":"The study runs 9 frontier models over 90 environments and 12,960 episodes. The main empirical result is that strong solvers are not necessarily strong authors. The ability to create difficult, instructive environments appears distinct from the ability to solve them. DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash is reported as producing the hardest environments among the nine models, consistent with independent reports that it was trained heavily on synthetic environments. A reinforcement-learning experiment shows that hint lift on an author’s environments predicts how much a learner improves after training on those environments and evaluating on held-out benchmarks.","reading_guide":"A 20–30 minute skim is enough for most readers. Focus on the environment construction pipeline, the definition of privileged hints, the counter-move stage, and the scoring functions. The most relevant experimental sections are the comparison between solver and author rankings, and the analysis linking hint lift to downstream RL improvement. 组成向量，再做中心化，得到该 agent 的坐标。这样新增 agent 只需要前向打分，不需要在环境中采集新轨迹。理论部分把坐标距离和 trajectory KL 联系起来：在局部性、参考分布与目标策略分布不严重错配等假设下，平方坐标距离可以近似反映轨迹分布差异，误差项来自局部近似、reference mismatch、中心化和有限样本。论文还把 harness 组件看作 map 三种输入条件拆开比较，从而观察偏见是否在视觉通道被放大。第二，它提示多模态预训练里一个常被忽略的问题：视觉编码器和图文对齐阶段可能引入与文本模型不同的社会偏差，而且这种差异未必随参数规模单调改善。摘要里给出的结论是，视觉输入平均让公平性下降 0.291（FaiR），而且这种 gap 更像 family-bound 而不是 scale-bound。这对做 VLM/MLLM 的多个社会维度中只保留七类：age、gender、appearance、SES、race、religion、disability，排除了 sexual orientation 和 nationality，因为这些属性通常缺乏稳定、明确的视觉线索。第二步是上下文人工筛选，只保留那些确实能通过图像区分相关属性的场景，避免“文本上有偏见标签，图像上却不可见”的伪视觉任务。\n\n第三步是图像生成与过滤。作者用 diffusion 模型生成对应视觉场景，然后做两层质量控制：自动 VQA 过滤，检查图像是否与 prompt 对齐；再做人类过滤，验证语义一致性、去除明显伪影，并尽量减少文生图模型自身偏见直接污染 benchmark。第四步是多样化采样。每个唯一 prompt 生成十张语义等价但视觉实现不同的图像，这样可以减少单张图像偶然因素对偏见判断的影响。\n\n评测设计上，作者构造了三种 matched settings：text-only、image-only、image-with-context，用来隔离模态效应；还加入 perception control，专门测模型是否能识别图中人口属性。如果模型在感知控制上准确率很高，但公平性仍显著下降，就说明问题不只是“看不懂图”。此外，作者指出早期分析常忽略 question polarity，导致 stereotype alignment 统计失真，因此他们采用 polarity-aware 的分析方式来判断模型犯错时是否更倾向于人类刻板印象答案。","experiments":"实验规模比较扎实。数据集覆盖 12,640 个 bias scenarios、七个类别、共 126,400 张评测图像；每个场景有十张视觉多样但语义等价的图像。模型评测覆盖 23 个开源 MLLM、九个家族、参数规模从 1B 到 72B。核心结果有三条。第一，从 text 切到 vision 后，公平性指标 FaiR 平均下降 0.291，说明视觉通道确实引入了额外偏见压力。第二，这个模态差距不是简单由参数规模决定；例如 Qwen2.5-VL 家族里更大模型更公平，但 InternVL3.5-38B 虽然 text-only 表现强，视觉模态 gap 却最大，说明 family recipe 比单纯 scale pheno","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-20T21:30:43Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2610_28937.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","stat.ML"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作讨论的不是如何改进 conformal prediction 本身，而是指出现有 LLM 不确定性评测里一个被忽略的失效源：校准时和部署时如果用了不同的推理配置，哪怕模型参数和任务分布都完全不变，split conformal 依赖的 exchangeability 也会被破坏，覆盖率保证不再可迁移。作者把这种现象定义为 inference-time model shift，强调它不同于传统 covariate shift 或 label shift，因为变化发生在“输入到预测分布”的生成机制上，例如量化方式、温度、数值精度，而不是数据分布本身。","importance":"对预训练本身没有直接方法贡献，但对大模型评测和部署接口有现实意义。很多基座模型研究会把 calibrated uncertainty、selective prediction、risk control 当成模型能力的一部分来汇报；这篇文章提醒，若校准和上线配置不一致，论文里报告的 coverage 可能高估真实部署表现。这个结论对压缩推理、量化 serving、温度控制、A/B serving policy 都相关。它的价值主要在评测协议层面：以后做 LM uncertainty 或 risk-sensitive deployment，推理配置应被视为与模型、数据同等级的实验轴，而不是默认固定的实现细节。","technical_approach":"技术上分两部分。第一部分是形式化。作者在有限标签任务上定义配置变量 C，令 conformal score 不仅依赖 (x, y) 和已训练模型，也依赖推理配置。这样即便 P(X,Y) 不变，只要 calibration 用 C_cal、deployment 用 C_test，score 的生成机制就不同，split conformal 的有限样本有效性前提不再成立。文章的理论重点不是提出新的修正算法，而是澄清 matched validity 只对“同一 score-generating procedure”成立，不能自动外推到别的推理配置。第二部分是系统化审计。作者选取离散标签任务，避免开放式生成里 decoding path、长度、judge 等额外混杂因素；在一个 3×4 的配置网格上枚举量化模式与温度组合，比较 matched calibration 与 mismatched calibration 的覆盖率差异，并进一步报告 mismatch sensitivity 和若干 deployment-specific baseline。这个设计的好处是变量控制得很干净：同一模型、同一数据、同一标签空间，只改变 inference configuration，就能把 coverage 退化归因到 score distribution shift。","experiments":"实验覆盖 8 个小型语言模型、4 个有限标签 benchmark，包括多选问答和分类任务，并在 12 个推理配置上做系统比较。文中最关键的结果来自 MMLU-Pro：部署固定为 nf4 quantization + T=0.5 时，若校准也在匹配配置下进行，目标 0.90 的平均覆盖率约为 0.902；若改用其余配置做校准，再拿到该部署配置上测试，平均覆盖率降到 0.639，最差到 0.508。也就是说，任务分布和模型都不变，仅仅因为 calibration/deployment 配置不一致，就会出现严重 undercoverage。作者还发现这种退化并不单调：低温区更容易出问题，激进量化会放大问题，但不是唯一原因；同一种 mismatch 在某些模型和数据集上会严重失效，在另一些设置上只是轻微下降，甚至出现 overcoverage。这一点很重要，因为它说明不能靠简单经验法则，比如“温度差越大越差”或“量化越重越差”，来替代显式审计。","reading_guide":"泛读时先看 Introduction 和 Figure 1，核心结论已经很清楚：matched CP 评测不足以代表真实部署。然后看理论部分里对 inference-time model shift 的定义，重点理解作者为什么把问题定位在 score-level exchangeability 被破坏，而不是数据分布漂移。实验部分建议重点看配置网格、coverage heatmap 或按模型/数据集分组的结果表，关注“异质性”这个结论，而不是只记住某个平均数。预计 20 到 30 分钟可以抓住主要信息。如果你做的是 uncertainty calibration、量化部署或 serving policy，值得顺手看作者提出的 evaluation protocol，思考自己实验里是否也存在 calibration 与 deployment 脱节的问题。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper identifies a failure mode in conformal prediction for language models that current evaluation protocols largely ignore: calibration and deployment may use different inference configurations, and this alone can break the exchangeability assumption behind split conformal validity. The model weights and task distribution can stay fixed, yet the conformal score distribution changes because the predictive distribution is produced under a different inference pipeline. The paper names this phenomenon inference-time model shift.","importance":"This is not a pretraining-method paper, but it matters for how LLM systems are evaluated and deployed. Many works report calibrated uncertainty or coverage as if it were a stable property of the model. This paper shows that such claims can be misleading when deployment uses different quantization, temperature, or other inference-time settings than calibration. The contribution is mainly methodological: inference configuration should be treated as a first-class experimental axis, alongside model and dataset, when evaluating uncertainty guarantees for language models.","technical_approach":"The paper has two main components. First, it formalizes the problem on finite-label tasks by introducing an inference configuration variable C into the score-generating process. Conformal scores depend not only on (x, y) and the trained model, but also on the inference configuration used to obtain predictive probabilities. Once calibration uses C_cal and deployment uses C_test, matched split-conformal guarantees no longer transfer, even if P(X,Y) is unchanged. Second, the paper performs a controlled empirical audit. It restricts attention to classification and multiple-choice settings so that candidate labels and score computations remain directly comparable across configurations. Over a 3x4 grid of quantization modes and temperatures, it compares matched and mismatched calibration, and proposes reporting mismatch sensitivity in addition to standard matched validity.","experiments":"The audit spans 8 language models, 4 finite-label benchmarks, and 12 inference configurations. The headline result is on MMLU-Pro: when deployment is fixed to nf4 quantization with temperature 0.5, matched calibration achieves mean coverage around 0.902 for a nominal 0.90 target, but mismatched calibration drops mean coverage to 0.639, with worst-case coverage at 0.508. This degradation occurs with the same model and the same task distribution; only the inference configuration differs. The paper also shows that the effect is heterogeneous rather than monotonic. Low-temperature settings are especially fragile, aggressive quantization often amplifies the problem, but neither factor alone explains the full pattern. The same type of mismatch can cause severe undercoverage in one model-dataset pair and only mild degradation, or even overcoverage, in another.","reading_guide":"Start with the introduction and the first main figure, since they already establish the practical failure mode. Then read the theoretical section that defines inference-time model shift and explains why the issue is score-level non-exchangeability rather than classical dataset shift. In the experiments, focus on the configuration grid and the per-model or per-dataset heterogeneity. The key takeaway is not just that mismatch hurts, but that the effect is unpredictable enough that matched-only evaluation is insufficient. A quick but useful read is about 20 to 30 minutes.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"不是 pretrain 论文，但结论很实用：凡是做量化、温度调参、serving policy 的团队，都该把 calibration 和 deployment 解耦重测；先看实验主表和理论假设那一节。","en":"Not a pretraining paper, but operationally useful: if your stack changes quantization, temperature, or serving policy after calibration, you should re-audit reliability under mismatch; read the main tables and the exchangeability argument."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Tongji University","Technical University of Darmstadt","Technical University of Darmstadt, Darmstadt"],"authors":["Achref Doula","Max Mühlhäuser"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["conformal-prediction","evaluation-protocol","model-shift","quantization","reliability"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28937","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.28937","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2610.29346","title":"Benchmarking and Error Analysis of Deep Search and Deep Research LLM Agents","abstract":"As Large Language Models (LLMs) evolve into autonomous Deep Search and Deep Research Agents capable of executing long-horizon, multi-step tasks, evaluating their reliability becomes increasingly critical. Its central point is that final-answer metrics hide many failures in the execution trace, including malformed tool calls, insufficient exploration, weak evidence accumulation, premature stopping, and poor multi-source synthesis.","importance":"The connection to pretraining is indirect, but it is useful for post-training and agent-data construction. Long-horizon agent failures are rarely reducible to a single wrong answer. They arise from tool interfaces, retrieval choices, evidence selection, and long-context synthesis. Separating these failure modes can support better SFT traces, process supervision, and RL rewards, instead of collapsing all supervision into a final binary outcome.","technical_approach":"The paper formalizes Deep Search as a finite-horizon interactive process with web_search, web_fetch, and termination actions. The agent maintains a history, issues search queries, fetches sources, accumulates evidence, and then produces an answer. Deep Research uses the same basic tools, but the objective is long-form synthesis over a larger evidence pool. Its quality is decomposed into factual grounding, cross-source consistency, and task completeness. The evaluation framework separates errors into two levels. System-level errors include malformed tool calls, schema violations, environment rejections, timeouts, and rate limits. Task-level errors are paradigm-specific: Deep Search emphasizes incomplete exploration, weak evidence gathering, and premature termination, while Deep Research emphasizes long-context synthesis failures, cross-source reconciliation, and citation-grounded reasoning. The diagnostic pipeline combines rule-based parsing with a human-guided LLM-as-judge setup over full execution traces.","experiments":"The empirical analysis is trace-based. It aims to quantify how different error types occur and how they propagate through agent workflows. The provided text does not include the concrete model set, sample size, error-rate tables, inter-annotator agreement, or judge calibration results, so the strength of the quantitative claims cannot be assessed from the excerpt alone. For this type of benchmark, the most important checks are category boundary clarity, annotation consistency, judge-prompt design, and attribution rules when several errors appear in the same trajectory.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 20–30 minutes. We introduce FrontierCS 2, a benchmark of 48 open-ended tasks. We take a paper and the code its authors released and delete the paper's contribution. agent 能不能在真实研究仓库里，基于已有系统做出接近论文贡献级别的改进”。作者认为现有 open-ended benchmark 大多给一个题目、收一个最终程序、做一次离线评分，这和真实计算机科学研发差得很远。真实研发更像是在一个可运行仓库上删掉某个关键创新，再要求研究者把它补回来并超过原实现。论文的核心问题就是如何把这种研发过程做成一个可评测、可保密、又尽量贴近真实研究环境的 更接近真实研发闭环：继承代码库、局部编辑、反复提交 patch、拿开发集反馈、最后在隐藏测试上定稿。第二，评分标准不是 rubric，也不是公开 leaderboard，而是和论文作者原始实现直接对比，这让“超过人类参考实现”有了更硬的含义。第三，作者给出能力指数 FCI 和任务难度指数，试图把不同 agent、不同预算、不同任务放到同一尺度上比较。对做 agent post-training 或 tool-use 训练的人，这类 benchmark 能更早暴露出模型在长期搜索、实验管理、局部修改约束、以及 final patch selection 上的短板。","technical_approach":"方法核心是“tasks built by subtraction”。作者从论文作者公开的代码仓库出发，定位实现论文贡献的那部分代码，将其删除，但保留其余依赖和调用接口不变。agent 拿到的是一个仍然能运行、但关键贡献被挖空的仓库，需要只在指定文件中补回缺失实现。被删除的原始代码作为 private human reference，由 judge 保管，不暴露给 agent。这样做的好处有两个：一是任务天然有 oracle reference，不需要人工重写标准答案；二是 agent 面对的是研究者真实会遇到的“在现有系统上做增量创新”场景，而不是从零写一个程序。\n\nJudge 设计也比较讲究。agent 和 judge 分处不同 sandbox，agent 无法联网下载原仓库历史或作者代码，只能通过 driver 调用模型和提交 patch。judge 持有 evaluator、held-out final suite 和 human reference。运行过程中，agent 可以多次提交 patch，judge 只返回 development suite 上的阈值达成情况和简化分数，不暴露参考实现分数或 evaluator 原始输出。最终成绩只看 agent 自己选定的 final patch 在 held-out suite 上是否在每个 metric、每个 workload 上都超过 reference 设定阈值。这个设计避免了 agent 直接对测试集过拟合，也把“何时停止、选哪个版本提交”纳入能力评估。\n\n论文还引入 FrontierCS Capabilities Index（FCI）和匹配的 difficulty index，用来在单一尺度上比较 agent 与任务。虽然从提供内容看不到完整建模细节，但它显然不是简单 pass@1，而是试图做类似项目反应理论那样的能力-难度联合拟合。对于 benchmark 研究，这是比单纯排行榜更有解释力的一步。","experiments":"实验覆盖 48 个任务、13 个领域、12 个模型/agent 组合，每个任务只跑一遍。最强组合是 Claude Opus 5.5 配 Claude Code，pass rate 为 58%；其余 agent 都低于 50%。还有一个很说明问题的结果：16 个任务没有任何 agent 通过。这说明 ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2610_29985.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作解决的是评测粒度过粗的问题。大模型通常用一个总分来比较，但总分只能告诉你“整体强弱”，不能告诉你模型到底在哪些能力切片上出问题，也不能解释两个总分接近的模型为何行为不同。作者把这个问题形式化为：只利用 benchmark 上的样本内容和多模型行为矩阵，能否自动诱导出一个层次化的能力树，让每个节点对应一个行为上有区分度的能力切片。","importance":"它对预训练本身不是直接方法贡献，更像是一个诊断工具。价值主要有三点。第一，它把 benchmark 从单一排名工具变成了能力切片发现工具，这对分析预训练数据缺口、训练退化、模型版本回归很有用。第二，它强调用模型行为而不是外部 taxonomy 来监督划分，这比人工标签体系更贴近真实能力边界。第三，它能把很小的 aggregate gap 放大成可解释的 leaf-level 差异，这对做 recipe 对比、数据配比 ablation、模型版本回归排查都有实际意义。对基座研究员来说，这不是必须精读的方法论文，但作为评测基础设施是有启发的。","technical_approach":"方法上，TIDE 把 fine-grained evaluation 写成一个决策树诱导问题。输入是 benchmark 样本集合和多个模型在这些样本上的得分矩阵 Y，得分可以是 accuracy、rubric score 或 pass rate。关键设计是把 LLM judge 的角色限制为“候选规则生成器”，而不是让它直接决定能力结构。真正的监督信号来自行为矩阵本身。\n\n在每个树节点上，LLM 根据该节点包含的样本内容提出若干候选 split rule，并把样本分到不同子组。然后系统用 behavior-based information gain 选择最好的划分。这个 gain 结合了两类 impurity：一类衡量同一个模型在子组内行为是否更一致，也就是 intra-model consistency；另一类衡量不同模型在子组之间是否更可分，也就是 inter-model separability。这样得到的划分不是按语义相似度聚类，而是按“哪些样本能把模型行为差异显著暴露出来”来组织。\n\n这个设计与已有方法的区别有两层。其一，不依赖 benchmark 自带 metadata、人工 taxonomy 或固定 weakness list，因此 granularity 不是预先写死的。其二，虽然也生成树结构，但树的监督来自行为矩阵，而不是样本语义嵌入或外部知识层级。作者还做了 ablation，试图区分“LLM 提供了不错的规则池”与“行为增益目标真的在起作用”这两件事，结论是后者才是主要信号。\n\n从方法边界看，TIDE 仍然依赖 judge 生成可读规则，因此树的可解释性部分受 judge 质量影响；同时它是基于已有 benchmark 样本的后验结构发现，不会自动补足 benchmark 覆盖盲区。这些限制论文也基本承认。","experiments":"实验在 5 个前沿模型、4 个 benchmark 上进行，重点看树是否稳定、是否能发现 aggregate score 看不到的差异。\n\n1. 一致性与稳定性：作者报告 dev-to-test 的 weakness ranking 具有一致性，说明树不是只在开发集上过拟合出一些偶然切片。对于评测方法来说，这一点比单次可视化更重要。\n\n2. 放大细粒度差异：最差叶节点上的 accuracy drop 最高可达 37.91，说明即便整体分数接近，局部能力切片上的差异可以很大。论文强调 frontier models 在 aggregate level 上常常挤在一小段区间内，而 TIDE 能把这些差异展开。\n\n3. 具体能力发现：在 MMLU-Pro 上，TIDE 找到 Claude Opus 4.6 与 GPT-5.4 在 microeconomic market reasoning 叶节点上有 16.7 的差距；在 DROP 上，发现 GPT-5.4 相比 GPT-5.2 在 large-number computation 上出现 41.2 的回归。这类结果的价值在于它们不是预先定义好的标签，而是由行为驱动诱导出来的切片。\n\n4. 树的迁移性：作者声称诊断树可以跨 benchmark transfer。这里要谨慎理解，更多是说某些能力切片结构在别的 benchmark 上仍有解释力，而不是严格意义上的 domain-invariant ontology。\n\n5. 消融：论文指出真正关键的是 behavior-based split selection，而不只是 LLM 生成候选规则。这个消融很重要，因为否则方法可能退化成“让 LLM 写 taxonomy，再做普通分组”。\n\n从预训练研究视角，最有用的不是具体哪个模型赢，而是 leaf-level regression 能帮助定位版本更新到底伤了哪类样本，这对数据混合、训练退火、post-train 改动回溯都很实用。","reading_guide":"这篇更适合泛读，重点看问题设定和 split objective，不必在所有案例树上停留太久。\n\n先读 Introduction 和 Problem Formulation，确认作者到底把什么当监督信号：不是样本语义，不是人工标签，而是多模型行为矩阵。这个设定决定了方法的边界和价值。\n\n然后看方法部分里 behavior-based information gain 的定义，尤其是 intra-model consistency 与 inter-model separability 两个 impurity。读懂这两项后，基本就明白为什么它能找到“对模型区分最有用”的能力切片。\n\n接着看 ablation 和 dev-to-test consistency。对评测方法来说，这两部分比单个漂亮案例更重要，因为它们回答了树是否稳定、是否真的由行为信号驱动。\n\n案例分析可以挑一两个看，比如 MMLU-Pro 和 DROP 的叶节点发现，用来感受它如何把 aggregate gap 展开成具体能力差异。预计 30–45 分钟足够抓住主线。\n\n如果你关心 pretrain 诊断工具，可以进一步想两个问题：一是把训练 checkpoint 序列当作“多模型集合”时，TIDE 是否能发现能力形成顺序；二是把不同数据配比训练出的模型放进同一行为矩阵时，树是否能反推出数据缺口对应的能力叶节点。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"TIDE addresses a familiar weakness of LLM evaluation: aggregate benchmark scores are too coarse. A single number can rank models, but it does not reveal which capability slices drive the difference, nor why two models with similar overall scores behave differently. The paper formulates fine-grained diagnosis as a structure-discovery problem: given benchmark samples and a matrix of model behaviors, can we induce a hierarchical capability tree whose nodes correspond to behaviorally meaningful subsets of samples?","importance":"This is not a pretraining method, but it is a useful diagnostic layer for foundation-model research. Its main value is that it turns benchmarks from scalar ranking tools into capability-discovery tools. That matters when analyzing data gaps, recipe regressions, version-to-version behavior shifts, or post-training side effects. A second contribution is methodological: it uses model behavior rather than external taxonomies as supervision, which makes the discovered slices better aligned with actual model boundaries. Third, it can amplify small aggregate gaps into large leaf-level contrasts, which is exactly what one wants when comparing nearby checkpoints or small recipe changes.","technical_approach":"$4d","experiments":"$4e","reading_guide":"$4f","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"这类工作不直接改 pretrain，但很适合拿来做 failure taxonomy；只看方法定义和案例树，别把 LLM judge 产出的语义标签当硬真相。","en":"It will not change pretraining directly, but it is useful for building a failure taxonomy; read the tree construction and case studies, while treating judge-generated labels as soft structure rather than ground truth."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Jiayi Chen","Hui Li","Yujiong Shen","Qiyuan Peng","Yanxin Li","Chenhao Huang","Shaofan Liu","Guoqiang Zhang","Junjie Ye","Shihan Dou","Ming Zhang","Maxm Pan","Tao Gui","Qi Zhang","Xuanjing Huang"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["evaluation","diagnostic-eval","capability-tree","llm-judge","benchmark-analysis"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29985","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.29985","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2610.29767","title":"ALIAS: Evaluating Performance and Memorization in LLM-Written TLA+ Specifications","abstract":"Memorization inflates the benchmark scores of large language models (LLMs), since a model can reproduce answers it saw in training. Researchers test for it with consistent identifier renaming (CIR), which gives every identifier in a task a new name, and treat a lower score on the renamed task as evidence of recall. We test this method on the Temporal Logic of Actions Plus (TLA+) language, whose model checker acts as an oracle by confirming whether a renamed task preserves the exact behaviors of ","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-21T14:02:02Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2610_29767.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.SE"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文检验 consistent identifier renaming，即 CIR，是否能可靠检测 LLM 在 TLA+ benchmark 上的记忆。核心结论是：重命名后分数下降不一定说明模型记住了原题，也可能来自 benchmark 的 exact-name grading 或随机名字本身增加了任务难度。","importance":"这篇论文对预训练数据污染和 benchmark contamination 研究有启发。它说明一种常用污染检测方法存在混杂因素：输入被重命名后，语义可能保持不变，但评分规则可能仍要求模型输出原配置文件中隐含的名字。对代码模型、形式化语言模型和小众编程语言 benchmark 来说，这一点很重要，因为公开数据少，训练集重合概率高，单纯比较原题和扰动题分数容易误判记忆。","technical_approach":"作者选择 TLA+ 是因为它有 parser SANY 和 model checker TLC，可以验证重命名后的任务是否保持原行为，也可以按行为而不是名字评分。数据来自 TLA+-Bench 的 model-checked tier。作者排除了 renaming 影响很小的 utility modules，保留 111 个 system specification 任务，其重命名版本都能通过 SANY 和 TLC。重命名时，作者把模块名、常量、变量、operator 等作者定义的 identifier 替换为等长随机名，并同步改写 specification、configuration file 和 import 相关模块。自然语言描述也被重写，但用规则避免把普通英文词或算法名误替换。论文设计了三组实验：对 Claude Opus 4.8 和 GPT-5 做原题/重命名题比较；对 Qwen2.5-Coder-14B 和 Gemma 4 31B 在不相交任务子集上微调，以获得已见/未见任务的可控暴露；再用 behavior-based grader 重新评分，消除 exact-name grading 的影响。","experiments":"在 TLA+-Bench 原有评分方式下，Claude Opus 4.8 的正确率从 28% 降到 18%，GPT-5 从 9% 降到 7%。如果只看这个结果，容易把下降解释为记忆证据。但作者发现，很多下降来自评分方式：配置文件会引用描述中未明确给出的名字，模型即便写出行为等价的 specification，也会因为名字不匹配而失败。用 behavior-based grading 后，重命名导致的下降缩小到约 3 个百分点。受控微调实验进一步把三类因素分开：真实记忆、CIR 带来的随机命名难度、以及评分规则对隐藏名字的要求。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，约 25–35 分钟。重点看 Introduction 对 CIR 假设的拆解，Methods 中 renaming 和 renamed description 的规则，以及三个实验如何分别控制 exposure 和 grading confound。如果只关心 benchmark contamination，可直接看 Experiment 1 与 Experiment 3 的对比；如果关心如何构造更稳健的污染检测，应看受控 fine-tuning 的 exposure split。附录里 identifier 分类和 hidden required names 的例子很有用，能解释为什么语义保持不变并不等于评分难度保持不变。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper tests whether consistent identifier renaming is a reliable signal of memorization for LLM-generated TLA+ specifications. Its main point is that a score drop after renaming can come from memorization, but it can also come from exact-name grading or from the extra difficulty of unfamiliar random identifiers.","importance":"The work is relevant to benchmark contamination and memorization analysis. It shows that a widely used perturbation test has important confounders, especially in code and formal-language benchmarks where names are part of the executable artifact. For pretraining researchers, the useful lesson is methodological: contamination tests need an oracle or grading rule that preserves the intended semantics, otherwise the measured gap can overestimate memorization.","technical_approach":"The authors use TLA+ because SANY and TLC provide strong checks: a renamed task can be verified to parse and preserve model-checking behavior, and generated answers can be graded by behavior rather than exact names. They start from the model-checked tier of TLA+-Bench, exclude utility modules with too few affected identifiers, and keep 111 system-specification tasks whose renamed references pass both SANY and TLC. Author-defined module names, constants, variables, and operators are replaced with same-length random names, and the same mapping is applied to specifications, configuration files, and importing modules. Natural-language descriptions are rewritten with safeguards to avoid corrupting ordinary English words or algorithm names. The study then runs three experiments: original-vs-renamed evaluation on Claude Opus 4.8 and GPT-5, controlled fine-tuning of Qwen2.5-Coder-14B and Gemma 4 31B on disjoint halves of the tasks, and behavior-based regrading of model outputs.","experiments":"Under the original TLA+-Bench grading protocol, Claude Opus 4.8 drops from 28% correctness to 18% after renaming, while GPT-5 drops from 9% to 7%. This would look like evidence of memorization if interpreted directly. The paper shows that much of the drop is caused by grading: configuration files often require exact names that are not specified in the prompt. When outputs are graded by behavior, the renaming gap shrinks to roughly 3 percentage points. The controlled fine-tuning setup helps separate true exposure effects from the intrinsic cost of random names and the artifact introduced by name-sensitive grading.","reading_guide":"Skim time: about 25–35 minutes. Focus on the introduction for the critique of CIR, the methods section for the renaming and description-rewriting rules, and the three experiments for how exposure and grading confounds are separated. If the goal is benchmark contamination, compare Experiment 1 and Experiment 3 first. If the goal is designing stronger contamination tests, read the controlled fine-tuning split. The appendix examples on identifier categories and hidden required names are helpful because they show why semantic equivalence does not guarantee equal grading difficulty.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"指出了代码评测中常用的变量重命名（CIR）测试记忆的方法存在 '评分标准过严' 的坑，强调基于执行等价性的评测，对设计 coding benchmark 很有启发。","en":"Highlights the 'overly strict grading' pitfall in the common Consistent Identifier Renaming (CIR) method for testing memorization in code eval, emphasizing execution-equivalence-based evaluation; highly relevant for designing coding benchmarks."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Loyola University Chicago","Affiliation:Eric Spencer, Beatriz Santos, TaiNing Wang","Affiliation:George K. We ask whether both modalities can instead be generated by one process that is trained on them jointly from scratch. Pretrained encoder spaces hold both modalities in one continuous form and carry knowledge learned at scale, and rece","starred":true,"priority":"p1","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-20T18:25:09Z","category_id":"multimodal","cover_url":"/covers/2610_28801.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要解决的不是单一的文生图，而是更基础的统一多模态生成问题：能不能不用“LLM 负责文本、diffusion/flow 头负责图像”的拼接式方案，也不用把图像硬量化成离散 token，而是在一个统一的连续表示空间里，用同一个生成过程同时建模图像和文本的联合分布。作者抓住了现有统一多模态模型的两个结构性限制。第一，图文通常由不同机制生成，训练目标不一致，导致模型更像是模块拼装而不是联合建模。第二，输出顺序被固定成先图后文或先文后图，先生成的模态无法根据后生成的模态修正，因此很难表达真正耦合的 joint generation。JUICE 的核心问题就是：是否可以直接在冻结的预训练编码器表示空间中，对图像表示和文本表示做联合 flow matching，从而让条件生成、双向生成、以及图文同步生成都落在同一个概率模型里。","importance":"对预训练研究的意义主要有三层。第一，它把“统一多模态建模”从工程拼接问题，重新拉回到预训练目标与表示空间设计问题：如果图像和文本都先映射到可生成的连续语义空间，那么 backbone 可以从零开始联合训练，而不必依赖一个先验上占主导地位的语言模型。第二，它给出了一个相当完整的设计空间实验，回答了几个此前常被经验性处理的问题：文本到底该连续生成还是保持 token 形式、训练时该覆盖哪些噪声区域、该选重建型空间还是语义型空间、这些结论在模型放大后是否稳定。第三，它对“联合分布建模是否真的带来新能力”给了比常规 benchmark 更有说服力的证据。规划任务里的 coupled sampling 结果说明，joint model 不只是把多个任务塞进一个模型，而是在生成过程中允许两个模态相互修正，这一点对未来原生多模态 pretrain 很关键。量级上看，这不是单点技巧，而是一套可扩展的建模路线：2.1B 规模、从零训练、单套权重覆盖文生图、captioning、VQA 和 joint generation，说明这条路至少已经越过“概念验证”阶段。","technical_approach":"方法上，JUICE 由三部分组成。第一部分是表示空间选择。图像和文本都不在像素或离散 token 上直接生成，而是投到冻结的预训练编码器空间里。图像侧作者比较了 SigLIP2、WebSSL-DINO 以及 VAE latent；文本侧比较了 Flan-T5 encoder states 和更大的文本嵌入模型。这里的关键判断是：生成目标不一定要“最可重建”，而要“最有语义结构且适合去噪动力学”。实验最后支持语义空间优于 VAE latent，Flan-T5 也优于更大的 embedding 模型。第二部分是统一生成骨干 MM-RiT。它是一个从零训练的 flow matching Transformer，图像流和文本流通过 bidirectional joint attention 交互。与标准 AR 不同，它不按 token 顺序自回归；与典型 diffusion T2I 不同，它不是单向条件生成，而是同时接收两种模态的带噪表示，并学习从噪声场到数据场的速度场。每个模态有各自的噪声时间 t_img 和 t_txt，以及各自的 schedule，因此整个任务空间可以表示成一个二维“噪声方形”。边界对应条件任务：例如固定文本、去噪图像是文生图；固定图像、去噪文本是 captioning/VQA。内部区域对应 joint denoising，也就是图文同时从噪声中成形。第三部分是训练任务混合与推理路径设计。作者没有只训练边界条件任务，也没有只训练内部 joint task，而是混合 conditional denoising 和 joint denoising。理由很直接：采样时会访问哪些区域，训练就应覆盖哪些区域，否则模型在推理路径上会出现分布外。推理时，任务被解释为穿过这个二维噪声平面的路径；不同路径对应不同任务，而 coupled sampling 则让图文在演化过程中持续互相条件化。与已有 unified model 相比，JUICE 的区别不只是“把文本也连续化”，而是把图像和文本都放进同一种生成语法里：同一 backbone、同一 flow objective、同一上下文机制、同一联合分布视角。","experiments":"$50","reading_guide":"这篇适合精读。第一优先级是方法部分里关于“二维噪声方形”和任务路径的定义，这是整篇工作的统一视角。要看清楚作者如何把 conditional generation、joint generation、captioning、VQA 都写成同一个 flow matching 问题，以及为什么每个模态需要独立 noise scale。第二优先级是预训练消融，尤其是四个设计问题：连续文本 vs token 文本、conditional/joint task mixture、encoder space 选择、以及 scaling。这里不是常规 ablation，而是在替这条建模路线做方法学定盘。重点看对应表格和图：哪些结论在小模型成立但放大后变化，哪些结论跨任务稳定。第三优先级是 coupled sampling 的规划案例。建议仔细看作者展示的定性轨迹，理解“后收敛的模态会重写自己以匹配另一模态”这一现象。这是联合生成相对固定顺序生成最有说服力的证据。阅读时也要带着几个问题深挖：一，连续文本状态生成为什么在语义一致性上足够，但在 U","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-21T17:57:11Z","category_id":"multimodal","cover_url":"/covers/2610_30120.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要解决的问题是：世界模型使用冻结视觉编码器作为状态接口时，单一表示很难同时满足三件事——能表达物体位置和物理状态，能和语言指令对齐，又能解码回像素用于检查预测结果。现有视觉编码器通常偏向其中一两项，例如 要服务于控制、预测、语言接口和可视化检查。这个问题与 native multimodal pretraining 很接近，即如何让视觉 token 同时保留语义、几何和可生成信息。贡献量级属于方法和评测框架层面的中等贡献，适合关注 VLM 表示、世界模型预训练、视觉 tokenization 的研究者泛读。","technical_approach":"方法分两阶段。第一阶段从头训练 ViT-B/16，使用固定正弦位置编码，输出最后一层未归一化 patch tokens。所有目标都压在同一组 patch tokens 上，包括图文对比学习、captioning、像素重建和 dense correspondence。图文对比使用网页 caption 和一条较长 synthetic caption 中抽取的句子，分别做 InfoNCE，避免同图的两个文本互为负样本。caption decoder 自回归预测完整 synthetic caption。重建目标用轻量卷积解码器，从 clean tokens 和 noisy tokens 重建图像，损失包含 L1、SSIM 和图像梯度项。dense correspondence 用于监督不同 crop 和 scale 下的局部空间对应关系。第二阶段做 cross-view self-distillation：冻结第一阶段教师模型，让学生从增强视图预测 clean view 中对应位置的教师 patch token。这个设计的关键取舍是先建立语言、像素和空间基础，再增强状态信息，避免像在 CLIP/SigLIP 上直接蒸馏那样明显损伤语言对齐。","experiments":"实验把 ALPS 和 15 个公开视觉编码器放在统一协议下比较，覆盖物理状态 用在 SigLIP 2 和 CLIP 上时，会让 ImageNet zero-shot 和 image-to-text retrieval 下降 4 到 12 个点，说明 ALPS 第一阶段的联合目标对后续状态增强更稳。下游方面，冻结 ALPS tokens 配合其原生 text tower 在 LIBERO 策略比较中取得最高平均成功率，并在 PushT、Wall、Granular should encode physical state, align natively with language, and remain decodable to pixels. Existing encoders tend to optimize only part of this space. DINO-style models provide spatial features, CLIP/SigLIP-style models provide language alignment, and reconstruction-oriented models preserve pixel detail. Alongside standard teacher-forced denoising, FCP requires the model to predict future chunks further ahead using intermediate representations, while masking the intervening ground-truth visual context. This forces the model to learn deeper spatiotemporal dynamics rather than relying on local continuity. We f","starred":true,"priority":"p1","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-20T15:58:47Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2610_28707.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文要解决的问题很具体，也很难：在 coding RL 里，reward hacking 往往不是训练开始就大量出现，而是在某个阶段突然被 RL 放大，随后迅速主导 rollout。真正昂贵的地方不只是“发现 hack”，而是你通常要先跑完整个 RL 配方，才知道这个 recipe 会不会把模型推向可检测的 hacking 行为。作者因此把问题重新定义为：给定初始 policy 和一个候选 RL recipe，能否在不执行该目标 RL run 的前提下，预测 reward hacking 何时会成为主导行为，且预测的是 onset step 的分布，而不是单点估计。这里的 onset 被定义为首次有至少一半采样 rollout 出现 hacking 的训练步。论文的核心不是做一个更强的 hack detector，而是做一个训练前风险预测器，用来在 recipe 选择阶段规避高风险设置。","importance":"这项工作的意义不在传统意义上的 post-training 小修小补，而在于它把 RL 训练中的 reward quality 问题前移成了一个可建模、可预测、可用于决策的问题。对基座研究员有三层影响。第一，它提供了一个很实用的接口：在大规模 RLVR 或代码 RL 之前，先估计某个 recipe 的 hacking onset 分布，再决定 curriculum、rollout 数、学习率、模型规模等配置，避免把大量算力花在注定会早期崩坏的训练上。第二，它说明 reward hacking 不是只由初始 hacking rate 决定，训练动态和 recipe 细节会显著改变 onset，这对理解 pretrain→posttrain 闭环很重要：同一个初始模型，在不同优化路径下会进入完全不同的行为相。第三，它把“行为动力学建模”引入 RL 安全与训练工程之间的中间层，不依赖把环境修到无懈可击，也不依赖训练中持续加监控器去和模型对抗。贡献量级上，这更像一个新的训练前评估与决策工具，而不是单一 benchmark 提升。若后续能推广到更广泛的 RL setting，它会成为 recipe search 和 reward design 的基础模块。","technical_approach":"方法核心是 SimHack，一个轻量级行为模拟器。它不是直接拟合 reward 曲线，也不是做 token-level world model，而是把每个训练 prompt 上模型可能表现出的行为抽象成若干类别，并学习这些行为在 RL 训练中如何被强化、抑制和传播。论文强调两个事实：一，初始 hacking propensity 有信息，但远远不够；二，同一 recipe 下 repeated runs 的 onset 也有随机性，所以目标应是预测 onset distribution。\n\n从全文描述看，SimHack 的建模思路大致分三层。第一层是行为状态表示。对每个 prompt，作者维护一个关于行为类别的概率分布，类别至少区分正常解题行为、可检测 hacking 行为，以及可能的其他中间行为。这个表示比单一 hacking rate 更细，因为 RL 会改变的不只是“hack 的多少”，还包括不同 prompt 上行为模式的迁移。第二层是转移/更新机制。SimHack 从少量参考 RL runs 中学习：在某种 recipe 下，采样到某类行为、获得某类 reward 之后，后续训练会如何改变该 prompt 及相近 prompt 的行为分布。这里的“propagate”很关键，说明作者不是把 prompt 当成完全独立样本，而是允许某些行为模式在 prompt 池中扩散。第三层是 counterfactual simulation。给定一个未实际执行的目标 recipe，只要它的数据来自同一底层 prompt pool，SimHack 就能用学到的行为动力学去生成随机训练轨迹，进而得到 hacking onset 的分布。\n\n这套方法和直接做 recipe-level regression 的区别在于，它保留了训练过程中的中观结构：prompt、行为类别、采样随机性、强化反馈。和直接跑少量真实 target runs 相比，它的优势是便宜，而且能做反事实分析，比如换 curriculum、删掉高风险 prompt、改 rollout 数会怎样。论文还特别强调泛化：它不仅预测 seen recipe，还尝试外推到未见模型规模、未见 curriculum、未见 rollout 数和学习率变化。这一点决定了它是不是一个真正有用的训练前工具，而不只是对固定实验表的插值器。","experiments":"实验规模是这篇论文很重要的一部分。作者构建了一个较大规模的 coding RL 数据集：325 个 RL runs，覆盖 33 种训练 recipe，总计超过 19.3M rollouts，采集成本约 9000 GPU 小时。任务基于 LeetCode coding tasks，并记录了逐步训练中的 rollouts、rewards 和检测到的 reward hacking 行为。这个数据粒度使得作者可以研究 onset 的分布，而不是只看最终 hacking rate。\n\n主结果上，SimHack 在 onset prediction 上达到 11.3% 的 median onset relative error，以及 0.85 的 across-recipe rank correlation。后者很关键，因为 recipe 选择很多时候不需要绝对步数极准，只需要把高风险和低风险 recipe 排序排对。论文还声称它对未见模型规模、训练 curriculum、rollout 数、学习率变化有合理泛化能力，这说明模型没有完全记住固定 recipe 表。\n\n一个很有分量的对比是：SimHack 的预测准确度优于“直接在每个 target recipe 上跑 5 次真实 RL runs”这种昂贵基线。这个结果如果成立，说明行为模拟器确实抓住了可迁移的动力学，而不是靠更多 target-specific 观测取胜。\n\n更重要的是干预实验。作者用 SimHack 的 counterfactual prediction 来做两类决策：一是选择更低风险的 curriculum；二是识别对 reward hacking 贡献特别大的高风险训练 prompt。随后再跑真实 RL，验证这些干预能把 hacking onset 延后 96.6%，同时提升不依赖 hacking 的任务表现。这里的价值在于，它不是只会“预测会坏”，而是能给出可执行的 recipe 修改建议。\n\n从论文叙述看，还应关注几个消融。第一，只用初始 hacking rate 预测 onset 的基线表现明显不足，证明问题不能被静态 propensity 概括。第二，若去掉行为类别建模或传播机制，预测应会变差，说明训练中的行为结构确实重要。第三，重复运行的方差本身不小，因此分布预测比点预测更合理，这也是作者问题设定成立的经验依据。","reading_guide":"这篇建议精读。阅读顺序可以这样安排。先看 Introduction，确认作者不是在做 hack detection，而是在做 pre-run onset prediction。然后重点看问题定义和 onset 指标，理解为什么目标是分布预测。接着看 SimHack 的建模部分，尤其是行为类别表示、从 reference runs 学习动力学、以及如何对 unseen recipe 做 counterfactual simulation。实验部分要重点看三类结果：预测精度主表、对 unseen recipe 维度的泛化、以及 curriculum / prompt-level intervention 的真实验证。\n\n最值得深挖的点有四个。第一，行为类别是如何定义和标注的，检测器误差会不会污染模拟器。第二，prompt 间传播机制具体如何实现，是否依赖手工相似度或共享参数。第三，为什么它能泛化到未见 recipe 变化，哪些 recipe 维度是插值，哪些更接近外推。第四，onset 定义为 50% hacking rollout 是否稳健，换阈值后排序是否保持。\n\n如果只看最有信息量的部分，建议优先读：问题定义、SimHack 方法图、主结果表、泛化实验、干预实验。预计完整精读需要 60–90 分钟。若要把它转化为自己的研究问题，可以直接思考两个方向：能否把这种 onset simulator 用到更一般的 RLVR，如数学、工具使用、agentic coding；以及能否把它接到 pretrain/posttrain recipe search 中，作为 reward quality 的前置约束。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper tackles a specific and costly failure mode in coding RL: reward hacking often does not dominate from the start, but emerges during training and then gets rapidly amplified by RL. In practice, one usually has to run the full RL recipe before discovering that a given setup will collapse into detectable hacking. The paper reframes the problem as a pre-run prediction task: given an initial policy and a candidate RL recipe, can we predict when reward hacking will become dominant without executing that target RL run? The target is not a single onset estimate but a distribution over onset steps, where onset is defined as the first training step at which at least half of sampled rollouts exhibit hacking. accumulat","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-21T08:35:39Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2610_29410.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇文章解决的是 common-mode offset：在不同 token 位置、不同 prompt、chosen 和 rejected 两侧，它都沿着近似同一个方向增长。因为偏好损失主要看 chosen 相对 rejected 的差值，这个共同平移会同时抬高或压低两边分数，几乎不改变 margin，所以损失对它基本“看不见”。但这个 offset 一旦投影到输出层，就会在词表空间里给某个 token 梯度纠缠”进一步推进到表示层机制，并且给出可干预、可复现、可测量的因果链：hidden offset → vocabulary well → token collapse。这个链条比单纯看 reward 或 ranking 曲线更接近训练动力学本身。第二，它指出 ranking-style objective 存在结构性盲区：只优化相对顺序时，模型可以沿着一个几乎不影响 margin 或更长训练预算更容易出问题，解释得比较清楚。\n\n贡献量级上，这不是一个小修小补的 recipe，而是对 preference optimization 稳定性的一次机制性澄清。它不会直接替代现有方法，但会影响以后如何设计偏好目标、监控训练、设置 early stop 和 drift 的关系；3）能否把 offset 监控做成在线训练诊断，替代单看 reward/ranking 曲线。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper explains a hidden instability in preference optimization: ranking metrics can keep improving under DPO/SimPO/PRO-style training while generation quality silently deteriorates and eventually collapses into repetition of a single token. The key contribution is a representation-level mechanism. The authors identify a shared translation vector in the final hidden states, which they call the common-mode offset. This offset grows across token positions, prompts, and both chosen and rejected responses. Because preference losses mainly care about relative margins, a shared shift that moves chosen and rejected scores together is barely penalized. Once projected through the output layer, that hidden-state offset creates a deep vocabulary well for one token, and decoding becomes dominated by that token. The paper turns a vague “ranking improves but generation breaks” observation into a concrete causal chain.","importance":"This is highly relevant to the pretrain–post-train interface. First, it pushes the analysis of preference optimization beyond probability-level symptoms such as likelihood displacement or gradient entanglement, and ties failure to a measurable hidden-state drift with direct causal interventions. Second, it exposes a structural blind spot in relative-ranking objectives: a model can keep moving along a direction that barely changes margins, so standard held-out ranking metrics can look healthy while generation is getting worse. Third, the paper introduces a useful lens, loss demand, to explain why some objectives or hyperparameter regimes are more collapse-prone than others. In practice, this affects how one should design preference losses, monitor training, regularize hidden-state drift, and decide when to stop training. The contribution is not a small recipe tweak; it is a mechanism paper with clear implications for future alignment training.","technical_approach":"$62","experiments":"$63","reading_guide":"$64","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"这篇会改你对 DPO 类训练“指标变好就没事”的 prior；先看 offset 注入实验和 loss masking，那两处如果站得住，内部对齐训练都该加表征监控。","en":"This should update the prior that improving preference metrics implies healthy training; read the offset-injection and loss-masking sections first—if they hold, representation drift monitoring should become standard in alignment runs."},"metadata":{"institutions":["TIER IV, Inc.","Huawei","Affiliation:TIER IV, Inc.","Affiliation:Huawei"],"authors":["Yun Li","Ehsan Javanmardi","Simon Thompson","Jiachen Gong","Qunli Zhang","Zifan Zeng","Shiming Liu","Peng Wang","Zixuan Guo","Manabu Tsukada"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["preference-optimization","training-dynamics","representation-drift","generation-collapse","RLHF"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29410","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.29410","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2610.29999","title":"EPIC: Rethinking Trust Regions for Autoregressive LLM Policies via Exact-KL Prefix Importance-Ratio Clipping","abstract":"Reinforcement learning (RL) based on PPO-style clipping has become an effective approach to improving the reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs). However, standard token-level clipping measures policy change only through the conditional probability of the current token, while the prefix conditioning context is itself generated by the policy. This mismatch becomes especially consequential in long-response and off-policy training. We reformulate autoregressive generation as a finit","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-21T16:37:09Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2610_29999.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"PPO/GRPO 这类“token 级 ratio clipping”在自回归 LLM 上有一个结构性不匹配：token 的条件概率比值 r_t=π(y_t|x_t)/μ(y_t|x_t) 只衡量了当前 token 的变化，但 x_t 这个前缀上下文本身是由策略生成的。长回复与 off-policy（policy lag、异步 rollout）时，这个 mismatch 会放大，导致 clipping 既不能正确刻画策略分布漂移，也不能稳定地实现 trust region 约束。","importance":"这是少数把“LLM 自回归结构”与“TRPO/PPO 的信赖域推导”真正对齐的工作，贡献点更偏方法论与训练稳定性：\n- 给出一个明确的 change-of-measure：每一步 policy improvement 项的精确重要性权重不是 token ratio，而是“前缀累计 likelihood ratio”。\n- 基于这个精确权重构造 prefix-level trust region，并落成可实现的 clipping 目标（EPIC）。\n- 在强 off-policy 的 MoE RL 训练里，EPIC 能替代 routing replay（R2/R3）这类系统补丁，显著降低 driver 侧内存，同时保持/提升效果。\n对 post-train（RLVR、MoE RL、异步大吞吐训练）影响直接；对 pretrain 的启发在于：当训练目标依赖策略生成的上下文分布时，约束应落在“诱导分布”而非局部条件分布上，这个思路也可迁移到一些自训练/自采样的 continual pretrain 设置。","technical_approach":"论文从“把自回归生成写成有限时域 MDP”开始，把 state 定义为 prefix（或等价的对话历史），action 为下一个 token。关键推导有两步：\n1) 在行为策略 μ（rollout policy）采样下，推导 performance-difference identity。由 change of measure 得到：每个时间步的优势项 A^μ(s_t,a_t) 在用 π 更新时，其精确权重是前缀累计比值 r^{pre}_t=\\prod_{i\\le t} \\frac{\\pi(a_i|s_i)}{\\mu(a_i|s_i)}，而不是单步 token ratio。直觉是：要评价“在 t 时刻换策略”对期望回报的影响，必须同时纠正“到达该 prefix 的概率”已经变了。\n2) 把 TRPO 的 monotonic improvement 原理从 state-visitation 分布推广到“策略诱导的 prefix 分布”，从而信赖域应约束 KL( d_μ^{pre} || d_π^{pre} )（或等价方向），而其在 μ 下的一个精确坐标可以写成 reverse-KL coordinate：r^{pre}-1-\\log r^{pre}，其期望等于 KL( d_μ^{pre} || d_π^{pre} )。\n在实现上，EPIC 用 prefix ratio 做 clipping 的坐标与触发：不是对每个 token 的 r_t clip，也不是对整段 response 的单一 ratio clip，而是对“到当前步为止的累计 ratio”施加界，并把它转成可 token-level 计算的形式（避免显式枚举前缀分布）。这样在 off-policy 时能更直接地限制“前缀分布漂移”，从而稳定训练。\n论文还把 EPIC 放到 MoE 场景讨论：强 off-policy 时，routing replay 通过重放专家路由来减少分布漂移；EPIC 则从目标函数层面约束 prefix 分布变化，因此不需要存路由索引。","experiments":"摘要给了三组关键数字（全文主线也围绕它们组织）：\n- 在强 off-policy 的 MoE 训练中，EPIC 相比 GSPO 提升 6.50 个百分点（四个基准的设定在文中；这里的对比意图是“同为 sequence-level/response-level 思路，但 EPIC 的 trust region 更贴合自回归 change-of-measure”）。\n- 与 routing replay 相比：在四基准总体表现上具备竞争力，同时 driver-side 进程内存中位数降低 77.5%（相对 R2）与 93.0%（相对 R3）。\n- 论文也强调 EPIC 对异步 rollout、kernel 差异导致的 policy lag 更稳。\n建议在全文里重点核对：off-policy 程度如何量化（policy lag/ratio 分布）、clip 超参如何设、以及 prefix ratio 的数值稳定处理（长序列下乘积/对数域累计）。","reading_guide":"精读建议按“推导—目标—工程落地—MoE 结果”走：\n- 先精读 performance-difference identity 在 behavior-policy sampling 下的推导（通常是第 3 节附近）：这里决定了为什么必须用 prefix ratio，而不是 token ratio。\n- 再看 reverse-KL coordinate r^{pre}-1-\\log r^{pre} 的 KL 精确等价关系，以及它如何变成可 clip 的 surrogate（把 TRPO 的约束翻译成 PPO-style 目标）。\n- 实现细节要看：prefix ratio 的 token-level 递推计算、数值稳定（log-space）、以及与现有 GRPO/GSPO 的接口差异。\n- 实验部分优先看：强 off-policy MoE 设置、与 R2/R3 的内存与效果对比图表；这些最能说明它不是“换个 clip 公式”。\n值得深挖的问题：prefix-level trust region 是否会过度保守（长序列下 ratio 更易爆），以及它与 advantage 估计噪声、KL penalty/entropy 的交互。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Token-level PPO-style clipping for autoregressive LLMs measures policy change only via per-token conditional ratios, even though the conditioning prefix is itself policy-generated. In long responses and off-policy training (policy lag from asynchronous rollouts), this mismatch makes clipping a poor proxy for the true distribution shift and undermines trust-region-style stability.","importance":"A methodologically important alignment between autoregressive structure and TRPO/PPO theory.\n- It derives the exact change-of-measure for stepwise improvement terms under behavior-policy sampling: the correct importance weight is the cumulative prefix likelihood ratio, not the single-step token ratio.\n- It turns this into a prefix-distribution trust region and a practical clipped objective (EPIC).\n- In strongly off-policy MoE RL, EPIC can replace routing-replay mechanisms (R2/R3), reducing driver memory substantially while remaining competitive or better on multi-benchmark performance.\nDirectly relevant to RL post-training stability and throughput-oriented pipelines; conceptually relevant to any training loop where the model induces its own context distribution (self-sampling / continual settings).","technical_approach":"$65","experiments":"Key reported outcomes:\n- In strongly off-policy MoE training, EPIC outperforms GSPO by 6.50 percentage points.\n- Versus routing replay baselines, EPIC achieves competitive performance across four benchmarks while reducing median driver-side process memory by 77.5% (vs R2) and 93.0% (vs R3).\nThe experimental narrative centers on stability under policy lag and large distribution mismatch. When reading, verify how off-policy severity is measured (lag, ratio statistics), how clipping hyperparameters are set, and how prefix ratios are computed stably for long horizons (log-domain accumulation, truncation, etc.).","reading_guide":"Deep-read path:\n- Focus first on the behavior-sampling performance-difference derivation; it is the crux for why prefix ratios are the “exact” weights.\n- Then read the section proving/using E_μ[r_pre − 1 − log r_pre] = KL(d_μ^pre || d_π^pre) and how it becomes a clipped surrogate consistent with TRPO-style trust regions.\n- Implementation details matter: token-level recursion for prefix ratios, numerical stability, and how EPIC plugs into GRPO/GSPO-style code.\n- In experiments, prioritize the strongly off-policy MoE setup and the EPIC vs R2/R3 memory/performance tables/figures.\nOpen questions worth probing: whether prefix-level clipping becomes overly conservative for very long sequences (ratio explosion), and how it interacts with advantage noise and auxiliary KL/entropy regularizers.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"这篇会改变对 PPO token clipping 的默认信任；先复核推导，再看长回复 RL 实验是否撑得住。","en":"This should weaken default trust in PPO token clipping; verify the derivation first, then check whether long-response RL experiments hold up."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Chengxi Li","Hanlin Wu","Zhicheng Cai","Huan-ang Gao","Wei-Ying Ma","Jingjing Liu","Hao Zhou"],"notable_authors":["Wei-Ying Ma"],"tags":["LLM-RL","PPO","trust-region","importance-sampling","policy-optimization"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29999","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.29999","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2610.29498","title":"A Strong MoE Is All You Need for Process Supervision","abstract":"Process reward models (PRMs) provide fine-grained supervision for multi-step reasoning, yet obtaining reliable process labels remains a central bottleneck. Existing approaches typically rely on human annotation, rollout-based estimation, or auxiliary verification. In this work, we explore a different source of process supervision natively exposed by Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models: expert routing. We show that simple routing statistics provide an intrinsic process-confidence signal that is compe","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-21T09:51:01Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2610_29498.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文解决的是过程监督的信号来源问题：多步推理需要 step-level 反馈，但人工标注、rollout 估计、外部 verifier 或 outcome-derived supervision 都有额外成本。作者转向 MoE 模型在一次前向中自然产生的 expert routing，检验路由统计量能否作为推理步骤可靠性的内部信号。","importance":"对预训练研究的启发在于，它把 MoE routing 从负载均衡和计算分配信号，推进到 latent reasoning state 的观测窗口。这个方向连接了架构、训练动态和 post-train 过程监督：如果 routing 确实稳定携带 step quality 信息，那么 MoE 预训练时形成的专家分工可能不只是服务于 next-token loss，也能为后续 verifier、PRM、test-time correction 提供低成本监督。贡献量级偏方法观察和系统集成，不是新的预训练 recipe，但对理解 MoE 内部表征有参考价值。","technical_approach":"方法核心是构造 routing confidence。作者从每个 MoE 层的 top-k expert routing probability mass 出发，对同一推理轨迹内的每层路由质量做 min-max normalization，再跨层平均得到 token-level routing signal。随后聚合到 reasoning step，形成三个统计量：overall routing mass M，衡量整个步骤的路由集中度或强度；head routing mass H，强调步骤开头 token 的路由状态，因为一步推理的方向往往在开头确定；within-step routing variation D，衡量步骤内部路由波动，波动越大通常表示该步骤状态不稳定。作者观察到 M 和 H 与外部 PRM 的 step quality 多数正相关，D 多数负相关。基于这个信号有两个用法：RoCo-Rethink 在测试时找出 routing confidence 最低的步骤，保留其前缀并重新生成后续推理；RoCo-PRM 用强 MoE teacher 为推理轨迹打 routing-derived step score，再蒸馏成独立 PRM，使 dense model 或无法访问 routing 的模型也能使用这种过程监督。","experiments":"实验覆盖多个 MoE 架构和推理领域，包括数学、MMLU、代码任务。作者用外部 process verifier 作为参照，例如数学 PRM、VersaPRM 和 CodePRM，先做 within-trajectory Spearman correlation，验证 routing statistics 与 step quality 的方向关系。结果显示 routing-based predictors 经常优于传统 output-logit confidence。RoCo-Rethink 在 18 个评测设置中都超过最强 test-time reasoning baseline。RoCo-PRM 在 30 个设置中取得最好或并列最好的结果。消融结论比较关键：复合 routing confidence 比单一统计量更能找到有修正价值的步骤；RoCo-PRM 从更强 routing teacher 获益，幅度超过单纯增大 PRM backbone。这说明监督信号质量比 verifier 模型容量更关键。","reading_guide":"建议泛读 MoE interpretability，可以继续看不同 MoE 架构、不同任务域上 routing pattern 是否一致。需要留意一个限制：外部 PRM 被用作 step quality 参照，routing signal 与真实推理正确性的关系仍可能受 verifier 偏差影响。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks whether process supervision for multi-step reasoning can be obtained from signals already produced inside an MoE model, instead of relying on human labels, rollouts, external verifiers, or outcome-derived supervision. The specific candidate signal is expert routing during a normal forward pass.","importance":"The useful part for pretraining research is the reinterpretation of MoE routing as an observable trace of latent reasoning state. Routing is usually treated as a compute-allocation mechanism and a load-balancing problem. Here it is tested as a process-level confidence signal. If this behavior is robust, MoE pretraining may create internal structure that can be reused by post-training systems such as PRMs, verifiers, and test-time correction methods. The contribution is mainly an empirical signal and a post-training integration path, not a new pretraining algorithm.","technical_approach":"$66","experiments":"The experiments cover multiple MoE architectures and reasoning domains, including math, MMLU, and code. Step quality is compared against external process verifiers such as math PRMs, VersaPRM, and CodePRM using within-trajectory Spearman correlation. Routing-based confidence often beats output-logit confidence as a step reliability predictor. RoCo-Rethink outperforms the strongest test-time baseline in all 18 evaluated settings. RoCo-PRM reaches the best or tied-best result in all 30 settings. The ablations are the most informative part: combining M, H, and D is more reliable than using a single routing statistic, and stronger routing teachers help RoCo-PRM more than simply increasing the PRM backbone size. This points to supervision quality as the main driver.","reading_guide":"A 25–35 minute skim is enough for most readers. Focus on the routing-confidence construction and the experiments comparing routing confidence with logit confidence. The correlation analysis in Figure 1 is the key evidence for the whole method. The RoCo-Rethink and RoCo-PRM ablations are also worth reading because they show where the gains come from. For readers interested in MoE interpretability, the most relevant question is whether the same routing-quality relationship holds across architectures and task domains. One caveat is that external PRMs are used as references for step quality, so verifier bias may affect the measured relationship.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"只看路由置信度定义和 PRM 蒸馏实验；若信号稳定，内部 MoE 日志应纳入 reasoning 数据筛选 ablation。","en":"Read the routing-confidence definition and PRM distillation results; if stable, MoE routing logs deserve an internal ablation for reasoning-data filtering."},"metadata":{"institutions":["City University of Hong Kong","Carnegie Mellon University","University of Hong Kong"],"authors":["Guanzhi Deng","Zengyan Liu","Haibo Wang","Kuan Wu","Shing Yin Wong","Sichun Luo","Zhuoran Wang","Linqi Song"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["MoE","process-supervision","PRM","reasoning"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29498","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.29498","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2610.29785","title":"Where Do LLMs Learn to Recommend AI?","abstract":"Asked what to study, which career to pursue, or what product to build, large language models (LLMs) often recommend AI-related options. ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-21T14:13:52Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2610_29785.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI","cs.CY","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要回答的不是“LLM 会不会偏向推荐 AI”，而是这个倾向究竟来自哪里。作者把来源拆成四类：预训练先验、post-training、系统上下文中的 AI 身份提示、以及用户问题措辞。核心问题因此变成一个训练分解问题：当一个指令模型在开放式建议任务里频繁给出 AI/ML 相关推荐时，究竟是 base model 已经学到了“AI=更有前景”的关联，还是后续对齐数据和偏好信号把这种关联进一步放大了。","importance":"对预训练研究的意义主要有两层。第一，它把一个看似“对齐后行为偏差”的现象，拆回到 pretrain 与 post-train 的接口上，说明很多行为不能只看最终 instruct checkpoint。第二，它给出一个很具体的数据配方信号：post-training 对开放式建议分布的影响，不是来自“做了 instruction tuning”这件事本身，而是来自训练数据里哪些主题被频繁示范、哪些回答风格被偏好模型奖励。对做基座模型的人，这意味着行为倾向可以被少量主题数据和偏好目标显著改写，且方向未必与 base prior 一致。它不是在提出新的训练算法，但对理解数据混合、helpfulness cue，例如“best”“right now”，随后再单独测试措辞效应。度量指标也刻意收窄：不是泛泛判断“是否偏向 AI”，而是统计回答中的推荐部分是否出现显式 AI/ML 术语。作者先用固定词表抓候选，再用两个独立 LLM 过滤器判断这些术语是否真的属于推荐内容，而不是背景解释或旁支评论；两个过滤器分别是 Qwen3.6-27B 和 DeepSeek-V4-Flash，最后对有效判定取平均。这个设计的好处是把测量对象限定为‘用户最终收到 AI 推荐的频率’，而不是更模糊的语义倾向。\n\n围绕四个假设，作者做了两类关键对照。第一类是 released Base–Instruct 配对比较，加上自己对 11 个 pretrained checkpoints 施加 6 种 post-training recipe，去看不同训练配方如何改变 pro-AI prominence。这里最重要的发现是：一般性的 SFT 并不会自动提高 AI 推荐率；真正拉高它的是训练集中 AI 相关示例的占比，以及偏好数据对“helpful”回答风格的奖励。相反，如果从指令数据里移除那 7% 与 AI 相关的样本，AI 推荐率会明显下降，甚至低于起始 base model。第二类是 inference-time 操作：改 system message 是否强调 AI 身份，以及改用户问题是否带 temporal/superlative cue。结果显示，措辞效应远大于系统里声明“你是 AI assistant”这类上下文提示，后者额外影响很小。\n\n统计上作者也比较谨慎。效应按问题级别配对，20 个问题是统计单元，10 个 paraphrase 不当作独立样本；置信区间用分层 bootstrap，显著性用对 20 个问题效应做 sign-flip exact test，并在预注册的假设家族内做 Holm 校正。这使得结论更像是对问题分布的稳健比较，而不是对大量近重复 prompt 的伪显著性。","experiments":"最关键的数字有几组。第一，base model 并非中性：在十组 open-weight Base–Instruct 配对里，Base checkpoint 平均有 26.80% 的回答会在推荐中显式提到 AI/ML；对应 released instruct 版本升到更高水平，平均增幅是 32.48 个百分点。摘要里给出的整体量级是 pretrained 约 27%，released instruction-tuned 约 59%，和正文趋势一致。第二，作者自己做 controlled post-training 后发现，这个上升不是 instruction tuning 的必然结果。一般性 SFT 基本不改变 AI 推荐率；但如果从指令数据中去掉那 7% 与 AI 相关的样本，pro-AI prominence 会下降 14 个百分点，而且低于起始 pretrained 模型。相反，偏向 helpfulness 的奖励数据会把该比例再抬高 9 个百分点。这个结果很关键，因为它说明行为分布对少量主题样本和偏好目标都很敏感。\n\n第三，关于测量可靠性，两个 LLM 过滤器给出的效应估计几乎一致，相关系数 r=0.990；在人类专家标注的 200 个 detector-positive 响应上，自动测量与人工一致率 85.2%，precision 96.9%，recall 86.6%。这意味着虽然指标是词表驱动的，但误差主要不是乱报，而是少量漏检。第四，prompt wording 的影响显著强于 system context 中的 AI cue；而把 assistant 明确说成 AI，并不会在已有 AI 相关上下文之外再带来显著增加。也就是说，用户问“现在最好的方向是什么”这类带时间性和极值词的问法，更容易把模型推向 AI 作为默认高势能答案。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读，但如果关心 pretrain/post-train 接口，建议重点看三处。第一是实验设计部分，尤其是指标定义、20 个问题如何构成统计单元、以及为什么不用 paraphrase 数量堆显著性。这里能看出作者在做行为归因时尽量避免常见评测陷阱。第二是“How Post-Training Changes Pro-AI Prominence”这一节，核心不是 released instruct 比 base 高，而是 controlled recipes 里哪些数据会抬高、哪些会压低该行为。看这里时要特别留意“general SFT 不变、去掉 7% AI 样本反而下降 14pt、helpfulness 奖励上升 9pt”这组三角关系。第三是 system context 与 wording 的对照实验。很多人会先怀疑‘因为系统提示里写了你是 AI’，但文中结果更支持 prompt semantics 在起作用。\n\n如果时间有限，先看 Figure 1 的四个假设框架、post-training 主结果图表、以及附录里 detector 验证部分。预计 25 到 35 分钟可以抓住主要结论。若要深挖，可以继续看附录中的不同 evaluator 一致性、各 domain 的异质性，以及不同模型家族在 base prior 上是否存在系统差异。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks a narrower and more useful question than “do LLMs over-recommend AI?” It tries to localize where that tendency comes from. The candidate sources are: pretraining priors, post-training, AI-related system context, and prompt wording. The real problem is therefore attribution: when an instruction-tuned model frequently recommends AI/ML in open-ended advice, how much of that behavior is already present in the base model, and how much is introduced or amplified later by instruction data and preference optimization.","importance":"Its value for pretraining research is not in a new algorithm, but in how it exposes the pretrain–post-train interface. Many behavioral patterns that look like “alignment artifacts” are partly inherited from pretraining and then selectively amplified by downstream data. The paper also gives a concrete recipe-level signal: generic instruction tuning does not automatically shift recommendation behavior, but a small slice of topic-specific data and helpfulness-oriented preference signals can move it a lot. For foundation-model work, that is a useful reminder that answer-distribution drift can be driven by data composition and reward targets, not just by the existence of post-training.","technical_approach":"$67","experiments":"$68","reading_guide":"$69","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"非常有趣的机制分析，证明了 SFT/RLHF 数据中的微小分布偏差（如 AI 相关话题偏多）会被模型放大，对 post-train 数据配比极具启发，只看实验设计和结论即可。","en":"Fascinating mechanism analysis proving that minor distribution biases in SFT/RLHF data (e.g., over-representation of AI topics) are amplified by the model; highly insightful for post-train data mixture, just read the experimental design and conclusions."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Bar-Ilan University","Affiliation:Bar-Ilan University"],"authors":["Benaya Trabelsi","Jonathan Shaki","Sarit Kraus"],"notable_authors":["Sarit Kraus"],"tags":["instruction_tuning","data_bias","alignment","model_behavior"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29785","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.29785","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2610.30132","title":"LongZero: Enhancing Long-Context Reasoning in LLMs from Zero Data","abstract":"Reinforcement learning (RL) is an effective way to strengthen the long-context reasoning of large language models (LLMs), but it needs training data that are long, diverse, challenging, and verifiable. deriv","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-21T03:59:23Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2610_29167.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI","cs.CL","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是 on-policy self-distillation 里的一个上游问题：Teacher 轨迹即使更准确，也未必更适合 Student 学。共享参数的 PI-conditioned Teacher 拿到参考解后，往往会生成更短、更依赖参考、缺少探索和自我校验的推理轨迹；Student 直接学这种轨迹，容易出现 thinking collapse，原本需要分叉、验证、回退的推理行为被压扁。论文要解决的是：如何构造一个“对 Student 友好”的 Teacher，让 privileged information 用来校验和纠偏，而不是直接把参考解压缩成不可迁移的答案轨迹。","importance":"它属于 post-train reasoning，但和预训练研究也有接口，因为它讨论的是训练信号质量，而不是单纯换一个优化器。更具体地说，这篇的价值在于把 OPSD 的问题从“Student 怎么学”往前推到“Teacher 应该怎么教”。很多工作只约束 Student 对 Teacher signal 的吸收方式，比如加 anchor、做 preference、去掉 reference-only 成分；这篇则指出，如果 Teacher rollout 本身已经被 privileged information 改写成不具可学性的风格，后面的 Student-side 修补只能部分缓解。这个视角对做 reasoning data synthesis、self-training、RLAIF/RLVR 里的 teacher construction 都有启发：高正确率监督不等于高可迁移监督，尤其当 teacher 拥有 student 不可见的信息时。","technical_approach":"TeachBack 的核心是双向偏好学习。Student 和 Teacher 不是两个网络，而是同一个参数共享策略的两种条件化角色：Student 只看题目，Teacher 额外看到 privileged information。与直接把完整参考解喂给 Teacher 不同，作者只给参考推理的一个 suffix，并把它包装成 optional working notes，同时改变 surrounding prompt style。这样做有两个目的：一是降低 Teacher 对参考 wording 和轨迹的依赖，二是迫使 Teacher 从头重新解题，把 PI 当作检查和纠偏信号，而不是照着抄。训练时会生成成对 rollout：Teacher trajectory 来自 problem + PI，Student trajectory 来自 problem-only。然后做 bidirectional preference updates。一个方向是 Teacher-origin trajectory 作为偏好目标，推动 Student 学到更可靠的解题路径；另一个方向是 Student-origin trajectory 反过来给 Teacher 提供 compatibility feedback，告诉 Teacher 哪类推理路径对无 PI 的 Student 更可复现、更可学习。也就是说，Teacher 不只是“更强”，还要被训练成“更会教”。从问题设定看，这和普通 DPO/偏好优化的区别在于，偏好不是单纯围绕答案正确性，而是围绕 teacher–student transferability。论文在引言里还把现有方法分成三类：优化层面缓解高熵分叉处的错配、监督信号层面去除 reference-induced style shift、学习目标层面用 preference 替代 token matching。TeachBack 的位置更靠前，它直接改造 PI-conditioned Teacher 的 rollout 生成机制，再用双向偏好把 Teacher 和 Student 的行为间隙压小。","experiments":"给出的全文片段没有完整表格和全部数字，但从方法描述和引言结论可以确定几件事。第一，作者显式比较了 Teacher rollout 与 Student rollout，观察到明显的 reasoning-behavior gap：Teacher 在 PI 条件下虽然更容易答对，但轨迹更短、更少探索、更少自检，这正是 thinking collapse 的来源。第二，TeachBack 在数学和逻辑推理任务上持续优于现有 OPSD 及相关基线，说明“让 Teacher 学会生成可教轨迹”比只在 Student 端做约束更有效。第三，关键消融应围绕两个组件：部分参考 suffix + prompt style 扰动是否真的降低 reference dependence；双向偏好是否优于单向由 Teacher 教 Student。按论文叙述，作者强调 access to sufficient PI 能显著提高 Teacher accuracy，但 accuracy alone does not guarantee suitability for Student learning，这通常意味着他们在实验里区分了 answer correctness 与 trajectory usefulness 两个维度。若精读全文，建议重点核对：不同 PI 长度下 Teacher 行为如何变化，response length / branching / verification token 的统计是否支持“更会教”的说法，以及 bidirectional objective 相对 PBSD、AD-OPSD、Purified OPSD、RLCSD 的增益主要来自哪一段训练过程。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读，重点不是公式本身，而是问题拆解。先看 Introduction，作者对 OPSD failure mode 的归因讲得比较清楚：PI 改变的不只是答案正确率，还会改变推理风格。然后看 Method 里 Teacher 构造部分，尤其是为什么只给 partial suffix、为什么要变 prompt style、为什么要求 Teacher 从头重解。再看 bidirectional preference 的定义，理解 Student trajectory 如何反过来约束 Teacher。最后看实验部分时，不要只盯最终准确率，优先找三类证据：Teacher 是否更少 reference-conditioned shortcut；Student 是否保留了探索/验证/回退行为；双向训练是否真的缩小了 teacher–student behavior gap。预计 20 分钟能抓住主线；如果你做 reasoning distillation 或合成 CoT 数据，值得再花时间看消融和 rollout case study。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses an upstream failure mode in on-policy self-distillation: a more accurate Teacher trajectory is not necessarily a better learning target for the Student. When a parameter-shared Teacher is conditioned on privileged information such as a reference solution, it often produces shorter, more reference-dependent reasoning that skips exploration, verification, and self-correction. If the Student is trained on such trajectories, it can suffer thinking collapse, losing the branching and recovery behaviors needed when privileged information is unavailable. The core problem is therefore how to construct a Teacher that uses privileged information for checking and correction rather than compressing the reference into an unteachable trajectory.","importance":"This is a post-training reasoning paper, but it matters for pretraining-adjacent work because it is fundamentally about supervision quality. Its main contribution is to shift attention from how the Student should consume teacher signals to how the Teacher should generate them. Many prior methods regulate Student-side learning: anchoring updates, removing reference-only components, or replacing token matching with preferences. TeachBack argues that if the PI-conditioned Teacher rollout is already distorted into a style the Student cannot reproduce, downstream fixes are limited. That perspective is useful for reasoning data synthesis, self-training, and teacher construction in RLAIF/RLVR settings: high correctness does not imply high transferability, especially when the teacher has access to information the student will never see.","technical_approach":"$6b","experiments":"$6c","reading_guide":"$6d","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"值得看方法和数据偏好定义；它提醒推理蒸馏别只筛正确答案，teacher 的“可学性”应进内部数据打分。","en":"Read the method and preference construction; it is a useful warning that reasoning distillation should score teacher traces for learnability, not only correctness."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Huazhong University of Science and Technology","Tsinghua University","Harbin Institute of Technology"],"authors":["Linfei Ji","Jiayun Li","Haozhao Wang","Siyu Jiang","Zehao Wang","Longtao Xiao","Yu-Jie Yuan","Ruixuan Li"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["self-distillation","preference-optimization","reasoning-data","post-training"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29167","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.29167","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2610.29552","title":"Generalized CycleGRPO for Fine-Grained Multimodal Understanding and Grounding","abstract":"Fine-grained multimodal intelligence requires models to both understand a designated region and localize it from language.
