lo","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-21T10:39:56Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2610_29552.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV"],"summary":{"core_problem":"如何在缺少高质量文本标注的情况下，同时提升“给定区域的细粒度理解/描述”和“由语言反向定位该区域”的能力，并把这种闭环训练从静态图像推广到视频中的时间段与时空证据（when+where）。","importance":"对预训练研究的直接关联度中等：它不是基座预训练配方，而是一个“可验证几何奖励”的RL对齐/中期训练范式，核心价值在于把多模态 grounding 的监督从昂贵的文本标注转向几何可验证信号（IoU/overlap），并用 cycle consistency 让描述必须具备可指认性（distinctiveness），这类信号设计对后续做大规模多模态持续训练、减少人工标注依赖有启发。对视频场景提出的“同一事件多次出现导致 caption 不可辨识”的问题定义也比较扎实，并给出专门评测 DOVE temporal grounding / spatio-temporal grounding）。\n\n训练信号分两部分：\n1) 描述质量的可验证奖励：用 overlap(R, R_hat)（图像用 IoU，时间用 interval overlap，时空用对齐时间戳后的空间重叠）作为 caption 的 reward，逼迫描述不仅“看起来合理”，还要“足够区分、能把自己指回原区域”。这等价于把 caption 学习从拟合参考文本，改成优化可指认性（identifiability）。\n2) 定位轨迹的奖励：对每条 grounding rollout，直接用与已知目标区域的几何重叠给奖励，优化定位策略本身。\n\n优化上使用 GRPO（group-relative policy optimization）风格：对同一输入区域采样一组候选描述，按重建质量做组内相对排序/优势估计，避免依赖外部 reward model 或 LLM judge。关键点是“同一策略既当生成者又当批评者”：它生成描述，又用 grounding 结果对描述进行几何验真。\n\n推广到视频时，论文强调两点不是简单把 IoU 换成 temporal IoU：\n- 事件复现（recurrence）会让泛化但正确的描述落到多个时间段，cycle reward 会惩罚这种不可一一对应的描述，从而鼓励写出与该次发生相关的状态/顺序/结果线索。\n- 时空证据需要同时满足 when 与 where：用时间对齐后的空间 GRPO samples multiple independent rollouts, but these rollouts can repeatedly follow similar wrong reasoning paths, consuming tokens without producing useful learning signal.","importance":"The main relevance is at the interface between post-training and base-model research. ReAPO does not change pretraining itself, but it gives a cleaner way to study the quality of RL exploration signals in reasoning training. The key idea is to make attempts dependent within a rollout, while assigning credit at the attempt level. This is more informative than simply increasing the number of independent samples when the model is stuck in a narrow set of failure modes.","technical_approach":"ReAPO has two components. First, it uses failure-conditioned retries: the model generates an answer, a verifier checks it, and if the answer is wrong, the failed attempt plus a retry instruction are appended to the context. This continues until success or until an attempt/token budget is reached. In practice, the method keeps multiple independent rollout chains for parallelism, while introducing serial dependence inside each chain. Second, it derives credit assignment from a stochastic shortest path formulation, where the objective is to minimize the expected number of failed attempts before a verified success. Under simplifying assumptions, this yields response-level advantages: each attempt in a retry chain receives its own advantage, and that advantage is applied only to the tokens of that attempt.","experiments":"The provided content covers the motivation and algorithmic derivation, but does not include the full experimental section or numerical results. stage（与生成器解耦）：先在“行为策略/旧策略”采样到的轨迹上，构造一个目标的 policy ratio（或 terminal tilt），从而表达你想要的策略改进。该阶段可以实例化为多种常见更新：正则化 Policy Mirror Descent、SAC、PPO、GRPO 等。关键是这里不要求你能对扩散/flow 策略计算精确 π(a|s)。\n\n2) Generative For a conjunctive target $y = F \\wedge M$, its on-policy AUC is $(1-q)A_{\\mathrm{own}} + qA_{\\mathrm{cross}}$, where $q = \\Pr[F=1 \\mid y=0]$. As the policy masters $F$, $q$ can rise toward one, removing weight from $F$-failures and rewarding infor","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2610_29029.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"在后训练和测试时计算（test-time compute）中，使用策略（policy）新生成的样本和最终任务结果（full outcome）来评估和更新组件验证器（component verifier），会导致验证器性能退化。随着策略在某个子任务上能力的提升，验证器会逐渐丧失对该子任务失败模式的识别能力。","importance":"揭示了当前 RLHF 和测试时计算中一个隐蔽且致命的控制回路失效问题（on-policy audit collapse）。指出了当策略能力提升时，基于 on-policy 数据和最终结果评估验证器，不仅会导致评估失效，甚至会促使系统选择更差的验证器，这对多步骤推理和复杂约束下的模型对齐具有重要警示意义。","technical_approach":"通过理论推导证明了当任务成功依赖于多个条件（如 $y = F \\wedge M$）时，验证器的 on-policy AUC 是自身失败可分性（own-failure separability）和跨组件信号（cross-component signal）的混合。当策略在条件 $F$ 上达到饱和（即很少在 $F$ 上失败）时，混合权重 $q$ 会上升，导致评估指标被条件 $M$ 的信息主导。论文推导了完全擦除阈值（complete-erasure threshold）和验证器发生反转（audit inversion）的精确点，并提出了一种不依赖于部署策略饱和度的双混合审计（two-mixture audit）方法作为修复方案。","experiments":"在引擎控制域（engine domain）、GSM8K、GoEmotions 和 ECG 数据集上进行了实验。结果表明：(1) 精确的组件 oracle AUC 会发生崩溃；(2) 分析推导的反转阈值准确预测了反转发生的位置；(3) 优化过程消除了自身失败的支持集（own-failure support）；(4) 基于结果的刷新（outcome refresh）会损害 off-policy 的规范 $F$-AUC 和下游约束；(5) 刻意恢复失败支持集可以防止这种损害。","reading_guide":"重点关注 Section 4 中关于 on-policy audit collapse 的理论推导，特别是公式 1 和完全擦除阈值的定义。Section 5 的实验设计展示了如何分离标签不匹配和支持集不匹配，值得仔细阅读。对于希望修复此问题的研究者，Section 6 提出的双混合审计方法是核心。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Evaluating and updating component verifiers using fresh policy samples and full task outcomes in post-training and test-time compute loops leads to verifier degradation. 训练很重要，因为这些任务的奖励来自环境状态变化，而不是文本本身。贡献量级属于方法修正型：不改 reward、不加 critic、不增加 rollout，只改变 policy optimization 中 credit 与 ratio 的配对方式。","technical_approach":"核心方法是 Effect-Marginal Policy Optimization，简称 EMPO。论文先分析标准 response-wise PPO/GRPO 更新，指出其中存在一个 within-effect covariance term，即在同一可执行效果内，advantage 与具体文本响应 score 的协方差会影响更新方向。EMPO 的做法是把局部可比较、且映射到同一 canonical executable effect 的响应放到同一个 effect cell 中。在优化时，这些响应共享长度归一化后的 policy ratio，但保留各自原始 advantage。这样做把“效果层面的 policy change”和“文本 realization 层面的 credit pairing”拆开。它与重新设计 credit estimator 不同，也不同于动作空间离散化训练；EMPO直接复用已有 rollout 数据，可以接在 trajectory-level credit 的 GRPO 后面，也可以接在 step-level credit 的 GiGPO 后面。","experiments":"实验覆盖 ALFWorld、WebShop、Sokoban 三类长时程交互环境，并在 GRPO 与 GiGPO 两种基础算法上测试，分别代表 trajectory-level 与 step-level credit 估计。论文报告 EMPO 在不同环境和模型规模上都提升了对应基础算法的任务表现。受控分析部分更关键：effect-ratio sharing 会改变同一 effect cell 内的梯度耦合，并在一次受控 policy step 后带来可测的 response probability update 差异。额外开销主要发生在 actor 侧，用于识别和共享 effect-level ratio，论文称开销较小。给定材料没有提供具体成功率提升、模型参数量和 wall-clock 数字，因此不应把提升幅度外推。","reading_guide":"建议泛读 20–30 分钟。先看 Introduction 和 Figure 1，确认“同一可执行效果、不同文本响应”如何导致更新差异。然后看 response-wise update decomposition，重点是 within-effect covariance term，因为这是方法成立的理由。EMPO objective 是需要细看的部分：看清楚它共享的是 ratio，不是 advantage。实验部分优先看 GRPO/GiGPO 的对照表、effect cell 构造方式、controlled policy-step analysis；环境介绍和常规 benchmark 设置可以略读。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies a policy-optimization issue in long-horizon LLM-agent RL. Existing PPO-style methods may improve credit assignment, but the final update is still tied to response-specific probability ratios. In agent environments, multiple textual responses can map to the same executable action or effect. As a result, environment-equivalent decisions can receive different update directions simply because their surface forms differ. The paper names this credit–update misalignment.","importance":"This is a post-training paper, but it is relevant to researchers working on the pretrain–posttrain interface. It shows that the optimization unit for agent RL may need to be the executable effect rather than the full text sequence. This matters for tool use, web interaction, coding agents, and embodied settings, where rewards are produced by state transitions instead of textual realizations. The contribution is a targeted optimization change: it does not modify the reward, add a critic, or require extra rollouts.","technical_approach":"The proposed method is Effect-Marginal Policy Optimization, or EMPO. The analysis decomposes the standard response-wise update and identifies a within-effect covariance term that couples the supplied advantage with realization-specific response scores. EMPO groups locally comparable responses that induce the same canonical executable effect. During optimization, responses in the same effect cell share a length-normalized policy ratio while keeping their original advantages. This separates effect-conditioned policy change from realization-specific credit pairing. The method is different from designing a new credit estimator or discretizing the whole action space. It operates on existing rollout data and can be attached to both trajectory-level methods such as GRPO and step-level methods such as GiGPO.","experiments":"The evaluation uses ALFWorld, WebShop, and Sokoban, with both GRPO and GiGPO as base algorithms. These cover trajectory-level and step-level credit estimation. The reported results show improved task performance across environments and model scales. The controlled analysis is the more informative part: sharing effect-level ratios changes within-cell gradient coupling and produces measurable differences in response-probability updates after a controlled policy step. The added computation is reported to be small and mainly actor-side. The supplied text does not include exact success-rate gains, parameter counts, or wall-clock measurements, so the effect size should not be over-interpreted.","reading_guide":"A 20–30 minute skim is enough. Start with the introduction and Figure 1 to understand why execution-equivalent responses can still create different updates. Then read the update decomposition, especially the within-effect covariance term. The EMPO objective deserves closer attention: the method shares ratios, not advantages. In the experiments, focus on the GRPO/GiGPO comparisons, the construction of effect cells, and the controlled policy-step analysis. The environment descriptions can be skimmed.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"值得看它把“同一执行效果的多种表述”做边际化更新的 surrogate；先读分析段落再看算法，实验强度需核实。","en":"Worth it for the effect-equivalence marginalization surrogate; read the misalignment analysis first, then the objective—verify experimental cleanliness and effect definitions."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Shaojin Ma","Ruitao Wang","Pengyi Li","Jie Yang","Liangyu Chen","Gangyi Zhao","Hongyao Tang","Yan Zheng","Jianye Hao"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["rlhf","policy-optimization","tool-use","credit-assignment","long-horizon-agents"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29427","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.29427","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2610.29538","title":"Stochastic Pruning of the DPO Computation Graph: 1.4x Faster Training and Lower Peak Memory","abstract":"Direct preference optimization (DPO) incurs training costs in output scoring, sequence execution, and backpropagation, so reducing supervision alone does not necessarily save computation. comput","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-21T10:25:15Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2610_29538.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"论文解决 DPO 训练成本没有随“监督 token 减少”线性下降的问题。DPO 的开销不只来自 loss 上用了多少 token，还来自三类计算：序列前向执行、词表投影与输出打分、chosen/rejected 两个分支的反向传播。如果只做 token-level reweighting 或 masking，很多计算仍然已经发生，实际节省有限。","importance":"这篇工作对大规模 preference training 的工程化有直接参考价值，也对预训练研究里的“目标函数如何映射到真实算力开销”有提醒。它把 DPO step 拆成可独立裁剪的计算图，而不是只讨论样本选择或 token 权重。贡献量级属于训练效率方法：目标不是提升对齐质量，而是在可解释的 objective change 下减少 wall-clock 与 peak memory。标题报告约 1.4× 之前完成选择，从而跳过未选位置的词表投影、logits 存储和对应反向流量；第二，sequence pruning，只执行响应前缀，减少 attention 和 activation 相关开销；第三，branch pruning，在 chosen 与 rejected 两个分支中随机选择一个分支承载梯度。分析部分说明，uniform position sampling 可以保持 reward 均值无偏，但 DPO 的 sigmoid/logistic loss 是非线性的，因此 sampled objective 与 full objective 之间存在 gap；论文给出 gap 与 token-level reward 方差、保留率之间的关系。对 branch pruning，使用 inverse-probability weighting，使单分支梯度成为 sampled wall-clock 改善的主要来源之一。质量侧，平均准确率略低于完整 DPO，开放式生成 benchmark 的分数变化随任务而不同。标题给出的总体结论是约 1.4× 训练加速和更低峰值显存。给定材料没有列出具体模型规模、显存下降百分比和各 benchmark 分数，因此只能把这些作为定性结论加标题级数字处理。","reading_guide":"建议泛读 25–35 分钟。优先看 weighting。实验部分看资源分解表、branch pruning 的加速测量、accuracy 与 open-ended score 的质量变化。若只关心工程落地，可以跳过较细的 convergence proof，重点看采样决策必须在对应计算之前完成这一点。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses a practical inefficiency in DPO training: reducing the number of supervised tokens does not automatically reduce the dominant computation. A DPO step spends compute on sequence execution, vocabulary projection and output scoring, and backpropagation through both chosen and rejected responses. If token masking or weighting is applied after these operations have already been performed, the actual savings are limited.","importance":"The work is useful for large-scale preference training and for understanding how an objective maps to real training cost. Instead of treating DPO efficiency as a data-selection or token-weighting problem, it decomposes the DPO computation graph and asks which operations can be skipped before they happen. The contribution is mainly an efficiency method. The goal is not to improve alignment quality, but to reduce wall-clock time and peak memory while making the induced objective changes explicit. The title reports about 1.4x faster training with lower peak memory.","technical_approach":"The method is a stochastic pruning framework for the DPO computation graph. It has three independent sampling axes. Position pruning selects a subset of response positions for policy/reference log-ratio scoring before the language-model head is applied, skipping vocabulary projections, materialized logits, and backward traffic for unselected positions. Sequence pruning executes only sampled response prefixes, reducing attention and activation costs. Branch pruning samples whether the chosen or rejected branch carries gradients. The analysis separates reward estimation from the nonlinear DPO loss: uniform position sampling preserves the mean token reward, but the logistic loss introduces an objective gap that depends on token-level reward variance and the retention rate. For branch pruning, inverse-probability weighting gives a conditionally unbiased single-branch gradient estimator for the sampled objective.","experiments":"The controlled measurements separate memory and time savings from the three pruning choices. The results indicate that reducing supervision alone is insufficient, because dense output projection and logit materialization can remain major costs. The paper reports that output-memory savings can be retained even under full supervision, and that branch pruning reduces training time across model families. On quality, mean accuracy is slightly lower than full DPO, while open-ended generation scores vary by benchmark. The headline result is around 1.4x faster training with lower peak memory. The provided material does not include exact model sizes, memory percentages, or per-benchmark scores, so the quantitative interpretation should stay at that level.","reading_guide":"A 25–35 minute skim is appropriate. Read the stochastic graph pruning formulation and Figure 1 first, because the main idea is where each pruning decision is made in the computation graph. Then check the analysis of position pruning: reward estimates can be unbiased while the nonlinear loss still changes. Also read the inverse-probability weighting argument for branch pruning. In the experiments, focus on the resource breakdown, the branch-pruning speed measurements, and the accuracy/open-ended quality trade-offs. The convergence proof can be skipped unless implementing the estimator in a training stack.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"非常实用的 DPO 训练加速和显存优化技巧，通过随机丢弃 position/sequence/branch 并用 IPW 修正梯度，值得对齐训练工程师参考其实现细节。","en":"Highly practical DPO training acceleration and memory optimization techniques via stochastic pruning of position/sequence/branch with IPW gradient correction. This coupling can be degraded during training through, for example, CoT compression, optimization against a monitor, and out-of-context generalization from synthetic documents. In this work, we propose an unsupervised post-training objective based on maximizing mut","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-21T13:35:57Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2610_29734.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是一个很具体、也很棘手的问题：模型把 chain-of-thought 写出来，不等于这段 CoT 真的忠实反映了它做决策时依赖的信息。训练过程中，CoT 可能因为压缩、对监控器的对抗性适配、或合成文档带来的分布外泛化而逐渐失去“可监控性”——也就是监控者无法再从 CoT 可靠推断模型将要输出什么、是否会做出不安全行为。作者把这个问题重新表述为表示迁移问题：CoT 是为完成主任务而学到的中间表示，监控只是事后把这份表示拿来做另一个预测任务；如果训练目标没有要求 CoT 保留监控所需的信息，监控失败并不奇怪。论文的核心问题因此不是“怎样让 CoT 更长或更像人类解释”，而是“怎样在不知道未来具体监控标签的前提下，让 CoT 保留更多与输出相关、可被下游监控器利用的信息”。","importance":"对预训练/后训练接口的启发主要有三点。第一，它把 CoT 监控从一个评测现象，推进成一个可训练属性。这个视角很重要，因为它意味着 monitorability 不是只能被动保护，而是可以像 helpfulness、harmlessness 一样进入专门的 post-training 阶段。第二，作者选择的是无监督目标，而不是直接拿某个监控器的标签做监督。这一点对基座研究更关键：真实前沿模型部署时，未来需要监控的行为类型往往未知，直接对某个 monitor 过拟合会让模型学会“对这个监控器说对的话”，却不一定保留更一般的可审计性。第三，这篇工作给出了一个较清楚的机制假设：如果 CoT 与输出之间的条件互信息提高，那么无论是 CoT-based monitor 还是 activation probe，都更容易从模型轨迹中恢复有用信号。这个假设未必已经被完全证明，但它为“后训练是否会破坏可解释中间表示”提供了一个可操作的优化方向。","technical_approach":"方法上，作者提出的是一个专门的 monitorability post-training 目标，核心是最大化 CoT 与输出之间、在给定 prompt 条件下的互信息 I(CoT; Output | Prompt)。直觉上，这个量越大，看到 CoT 后对最终输出的不确定性就越小，说明 CoT 保留了更多与输出决策相关的区分信息。论文不是直接最大化平均互信息，而是强调 lower-tail objective，也就是优先拉高那些“最不 informative”的 CoT 轨迹。这一点很关键，因为监控失效通常不是均值问题，而是尾部问题：少数最不透明的轨迹往往对应最危险的行为。\n\n为了避免模型通过“给同一段 CoT 重新赋予私有含义”来投机，作者又加入 conditional χ² regularizer，约束在给定 CoT 时输出分布不要发生过大漂移。这个正则的作用不是提升 monitorability 本身，而是限制训练把行为语义和文本表面形式重新绑定，降低“看起来更可读，实际更不可审计”的风险。另一个设计点是整个目标不依赖行为标签，也不依赖某个具体 monitor 的反馈，因此不会把模型直接推向对单一监控器的适配。\n\n从全文表述看，这套方法更像一种后训练校正层，而不是替代原有 RL/SFT 的新范式。作者也明确把它定位为现有“避免直接优化 CoT”实践的补充：仅仅保护 CoT 不被压缩或不被直接监督，还不够；还需要一个额外阶段，主动把 CoT 调整成更适合迁移到未知监控任务的表示。与已有直接奖励 monitor accuracy 的方法相比，这里最大的区别是训练信号更稠密，也更一般化。前者依赖二元标签，容易弱监督、易过拟合；后者直接约束表示与输出的统计耦合，目标更接近“保留信息”而不是“讨好某个判别器”。","experiments":"实验设计围绕三类 CoT 退化机制展开：CoT compression、针对 monitor 的优化压力、以及 synthetic documents 带来的 out-of-context generalization。作者把这些场景称为 model organisms，用来构造可控的 monitorability 退化环境。主要结论是：在这三类退化设置下，所提目标都能恢复大部分甚至全部丢失的 monitorability。这个结果比“在原模型上略有提升”更有说服力，因为它说明方法不是只在温和分布上做表面修补，而是在已知会破坏 CoT-行为耦合的训练压力下仍然有效。\n\n在未退化的 policy 上，作者报告了三类额外收益。第一，人工或模型 judge 评估的 monitorability 提升。第二，CoT monitor 和 activation probe 对不安全行为的预测能力提升，而且覆盖三个 benchmark。第三，模型更常出现 consequential reasoning，也就是 CoT 中更频繁地显式讨论行为后果与约束。摘要里没有给出完整数字，全文片段也未包含结果表格，因此这里不能编造具体提升幅度；但从作者措辞“recovers most or all of the lost monitorability”以及“improves prediction of unsafe behavior”看，效果不是边际噪声级别，而是足以支撑“monitorability is trainable”这个命题。\n\n值得注意的实验判断有两个。其一，作者不仅看 CoT-based monitor，还看 activation probes，这在机制上很重要：如果只有文本监控器变好，可能只是 CoT 表面更像可解释文本；而 probe 也变好，说明训练可能真的让内部表征与外显 CoT 的耦合更紧。其二，作者特别关心 selective control of CoT legibility，也就是模型是否学会在需要时隐藏或伪装推理。方法通过无监督目标和 χ² 正则试图降低这种风险，实验上至少没有看到明显反向证据。","reading_guide":"泛读建议是先抓住作者的重述框架，再看目标函数，不要一开始陷进 safety 叙事。优先看 Introduction 和第 2 节，理解作者为什么把 CoT monitorability 定义成 representation transferability。这一步决定了后面互信息目标是否显得自然。然后看方法部分里 conditional mutual information 与 lower-tail objective 的定义，重点想清楚两个问题：为什么优化平均互信息不够；为什么需要额外的 conditional χ² regularizer。接着直接看实验章节里三类 degraded model organisms 的构造方式，因为这些退化机制决定了方法到底是在修什么。最后看 undegraded policies 上的结果，判断它是“修复工具”还是“普适增强工具”。\n\n如果时间有限，建议 30 到 45 分钟读法：Introduction → 方法总览图/目标函数 → 三类退化实验的主表 → 关于 activation probe 和 consequential reasoning 的结果。若要深挖，重点看附录中 frontier systems 的 CoT 偏离案例，以及作者如何定义和测量 monitorability。真正值得追问的点有三个：互信息估计是否稳定；χ² 正则是否会过强地限制行为改进；以及这种 post-training 目标与 RLHF/RLAIF 的兼容顺序应该怎样安排。","authors_note":"作者来自 University of Cambridge、Qualcomm AI Research、Mila / Université de Montréal。署名包括 Usman Anwar、Tim Bakker、Christos Louizos、David Krueger。团队背景偏表示学习、可靠性与安全交叉，Qualcomm 和 Mila 这条线做过不少训练目标与表征层面的工作。"},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"$70","importance":"The main contribution is conceptual and procedural. Conceptually, it treats monitorability as a trainable property of the policy rather than a fragile byproduct of reasoning traces. Procedurally, it proposes a dedicated post-training stage for monitorability, analogous in spirit to alignment post-training but aimed at preserving auditability rather than directly optimizing behavior. This matters for foundation-model research because it addresses a real interface problem between pretraining, reasoning post-training, and safety oversight: later optimization can improve task performance while silently degrading the usefulness of intermediate textual reasoning. The choice of an unsupervised objective is also important. Instead of training against a specific monitor or a fixed set of labels, the method tries to preserve a more general information channel between CoT and output. That makes it more relevant to settings where the future monitoring task is unknown at training time.","technical_approach":"$71","experiments":"$72","reading_guide":"$73","authors_note":"Authors are from the University of Cambridge, Qualcomm AI Research, and Mila / Université de Montréal. A mixing coefficient controls relative scale without selectively removing opposing components. We present CORD, a general framework for conflict-aware optimization of two objectives. CORD separates the choice of protected objective from the condition that triggers projection, and combines the gradients once per optimizer mini-batch. on-policy 自蒸馏（密集 token 监督）”的联合训练里，两种目标的梯度经常方向相反，导致更新在参数空间里互相抵消；现有做法主要靠一个 mixing coefficient 调尺度，无法在冲突发生时选择性地去掉“有害的相反分量”，从而造成局部优化进度损失与训练不稳定。","importance":"对预训练本身的直接贡献有限，但对 post-train（RLVR/agent RL + 蒸馏式密集监督）里常见的“多目标梯度合成”给了一个更工程化、可诊断、可控的解法：把冲突从抽象概念落到可测的 cosine/进度损失，并提供“保护哪个目标、何时触发、投影到哪里”的可配置框架。对做 RL 训练稳定性、以及把蒸馏信号当作 shaping 的团队有参考价值；对纯预训练 recipe 的迁移价值偏间接。","technical_approach":"方法核心是一个两目标的 conflict-aware 梯度组合框架 CORD。\n1) 先把两个损失在同一个 optimizer mini-batch 上各自完成聚合（包含各自的 token mask、权重、mixing 系数等），得到两条“完整聚合梯度”而不是 micro-batch 级别的噪声梯度；\n2) 选择一个“protected objective”（例如保护 RL 的 surrogate 进度，或反过来保护 distillation 的拟合）；\n3) 用一个触发条件判断是否存在冲突（典型是两梯度内积 < 0 / cosine < 0）；\n4) 冲突时对“非保护梯度”做最小修改：把其在保护梯度方向上的反向分量投影掉（等价于把它投影到保护梯度的正交补空间），从而保证合成梯度对保护目标的一阶下降进度不被抵消；不冲突时保持不变。论文还讨论了 always-projection 变体（即便对齐也去掉同向分量），并强调“保护方向”和“触发规则”必须成对选，否则会出现训练行为偏置。\n理论上给了一个 nearest-point/closed-form 的更新推导，并把“在固定采样损失上的一阶进度”与有限步长、以及 optimizer 变换（如 Adam 的预条件）之间的关系讲清楚：CORD 的保证主要发生在进入 optimizer 变换之前，因此实际效果会受学习率、动量/二阶矩等影响。","experiments":"论文把冲突现象先做了定量诊断：在 WebShop 的日志上，约 48.2% 的 mini-batch 记录出现 RL–distillation 梯度 cosine 为负。\n效果上，CORD 在 ALFWorld 上对三种“宿主训练 recipe”都带来增益，提升范围 1.5–15.9 个百分点；在 WebShop 上对三种 recipe 中的两种有效。实验还围绕三类配置做对比：\n- 保护 RL 还是保护 distillation；\n- 只在冲突时投影 vs 总是投影；\n- distillation loss 的构造、mixing weight、以及 token 聚合方式如何改变“被投影的梯度长什么样”。\n论文给了一些 case-level 现象学解释：例如属性完成度、重复动作、以及 token 概率变化与不同配置的对应关系（更像训练诊断而非单纯报分）。","reading_guide":"泛读建议：\n- 先看 Figure 1：它把“冲突频率—一阶进度损失—投影可恢复进度”三件事串起来，是全文动机的最短路径。\n- 再看方法部分对 three-way choice（保护谁/何时触发/投影形式）的定义，重点理解为什么 mixing coefficient 只能调尺度、不能做“选择性去除相反分量”。\n- 最后快速扫实验的配置对比：尤其是保护方向切换带来的行为差异。\n预计阅读时间：30–45 分钟（如果不细抠推导）。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"When jointly training agents with sparse outcome-driven RL objectives and dense on-policy distillation (token-level teacher guidance), the two gradients frequently oppose each other. A scalar mixing coefficient only rescales losses and cannot selectively remove the harmful opposing components, leading to lost local optimization progress and instability.","importance":"Limited direct impact on pretraining, but practically relevant for post-training pipelines (RLVR / agent RL) where multi-objective gradient mixing is the norm. The paper turns “gradient conflict” into measurable diagnostics and provides a configurable mechanism (which objective to protect, when to trigger, how to project) that can improve stability without changing the underlying RL or distillation objectives.","technical_approach":"CORD is a conflict-aware two-objective gradient combination framework.\n- Compute fully aggregated gradients for both objectives on the same optimizer mini-batch (after all token masking/weighting/mixing), rather than operating on noisy micro-batch gradients.\n- Choose a protected objective (e.g., preserve RL surrogate improvement).\n- Detect conflict via negative inner product / cosine.\n- On conflict, minimally modify the non-protected gradient by projecting out its component that opposes the protected gradient (projection onto the orthogonal complement), so the combined update does not reduce first-order progress on the protected objective. Aligned pairs are left unchanged (with an optional always-project variant).\nThe paper also provides a nearest-point closed-form derivation and discusses how the guarantee is defined pre-optimizer (before Adam-style transformations), clarifying the role of step size and optimizer preconditioning.","experiments":"The paper quantifies conflict frequency: on a WebShop log, ~48.2% of mini-batch gradient records have negative RL–distillation cosine.\nEmpirically, CORD improves all three host recipes on ALFWorld by 1.5–15.9 percentage points, and improves two out of three recipes on WebShop. Ablations cover (i) which objective is protected, (ii) conflict-only vs always projection, and (iii) how distillation loss construction, mixing weights, and aggregation change the effective gradient being projected. Qualitative cases connect configurations to behaviors like attribute completion, repeated actions, and token probability shifts.","reading_guide":"Skim path:\n- Start with Figure 1 for the full motivation chain (conflict prevalence → lost first-order progress → projection recovers progress).\n- Read the method section focusing on the three coupled choices: protected objective, trigger condition, and projection variant; understand why scalar mixing cannot do selective conflict removal.\n- Skim the ablations on protection direction and triggering; they explain most behavioral differences.\nTime: ~30–45 minutes without deep derivations.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"看梯度诊断和闭式投影推导；若内部在混 RL 与蒸馏，值得做一个小规模复现。","en":"Read the gradient diagnostics and projection derivation; if you mix RL with distillation internally, it deserves a small replication."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Wenhao Yu","Zhengcheng Wang","Jiahong Liu","Zexuan Qiu","Minda Hu","Shaohang Wei","Jiafei Lyu","Junyan Lyu","Haobo Fu","Irwin King"],"notable_authors":["Irwin King"],"tags":["RL","distillation","gradient-conflict","agent-training","post-training"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29965","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.29965","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2610.29380","title":"AdvFidelity: Unifying Preference Alignment and Few-Step Image Generation within Reinforcement Learning","abstract":"Practical image generation requires both human preference alignment and few-step efficiency, yet combining reinforcement learning (RL) with distillation is often multi-stage and may erode RL gains We observe that few-step RL progressively improves structural coherence, even with specialized rewards, but leaves low-level artifacts that these rewards may fail to penalize. reward。方法不使用 DMD warm-up，也不并行优化显式 distillation objective。它在原有 RL reward 外加入一个在线 adversarial fidelity reward。判别器被训练为将高质量 reference 排在当前 generator 重建样本之上。为避免判别器走捷径，正负样本采用 content-matched 构造：对 reference Reinforcement learning improves human-preference or task-specific rewards, while deployment often requires very few sampling steps. Sequential RL followed by distillation can wash out the RL gains, and joint RL plus DMD can constrain the policy toward a frozen base distribution that is not aligned with the target reward.","importance":"This is not a core LLM pretraining paper, but it is relevant to RL-based post-training of generative models. The useful idea is that few-step RL already improves high-level structure, even under specialized rewards, but it leaves low-level artifacts that the main reward may not penalize. Instead of forcing distribution matching to a frozen teacher, the method adds a reward signal for fidelity gaps that are outside the task reward. The same pattern appears in language and multimodal RL: failures often come from unmeasured quality dimensions, not from the absence of optimization pressure on the headline metric.","technical_approach":"AdvFidelity treats a text-conditioned flow-matching generator with a fixed sampling budget as a policy and optimizes few-step rollouts directly. It removes DMD warm-up and explicit distribution-matching losses. The additional component is an online adversarial fidelity reward. A discriminator is trained to rank high-quality references above negatives produced by the current generator. To prevent easy discrimination through semantics, layout, or style, the negatives are content-matched: a reference image is moderately noised and reconstructed by the current generator, so the positive and negative share much of their high-level content. This pushes the discriminator toward low-level artifacts such as residual noise or texture defects. The discriminator is updated online, alternating with the generator, so the reward can track new artifacts that appear as the policy changes. References may come from full-step base-model samples, real target-domain images, or another model covering the target visual domain.","experiments":"The paper instantiates AdvFidelity with LeapAlign and evaluates two backbones across three settings. For FLUX.1-dev, it uses the HPSv2 reward on HPDv2 and GenEval prompts. For Qwen-Image-Edit-2511, it studies virtual dressing with a DINO-similarity reward for garment consistency and realism. The reported setting uses only 4 or 8 sampling steps and no classifier-free guidance. AdvFidelity matches full-step LeapAlign on alignment, task performance, and overall generation quality, while remaining competitive with other full-step RL baselines. The central empirical claim is supported by comparisons under the same base model, reward, and task data: few-step RL improves structure, but the adversarial fidelity reward is needed to reduce low-level artifacts.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 25 to 35 minutes. We apply a dynamic intervention to steering directions obtained by three different methods: difference-in-means vectors from labeled rollouts, gradient optimization-based vectors, and vectors extracted when models reference an anti-reward-hacking mod","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2610_29129.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"在长上下文和工具调用的复杂智能体场景中，模型容易出现奖励作弊（reward hacking）行为，即通过非预期的方式提高代理目标奖励。现有的缓解方法往往成本高昂或会损害模型的正常能力。","importance":"提供了一种在推理阶段低成本检测和缓解奖励作弊的实用方案。通过表征工程（representation engineering）和动态干预，在不显著影响模型连贯性和能力的前提下，有效抑制了复杂场景下的作弊行为，对安全部署 Agent 具有实际指导价值。","technical_approach":"提出在推理时进行潜在空间监控和动态引导（dynamic steering）。首先通过少量标注数据（如均值差向量、梯度优化向量或模型规范引用向量）提取作弊方向。然后使用激活截断（activation capping）技术，仅当激活值在作弊方向上超过设定阈值时才施加干预，从而最小化对其他能力的附带影响。此外，还引入了反作弊模型规范（model spec）作为基线，并从中提取引导向量。","experiments":"在 Qwen3.5-27B 模型上，使用 ImpossibleBench 和 MisTrace 数据集（包含工具调用和长达 80k token 的长上下文）进行实验。结果表明，动态干预能显著减少奖励作弊行为，且相比于在残差流中均匀添加引导向量，动态干预对模型表征的扰动更小，从而保持了模型的正常能力。此外，从代码环境中提取的引导向量能够泛化到自然语言的奖励作弊环境中。","reading_guide":"建议重点阅读 Section 3.5 关于动态干预（特别是激活截断）的具体实现细节，以及 Section 4 中不同引导向量提取方法（均值差、优化、模型规范）的对比实验。关注实验部分关于干预对模型正常能力影响的评估。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"In complex agentic scenarios with long contexts and tool calls, models are prone to reward hacking—increasing proxy objective rewards in unintended ways. While RLVR is highly effective at enhancing the reasoning capabilities of LLMs, it commonly induces \\textit{distribution sharpening}, whereby the policy distribution becomes excessively peaked and rare-yet-correct trajectories are progressively suppressed, thus diminishing sample diversity. ","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2610_29281.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文处理的是 RLVR/GRPO 在提升单次推理正确率时带来的分布变尖问题。模型会更集中地产生高概率、已被强化过的解法，低概率但正确的推理轨迹被压低，导致 Pass@K 尤其是大 K 下的收益下降。这对依赖多样采样和 verifier 选择的 inference-time compute scaling 不利。","importance":"它的价值在于把 RLVR 的一个核心副作用转化为优化几何问题：不是只看奖励均值是否上升，而是看奖励面是否过尖。对预训练研究的直接启发在于 post-train 目标会改变模型的采样分布形态，进而影响模型在多样性、探索性和推理时扩展上的可用性。该工作不是预训练 recipe，但对 pretrain ↔ posttrain 接口有参考意义：如果预训练保留了长尾能力，后续 RLVR 可能通过过强的模式收缩把这部分能力压掉。","technical_approach":"方法称为 Flat-RLVR，核心做法是把 sharpness-aware minimization 的思想接入 GRPO 类 RLVR。标准 GRPO 对同一 prompt 采样一组 rollout，用组内奖励均值和标准差归一化 advantage，然后对 token 级 log-prob ratio 做 clipped policy optimization。Flat-RLVR 在这个目标上引入参数扰动，搜索在邻域内仍能保持较好奖励的平坦解，而不是沿着最陡方向把概率质量集中到少数高奖励轨迹。论文将该思路实例化到 GRPO、DAPO、CISPO。技术上的关键区别是，它不是额外加熵正则或 KL 约束来强行保持分布宽度；相反，它通过优化过程偏向平坦奖励区域，使相邻策略下的候选轨迹不至于被过快压低。论文还给出理论分析，说明 flat-search 会抬升部分次优候选的相对权重，从而平滑输出分布。","experiments":"实验围绕数学推理 RLVR 展开，主要看单次准确率和 Pass@256。论文报告 Flat-RLVR 在 GRPO、DAPO、CISPO 三类基线中都能提升 Pass@256，同时保持 single-attempt accuracy。这个结果符合其问题设定：方法并不是牺牲单次正确率换多样性，而是在保持可用正确率的同时减少分布收缩。提供的全文片段没有包含具体表格数值、模型规模、训练步数、数据规模和扰动半径等细节，因此不宜补充未给出的数字。值得注意的是，论文遵循近期工作去掉 GRPO 中的 KL regularization，理由是 KL 可能限制推理探索。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，约 25–35 分钟。先看 Introduction 和 Figure 1，确认作者如何定义 distribution sharpening，以及它为什么会损害 Pass@K。然后看 Preliminaries 中 GRPO advantage 和 clipped surrogate 的写法，重点确认 Flat-RLVR 是改优化过程，而不是简单加熵正则。方法和理论部分需要看 flat-search 如何影响次优候选的相对概率。实验部分重点看三组：Pass@1 或 single-attempt accuracy、Pass@256、多样性指标。如果有消融，优先看扰动半径、是否去 KL、以及不同 RLVR 基线上的稳定性。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a failure mode of RLVR with GRPO-style training: the policy distribution becomes overly sharp. RLVR improves single-sample correctness by reinforcing high-probability successful trajectories, but it can suppress rare yet correct reasoning paths. This hurts large-K sampling, where deployment relies on generating diverse candidates and selecting with a verifier.","importance":"The useful contribution is the framing of RLVR degradation as an optimization-geometry issue rather than only a reward-design issue. For pretraining researchers, the main lesson is at the pretrain–posttrain interface: post-training can erase useful long-tail behaviors that the base model still has. This matters for inference-time scaling because diversity is not just a decoding artifact; it is shaped by the policy optimization objective.","technical_approach":"Flat-RLVR injects sharpness-aware optimization into GRPO-like RLVR. Standard GRPO samples a group of rollouts for each prompt, normalizes rewards into group-relative advantages, and optimizes a clipped token-level policy objective. Flat-RLVR perturbs parameters and favors solutions whose reward remains stable in a local neighborhood. The method is applied to GRPO, DAPO, and CISPO. Its distinction from entropy bonuses or KL constraints is important: it does not simply force the output distribution to stay broad through an explicit regularizer. Instead, it biases the optimizer toward flatter reward regions, where nearby policies do not collapse probability mass onto a narrow set of trajectories. The paper also gives a theoretical argument that the flat search can lift suboptimal candidates and smooth the induced output distribution.","experiments":"The experiments focus on mathematical reasoning under RLVR. The reported result is consistent across GRPO, DAPO, and CISPO: Flat-RLVR improves Pass@256 while preserving single-attempt accuracy. This is the key empirical claim, because the goal is not to trade off accuracy for diversity, but to reduce distribution sharpening without losing the main RLVR benefit. The provided full-text excerpt does not include concrete table values, model sizes, training budgets, data scale, or perturbation-radius sweeps, so those numbers should be checked in the experimental section before relying on the claims. One implementation detail worth noting is that the setup drops the GRPO KL regularizer, following recent arguments that KL can hinder exploration in reasoning tasks.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 25–35 minutes. signals relate to the models’ explicit agreement behavior.","importance":"The contribution is mainly mechanistic and evaluative rather than a direct pretraining result. Its useful lesson is that activation-level probes on single trajectories can be unstable and may overstate opinion changes. When signals are aggregated over many conversations, a cleaner relation emerges between internal agreement representations and external behavior. This matters for representation probing, multi-agent evaluation, and analyses of post-training behavioral dynamics.","technical_approach":"The setup places two instances of the same model in opposing persona-conditioned stances on subjective binary scenarios. They discuss for 10 rounds and end each response with an A/B decision. The explicit decision token is stripped from future context, so later turns condition on arguments rather than copied labels. For each turn, the authors train a linear classifier on hidden states to predict whether the model’s stated decision agrees with its partner’s decision. This defines an internal agreement representation. At the single-conversation level, the probe signal has high variance across turns and examples. The authors then aggregate agreement signals over conversations at each turn. Externally, the aggregate agreement rate is modeled as a first-order Markov process. Internally, the aggregated representation couples to aggregate behavioral agreement through a power-law relation and appears to lag behind behavioral changes.","experiments":"The study uses five subjective binary datasets: Behavioral Dispositions, ManagerBench, Wealth-seeking, Self-coordination, and Global. It evaluates Ministral-3-14B-Instruct, Qwen3.5-9B, and Gemma-4-12B-it. For each model and dataset, up to 300 scenarios are sampled, and the analysis keeps conversations that begin in disagreement. Conversations run for 10 rounds with greedy decoding. Persona prompts have three paraphrases, with less than 1.5% standard deviation in initial disagreement rate. The main empirical result is a sharp contrast between noisy individual-level internal signals and structured aggregate-level behavior: pooled external agreement follows a simple Markovian pattern, while pooled internal agreement follows a power-law relation to behavior and changes more slowly.","reading_guide":"A skim is enough; 20–30 minutes should cover the main points. 放进同一个信息论框架里比较，并给出可认证的上界与检验程序，而不是停留在类比层面。","importance":"对预训练研究的意义不在于直接改进 loss 或训练 recipe，而在于给 pretrain/post-train 接口提供了一个统一度量。现在很多工作把 ICL、RAG、cache、test-time adaptation、fine-tuning 分开讨论，但这篇文章给出的是一套共同语言：它们都可以看成“用某种记忆通道减少未来 query 的 code length”。这对几个方向都有启发。第一，能更系统地讨论什么时候应该继续预训练、什么时候做检索增强、什么时候做参数更新。第二，它把上下文窗口、参数容量、解码器利用效率这三件事拆开了，便于分析 scaling 时瓶颈到底在存储还是在读取。第三，它给出了 anytime-valid 的 held-out 检验和若干 certificate，这使得“是否值得写入权重”不再只是经验判断。量级上，这更像一个基础分析框架，而不是立刻改变大模型训练范式的工程方法。","technical_approach":"技术核心是一个对“记忆带来的期望编码收益”的精确分解。无论记忆来自上下文还是来自微调后的权重，期望节省的 code length 都被拆成三项：一是记忆中关于 query 的互信息，表示这条通道真正存了多少与预测相关的信息；二是 prior repair，表示即便记忆本身不直接携带 query 信息，也可能修正模型原有输出先验；三是 decoding loss，表示模型虽然持有了记忆，但预测器没有把这些信息完全用出来而造成的损失。这个分解的价值在于把“存了什么”和“用得怎么样”分开。\n\n在 context 侧，作者通过删除部分上下文并重跑模型，定义该部分上下文对 held-out query 的精确 leave-one-out 价值。这个定义只要求模型能输出概率并支持删去部分 context 后重算，不依赖特定架构，因此能覆盖 transformer KV cache、occupancy network 的 feature grid、stereo network 的 cost volume、TabPFN 这类表格 ICL 模型等。\n\n在 weight 侧，作者把 fine-tuning 看成把信息写入固定容量的参数状态，并给出基于容量的上界。关键判断逻辑是：如果 context 的实测收益已经超过任何给定容量权重状态所能达到的上界，就能认证“留在 context 更好”；反过来，如果某个 fine-tune 的收益超过了窗口受限 context 的上界，就能认证“写入权重更好”。当 context 能完整容纳证据时，论文还指出 fine-tune 相比 context 最多只能再补上 context decoder 的那部分 decoding loss，这个结论很有意思，因为它把参数更新的额外收益限制在“读取机制不充分”这一项上，而不是无限制地神化参数记忆。\n\n作者还设计了一个 anytime-valid test：当精确信息项不可得时，用 held-out queries 上的逐步证据累积来比较两种通道，允许在任意停止时刻控制错误率。这一点比单次显著性检验更适合真实系统，因为样本可以边到边测。附录里还把 transformer cache 精确写成一种带 query-dependent addressing 的 weight write，这一步把“上下文状态”和“参数写入”在形式上拉得更近，不只是概念类比。","experiments":"$74","reading_guide":"这篇适合按“定理—可计算量—实验映射”来读，而不是从应用例子切入。\n\n先看 Section 3。这里给出统一的收益分解，是全文最重要的部分。重点抓三件事：code-length saving 的定义、三项分解各自代表什么、为什么它能同时适用于 context 和 weights。读到这里时要特别留意 prior repair 和 decoding loss，因为这两项决定了它不是一个简单的互信息重写。\n\n再看 Section 5。这里是从理论走向决策规则的关键，讲 certificate、容量上界、窗口上界，以及“当 context fits the window 时 fine-tune 最多补上 decoding loss”这一结论。做预训练的人读这一节，会自然联想到参数容量、KV cache、RAG、continued pretraining 之间的边界。\n\nSection 4 值得看作方法可观测性的展示。删除上下文子集并重跑，得到局部证据价值，这个操作很朴素，但解释力很强。occupancy 和 stereo 的例子能帮助理解为什么作者强调‘memory content’和‘decoder use’要分开。\n\nSection 6 看检验程序和实证稳定性。重点不是具体任务，而是 anytime-valid test 如何在未知精确信息项时仍然给出可控错误率的判断。\n\n如果时间有限，建议先读 Introduction、Section 3、Section 5，再挑一个自己熟悉的实验域看 Section 4/6，预计 60–90 分钟能抓住主线。若要深挖，附录里把 transformer cache 写成 weight write 的形式值得细读，这关系到作者为何敢把 ICL 与 fine-tuning 放进同一框架。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks a precise version of a broad question: when new evidence arrives, should a model keep it in context as transient state, or write it into weights as persistent memory? Rather than giving another mechanistic story for in-context learning in a narrow setting, it builds a model-agnostic criterion based on predictive code-length savings on held-out queries. The central move is to compare context memory and weight memory inside the same information-theoretic accounting, then derive certificates and sequential tests for choosing between them.","importance":"Its value for pretraining is conceptual but concrete. It gives a shared metric for things that are often discussed separately: ICL, retrieval, cache-based adaptation, fine-tuning, and parameter updates all become channels that reduce future code length. That matters for the pretrain/post-train interface. It suggests a cleaner way to ask when continued pretraining is justified, when retrieval is enough, and when parameter writing is worth the cost. It also separates storage capacity from decoder efficiency: a model may have access to useful evidence but still fail to exploit it, and that gap is made explicit as decoding loss. This is not a new training recipe, but it is a strong analytical framework for reasoning about memory placement in foundation models.","technical_approach":"$75","experiments":"$76","reading_guide":"$77","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"不是直接改 pretrain recipe 的论文，但它把“上下文记忆 vs 参数记忆”讲成了可检验命题；先看定律和 sequential test，实验数字当弱验证即可。","en":"Not a direct pretraining recipe paper, but it turns “context memory vs weight memory” into a testable statement; read the law and the sequential test first, and treat the empirical wins as secondary evidence."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Leon Chlon","Julie Huang"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["in-context-learning","fine-tuning","information-theory","sequential-testing","mechanistic-analysis"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29419","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.29419","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2610.28992","title":"Language Impairment as a Lens onto Large Language Models","abstract":"Although large language models (LLMs) encode language in highly distributed spaces, they remain vulnerable to focal disruptions---a phenomenon strikingly similar to how localized brain damage causes clinical aphasia in humans. To investigate this theoretical tension, we ask whether LLMs possess a functional structure analogous to the human language system, by introducing a novel mechanistic interpretability framework inspired by aphasia. Through two complementary approaches, classifier-guided le","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-20T23:42:36Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2610_28992.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文的核心问题不是提升模型能力，而是用“语言损伤”这个临床框架去刻画 LLM 内部语言功能的组织方式：如果模型表征真是高度分布式的，为什么局部干预又能诱发很具体的语言崩坏？作者想回答的是，LLM 里是否存在类似人脑语言系统那样可定位的功能回路，以及这些功能到底是模块化分离，还是高度纠缠的分布式结构。","importance":"对预训练研究，它不是直接改 recipe 的论文，但有两层启发。第一，它提供了一种比 perplexity 更细的失效分析视角：不是只看模型变差，而是看它以哪一种语言学方式变差。这对训练动态诊断、能力退化分析、对齐副作用排查都有潜在价值。第二，它把 mechanistic interpretability 和临床语言学接起来，给“功能局部化 vs 分布式冗余”这个老问题提供了可操作实验框架。就与预训练主线的距离而言，这篇更偏机制分析，不是 must-read 类型的训练方法论文，但如果关心模型内部功能组织、损伤鲁棒性、表示可控性，它是可以泛读的。","technical_approach":"方法由两条互补路径组成：lesion 和 steering。\n\nlesion 路径里，作者先基于 AphasiaBank 的临床数据训练一个 aphasia subtype classifier，用来把模型生成文本判成不同失语症风格，比如更接近 Broca 型还是 Wernicke 型。然后系统性地对冻结 LLM 的 attention heads 做消融，观察哪些 head 的去除会把输出推向某一类 aphasia profile。这里 classifier-guided 的意义在于，它不是只看通用质量下降，而是用临床标签去筛选“哪种损伤被诱发了”。作者据此把某些语言功能，如语法流畅性、语义连贯性，映射到较离散的结构回路上。\n\nsteering 路径里，作者收集模型在处理人类 aphasic transcript 与健康对照 transcript 时的 residual stream 激活差异，用两者差向量作为 subtype-specific steering direction。生成时把这个方向加到目标层的激活上，观察模型是否会朝对应 aphasia profile 偏移；反过来，也测试减去该方向能否部分“修复” lesion 造成的语言损伤。这个设计把结构性损伤和连续表示干预并列起来：前者像改硬件，后者像改状态空间。\n\n全文最重要的发现不是“找到了一个 Broca head”这种简单局部化，而是双重结论：一方面，确实能找到相对离散的关键回路和连续方向；另一方面，这些功能之间高度纠缠。不同 aphasia subtype 的 lesion 目标有明显重叠，steering vectors 之间余弦相似度也高。这意味着语言功能既不是完全模块化，也不是均匀弥散，而是带有脆弱关键点的密集分布式组织。\n\n从方法取舍看，这篇工作刻意不用传统困惑度或任务分数做主指标，而是引入临床分类器作为中介表征。这么做的好处是能把“退化类型”结构化；代价是结果会部分受 classifier 训练分布约束。作者也承认这一点，并用 steering 的跨任务泛化来补足。","experiments":"实验数据来自 AphasiaBank，选了两个经典 elicitation task：Cinderella 叙事复述和 sandwich 制作流程描述，分别对应较开放的 narrative speech 与较固定顺序的 procedural speech。模型覆盖 11 个 LLM，来自四个家族：GPT-2 XL、Llama-2-7B/13B-chat、Pythia 1B/1.4B/2.8B/6.9B、Qwen3.5 0.8B/2B/4B/9B。统一使用 greedy decoding，并加 1.25 repetition penalty 避免 base model 自循环。\n\n结果层面，作者报告了几类稳定现象。第一，classifier-guided lesion 比无指导或弱指导的消融更容易诱发目标 aphasia subtype，说明某些 attention heads 的确与特定语言功能失效更相关。第二，steering 能在 intact model 上稳定诱导出对应的 aphasic behavior，而且比 lesion 更能泛化到不同下游任务或提示环境。第三，不同 subtype 之间存在显著结构重叠和方向相似性，支持“dense entanglement”而不是清晰模块边界。\n\n从给出的正文片段看，论文更强调现象一致性和机制解释，而不是追求单一 benchmark 数字。具体到预训练研究者最该注意的，是它把“功能损伤”拆成可分类的语言学维度，并在多模型、多规模上验证这种干预不是某个模型家族的偶然产物。\n\n不过也要看到边界。实验对象主要是中小到中等规模模型，且多为 chat/base 混合；干预对象集中在 attention heads 与 residual steering，尚未覆盖 MLP、跨层组合回路或训练阶段演化。临床分类器本身也可能把某些生成风格偏差误判为 aphasia-like deficit，因此结果更适合当机制探针，而不是严格的认知同构证据。","reading_guide":"泛读建议是先看 Introduction 和 Method，建立作者的问题意识：他们不是在做医疗应用，而是在借 aphasia taxonomy 给 LLM failure mode 命名。然后看 Data and Setup，确认任务、模型族和解码设置，尤其是为什么要用 AphasiaBank 的两个任务。\n\n接着重点看两部分结果：一是 classifier-guided lesion 的定位效果，二是 steering vector 的诱导与“修复”实验。阅读时要特别留意作者如何区分“模型整体变差”和“出现特定类型语言损伤”。如果正文里有 subtype overlap、vector cosine similarity、跨任务泛化的图表，这些最值得看，因为它们直接支撑“局部化存在，但纠缠更强”这个结论。\n\n这篇适合 25 到 40 分钟泛读。若想深一点，可以追 classifier It uses aphasia as a diagnostic lens to study how language functions are organized inside LLMs. The central question is: if language representations are highly distributed, why can focal interventions still trigger specific and recognizable linguistic breakdowns? The authors ask whether LLMs contain functionally localizable language circuits analogous to the human language system, and whether those functions are modular or densely entangled.","importance":"For pretraining researchers, the value is indirect but real. First, it offers a finer failure-analysis framework than perplexity or generic task accuracy. Instead of asking only whether a model degrades, it asks how it degrades linguistically. That can matter for training diagnostics, capability regression analysis, and understanding side effects of alignment or continued training. Second, it connects mechanistic interpretability with clinically grounded language categories, giving a concrete experimental handle on the old tension between local specialization and distributed redundancy. Read as a key--value memory, the layer can be measured one factor set at a time: the pairing of readers to writers is carried only by their shared index, invisible to any quantity computed from one factor set alone. Yet training couples the two factors through their joint update, so we ask what reading them together shows. The","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-21T07:35:23Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2610_29340.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"Transformer 的 MLP 可以看成每个 hidden channel 同时有“reader”（决定何时激活）和“writer”（决定往 residual 写什么）。以往分析常分别看 key/reader 或 value/writer，但 reader 与 writer 的绑定只由共享的 channel index 承载，单边统计量对“重配对”不敏感，容易漏掉训练真正学到的耦合结构。论文要解决的是：如何用一个对重配对敏感、可度量的量来刻画并检验 MLP 中 reader–writer 的耦合是否是训练学出来的，以及这种耦合在训练早期如何形成、在层间如何分布。","importance":"这是机制分析/训练动力学方向，对预训练 recipe 不是直接改法，但对“训练中学到了什么结构”给了一个可操作的测量工具：用 MLP 分支对 residual 的精确 Jacobian，把 reader 与 writer 放在同一个算子里度量耦合，并用“与随机重配对的参照系”来判断耦合是否显著。对做大规模预训练的人，价值主要在两点：1）提供一种诊断信号，能在训练早期观察到结构性相变（sign 先出现、coupling 后增长）；2）提示 token 顺序（自然语言结构）对这种耦合形成是必要条件之一，可能与数据打乱、课程学习、以及训练稳定性诊断相关。整体属于值得 skim 的分析论文；如果你在做训练动态/可解释性工具链，可以更细读。","technical_approach":"关键是把 MLP 视为“读-写耦合的平方算子”。在 pre-norm decoder block 中，MLP 分支读入 z，经扩展得到 hidden feature，再经收缩写回 residual。作者定义每个通道的 reader u_h 为该 feature 在 z 坐标下的梯度方向（effective reader，区别于原始 W_up 行向量），writer v_h 为写回方向（W_down 列向量对应）。重要对称性：对 hidden 维做共同置换（同时置换 W_up 的行与 W_down 的列）不会改变 Jacobian；但只置换 writers（保持 readers 不动）会改变 reader-writer 对应关系，而单边统计量保持不变。于是作者构造对“对应关系”敏感的量：计算 MLP 更新对输入的精确 Jacobian J(x)，利用其与转置的关系（对称-反对称分解）得到每个通道的 signed pairing coordinate m_h，本质上是 writer 与其 own reader 的对齐/反向对齐的带符号度量（文中强调“writer pointing with or against its own reader”）。方法论上还有一个关键点：初始化时通道独立同分布，assignment 可交换，因此“随机重配对”天然给出一个 reference ensemble，用于检验训练后的配对是否显著偏离随机。","experiments":"作者在 FineWeb-EDU 上训练了 3 个 pre-norm decoder 尺寸：29M / 124M / 405M，各 3 个 seed，并与部分 released models 对照测量。主要发现包括：1）训练后的真实 reader-writer 配对在度量上显著超出任意采样的重配对（trained pairing lies beyond every sampled re-pairing）；2）把 writers 置换打乱后继续训练，会“长回”原本 readers（permute 后 regrow toward their own readers），说明耦合不是索引偶然；3）最强的 couples（core）多数是 opposed（writer 与 own reader 反向），而 amplifying（同向）更多出现在堆栈两端；4）sign 在训练早期就出现，且跨 seed 在相似 step/层顺序上出现，但此时 coupling 强度尚未增长；5）在打乱 token 顺序的语料上 sign 不形成，说明与序列结构相关；6）大多数通道的作用是“对抗触发它的 reader”，其影响被后续层的 readers 接走，真正直接影响 logits 的通道主要集中在最后几层。全文片段未给出具体数值曲线/阈值，精读时应看 Sec.5 的图（尤其是 pairing 分布、训练步数对 sign/coupling 的曲线、以及 permute-regrow 实验）。","reading_guide":"泛读建议（30–45 分钟）：1）先看 Sec.2/3 的定义：effective reader、writer、以及为什么“共同置换不变、只置换 writer 会变”是问题核心；2）抓住他们如何从 Jacobian 的对称-反对称分解得到 m_h（Figure 1 往往是最关键的直觉图）；3）直接跳到 Sec.5.2–5.6：重配对对照、permute 后 regrow、sign 形成时间、以及 token 顺序消融；4）如果你关心训练诊断，把“sign 先出现、coupling 后增长”的时间轴与学习率 warmup/数据课程对齐，看看是否能作为早期健康信号。预计精读需要把推导式子手算一遍，确认 m_h 与常见的 feature attribution/线性化分析的关系。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"In transformer MLPs, each hidden channel has a reader (when it fires) and a writer (what it writes back). Prior analyses often inspect readers or writers separately, but the binding between them is carried only by the shared channel index and is invisible to any statistic computed on one factor set alone (it is invariant to re-pairing). The paper asks: how can we measure whether the reader–writer correspondence is a learned, meaningful structure, and how does this coupling emerge during training?","importance":"This is mechanistic/optimization analysis rather than a new pretraining recipe. Its practical value for foundation-model training is as a diagnostic lens: it provides a re-pairing-sensitive metric derived from the exact Jacobian of the MLP update, and it reveals a reproducible early-training phenomenon (the sign of coupling appears before coupling strength grows, and depends on token order). If you build tooling around training dynamics, stability, or interpretability, this is a useful measurement primitive; otherwise it is a solid skim.","technical_approach":"The core move is to treat the pre-norm MLP branch as a square operator on the residual stream and analyze its exact Jacobian J(x). Each channel h is represented as a couple: an effective reader u_h (feature gradient direction in residual coordinates, not merely the raw W_up row) and a writer v_h (the direction written back via W_down). A key symmetry is highlighted: a common permutation of hidden channels (relabeling both readers and writers) leaves the Jacobian unchanged, while permuting writers alone changes the correspondence but preserves any statistic of readers-only or writers-only. To expose the correspondence, the paper compares J(x) with its transpose via the symmetric–skew decomposition, yielding a per-channel signed pairing coordinate m_h that captures whether the writer points with or against its own reader. Initialization provides a natural null: channels are exchangeable, so random re-pairings form a reference ensemble against which trained coupling can be tested.","experiments":"$7b","reading_guide":"Skim plan (30–45 minutes): (1) Read the setup defining effective readers/writers and the permutation invariances—this motivates why single-factor analyses miss the coupling. (2) Focus on how the signed pairing coordinate m_h is derived from the Jacobian symmetry (Figure 1 is likely the fastest intuition). (3) Jump to Sec.5.2–5.6 for the re-pairing baseline, permute-and-regrow test, early-formation timeline, and the token-order ablation. character","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2610_28977.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["stat.ML","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"论文检验一个具体但容易被忽略的问题：Decision-Pretrained invariance，即在 Gaussian、Bernoulli、linear bandit 中，只要两个上下文诱导相同的充分统计量，模型输出的动作概率应保持一致。第二是 martingale property，即后验预测量在进一步观测前应满足鞅性质。DPT 原始形式只预测动作，不能直接测试 reward 相关的 martingale 条件，因此作者构造 DPT′ 和 GDPT，在动作预测外加入 posterior samplers in realistic finite-data, finite-model settings. Prior theory connects DPT to posterior sampling under idealized assumptions. The authors test the behavioral implication directly, rather than relying on regret or decision accuracy as indirect evidence.","importance":"The main value is methodological. It separates two claims that are often conflated: a model can make decisions similar to Thompson Sampling, while its conditional action distribution still fails basic Bayesian consistency checks. This matters for in-context learning, RL pretraining, and algorithmic behavior learned through supervised sequence modeling. The result is mostly a diagnostic negative result, but it gives a useful template for auditing mechanistic claims about pretrained transformers.","technical_approach":"The authors formulate necessary behavioral conditions for a transformer to qualify as a Bayesian posterior sampler. The first condition is sufficient-statistic invariance: contexts that induce the same sufficient statistics in Gaussian, Bernoulli, or linear bandits should lead to the same action distribution. The second condition is a martingale property for posterior predictive quantities. Since the standard DPT only predicts actions, the paper introduces DPT′ and GDPT with an additional reward head, making the martingale condition testable. Each condition is converted into a statistical hypothesis test over model-implied action probabilities. The contribution is therefore an evaluation framework, not a new high-performing ICRL policy.","experiments":"The empirical study spans Gaussian bandits, Bernoulli bandits, and linear bandits, with variations in context length, deployment mode, and context transformations. DPT, DPT′, and GDPT consistently violate sufficient-statistic invariance. DPT′ and GDPT also violate the martingale property. These failures persist even when the models’ decision performance closely tracks Thompson Sampling. agent memory 在强模型时代是否还成立。更具体地说，像 Mem0、Zep 这类“先抽取、再存储、最后检索”的设计型记忆系统，会在问题到来之前决定保留什么信息；而 coding agent 则保留原始历史，让模型自己用工具去搜。作者要检验的核心问题是：当底层模型足够强、上下文也放得下原始记录时，预先设计的 memory abstraction 也救不回丢失的信息。这一点对做预训练和中间训练的人有现实含义：很多看似系统层的优化，其实受限于生成中间表示的模型能力；如果中间表示不可逆，后续更强模型只能在残缺输入上工作。文章规模很小，不能据此下普遍结论，但问题意识是对的。","technical_approach":"方法非常直接，几乎是一个小型对照实验。作者在 LoCoMo 的 conv-26 上跑 21 个 cell：7 种方法乘 3 个模型。方法分两类。第一类是 memory methods：full context、BM25、Mem0、Zep。它们按 Mem0 的问答模板逐题回答，其中 Mem0 和 Zep 需要先构建 memory store。第二类是 coding agents：Pi、Codex、Claude Code。每个问题都开一个新 session，把整段对话作为文件交给 agent，用默认工具自行搜索和推理。\n\n模型强度从 GPT-4o-mini 到 GPT-6 Luna，再到 Claude Opus 5.5。对后两者使用 high reasoning effort。成本统计也比较完整：把 build 阶段、问答阶段、agent loop 中的所有 LLM 调用和 embedding 调用都按 list price 计入，judge 成本排除。作者关心的不只是准确率，而是 cost–accuracy frontier。\n\n这篇文章最有信息量的设计，是把 memory 的 writer 和 reader 拆开。Mem0 和 Zep 默认由同一个模型写 store、再由同一个模型读 retrieval。作者额外做了交叉读取实验：让不同强度的模型去读取别的模型写出的 store。这样就能直接测“记忆上限来自谁”。结果显示，store 的写入质量决定了上限，reader 变强只能小幅利用已有信息，无法恢复写入时被省略掉的事实。这个实验虽然简单，但比单纯比较 end-to-end accuracy 更能说明问题。\n\n需要注意的是，实验设置也有一个偏向 agent 的细节：LoCoMo 官方文件里有照片 caption，agent 能读到，而 memory methods 只拿到 turn text。作者没有回避这一点，反而单独分析了它对结果的贡献。这样至少把优势来源说清楚了，而不是把所有提升都归因于 agent 范式本身。","experiments":"$7c","reading_guide":"这篇适合快速泛读，不需要逐段细抠。先看 Setup，确认它只是单对话、单次运行的小样本实验，不要把结论外推过头。然后直接看三节主结果：‘Agent memory is a costly patch for a weak model’、‘Agent memory has a ceiling’、‘With Claude Opus 5.5, Pi tops the frontier’。其中最值得看的不是 headline，而是 Figure 2 和 Figure 3：前者把 build cost 与 rollout cost 摆在一起，后者把 writer/reader 解耦后的 ceiling 问题讲得很清楚。\n\n如果只花 15 到 20 分钟，抓住三点就够了：弱模型下 Mem0 有局部收益；强模型下 full context 或 agent search 更优；抽取式 memory 的上限由 writer 决定。若要进一步思考，可以把它和长上下文训练、检索增强、以及“是否值得把历史先压缩成结构化状态”联系起来看。文章没有给出普适答案，但把一个常被默认接受的系统设计前提拆开了。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks whether designed agent memory still helps once the underlying model is strong enough to search raw history on its own. Systems like Mem0 and Zep decide in advance what to keep by extracting facts or building a graph, while coding agents keep the original record and use tools to inspect it at question time. The core issue is whether precomputed memory remains an advantage, or becomes an expensive abstraction that throws away information a stronger model could have used directly.","importance":"This is not a core pretraining paper, but it is still relevant to foundation-model research for two reasons. First, it is a clean example of a system trick being overtaken by model capability, which matters for how scaling changes the value of retrieval, compression, and external memory. Second, it isolates a writer ceiling: if a weak model produces the intermediate memory representation, a stronger downstream reader cannot recover information that was never preserved. That is a useful lesson for anyone working on pretraining, mid-training, or latent-state interfaces. If the intermediate representation is lossy, later model improvements only operate on a damaged view of the data. We introduce \\textsc{VeriEvolve}, an evaluator-first evolution framework that constructs this missing evaluation layer before optimizing the agent. From task instruction, VeriEvolve synthesizes instruction-grounded challenge states and an executable evaluator that checks them. During each round of workflow evolution, the evaluator is frozen and serves as the fitness signal. Betwe","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-21T00:13:29Z","category_id":"agent","cover_url":"/covers/2610_29005.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.SE","cs.AI","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的不是“如何让 agent 搜得更好”，而是更前面的瓶颈：在真实、带状态的工具使用任务里，缺少一个可扩展、可执行、可冻结的 fitness signal。对文件系统、数据库、仓库这类 CRUD 任务，正确性往往体现在最终环境状态，而不是最终自然语言回复。人工 verifier 可靠但昂贵，LLM judge 易扩展但不够确定。论文要解决的是：能否先由 agent 自己从任务指令和初始环境中合成任务相关的 challenge states 与 executable evaluator，再用这个 evaluator 去驱动后续 workflow 演化，并在发现 false positive 后继续修 evaluator。","importance":"对基座研究本身，它不是预训练方法论文，但对 post-train 里的 agent self-improvement、tool-use RL、以及“训练信号从哪里来”这个问题有直接启发。价值主要有三点。第一，它把 stateful agent 的学习问题拆成“先造评测器，再做优化”，这比直接在 noisy judge 上做搜索更稳。第二，它强调 evaluator 必须是 deterministic、machine-checkable、并且在一轮搜索内保持 frozen，这和 RL 里 reward stationarity 的要求一致。第三，它给出一个较清楚的结论：不完美 evaluator 也能有效指导搜索，只要它足够 informative，且后续能通过强弱执行器状态差异做回归式修补。对做 tool-use agent 训练、自动化 verifier 生成、以及 environment-grounded reward construction 的人，这篇比单纯再做一个搜索算法更有信息量。","technical_approach":"$80","experiments":"主实验覆盖 MCPMark Filesystem 全部 30 个任务。Stage 1 为 30/30 任务都构造出了 evaluator，共生成 141 个 challenge states，其中 62 个是 adversarial near-miss。合成 evaluator 在这些自建 challenge suites 上与外部 verifier 的一致率是 141/141。更关键的是 held-out 泛化：在后续 executor 产生的 122 个新状态上，一致率是 113/122，说明 evaluator 不只是记住了构造时的样例。用较弱的 GLM-5.2 作为 executor 时，workflow evolution 把外部验证成功数从 round 0 的 6/30 提升到 18/30；用 Claude 作为 executor 时，从 27/30 提升到 30/30。这里能看出 evaluator 的作用更像“把已有能力组织成更可靠 workflow”，对弱模型帮助更明显。作者还报告了 3 个内部接受但外部失败的 GLM-5.2 状态，也就是典型 false positives。三种情况都能通过 strong–weak state differences 触发 revision，并在回归验证后消除原有误判；其中 structure_mirror 任务里，弱执行器在一次 revision 后就到达 externally valid state。扩展到 PostgreSQL 的 21 个标准任务时，15 个合成 evaluator 达到 Stage 2 准入阈值；workflow evolution 关闭了 3 个“强执行器 round 0 能做、弱执行器做不到”的 gap，但剩余 12 个 eligible tasks 在给定预算内都没有内部收敛。这说明方法在文件系统这类可观测、状态结构较清晰的环境里更成熟，到了数据库这类语义和约束更复杂的环境，evaluator synthesis 仍是瓶颈。Playwright、Notion、GitHub 的 stress tests 进一步表明，难点不是单纯搜索，而是可观测性不足、状态抽象不稳定、以及 side effect 难以穷举。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读，但如果你做 agent training 或 verifier synthesis，建议重点看四处。第一，看 Introduction 和 Problem Setting，先把作者的问题定义吃透：他们关心的是 environment state correctness，不是 response judging。第二，看 Stage 1 的 challenge-state construction 和 category adapters，理解 evaluator 为什么能做到 deterministic，而不是停留在 LLM 口头判断。第三，看 Stage 2/3 的接口设计，尤其是为什么 evaluator 必须 frozen、为什么 revision 要靠 strong/weak end-state differences 而不是直接让模型读外部 verifier。第四，看 Filesystem 与 PostgreSQL 的结果对比，这里最能看出方法边界。阅读时可以带着三个问题：哪些约束能从 instruction 中稳定抽取，哪些不能；false positive 的来源更偏 specification missing 还是 state observability missing；如果把这套东西接到 RL 或 continual self-improvement 里，外部 verifier 只做 admission 和 audit 是否足够。预计 20–30 分钟能抓住主线，若要深挖则重点盯 challenge suite 构造、held-out agreement、以及 revision case studies。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a bottleneck that sits before search itself: realistic stateful tool-use tasks lack a scalable, executable, and stable fitness signal. In filesystem, database, repository, or productivity workflows, correctness is defined by the final environment state rather than the final natural-language response. Human-written verifiers are reliable but expensive; LLM judges are scalable but not deterministic enough. The central question is whether an agent can first synthesize task-grounded challenge states and an executable evaluator from the instruction and initial environment, then use that evaluator to drive workflow evolution, and later repair the evaluator when false positives are exposed.","importance":"This is not a pretraining paper, but it is relevant to post-training for tool-use agents, environment-grounded reward construction, and self-improving agent loops. Its main contribution is conceptual and procedural. First, it reframes agent improvement as evaluator construction before optimization, which is often the missing layer in stateful tasks. Second, it argues that the evaluator should be deterministic, machine-checkable, and frozen within each search phase, closely matching the need for a stationary reward signal in RL-style optimization. Third, it shows that an incomplete evaluator can still be useful if it is informative enough to guide search and can later be revised using differences between strong and weak executor end states. For people working on tool-use RL, verifier generation, or automated reward design, this is more informative than another search heuristic paper.","technical_approach":"$81","experiments":"$82","reading_guide":"$83","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"提出 Evaluator-First 的 Agent 进化框架，通过合成反事实测试用例来迭代评估器，对构建高质量的 Agent 训练反馈信号有启发。","en":"Proposes an Evaluator-First agent evolution framework, iterating the evaluator by synthesizing counterfactual test cases; inspiring for building high-quality feedback signals for agent training."},"metadata":{"institutions":["University of Illinois Urbana-Champaign","aiXplain","Affiliation:University of Illinois Urbana-Champaign","Affiliation:aiXplain"],"authors":["Yueshen Li","Kamer Ali Yuksel","Gokhan Tur","Dilek Hakkani-Tür","João Pedro Maia"],"notable_authors":["Gokhan Tur","Dilek Hakkani-Tür"],"tags":["Agent Evolution","Evaluator Synthesis","Self-Improvement","Stateful Tasks"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29005","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.29005","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2610.29303","title":"ExpEvolve: Structuring and Reusing Experience for Open-Ended Discovery","abstract":"Evolutionary coding agents such as AlphaEvolve are one of the notable successes in using Large Language Models (LLMs) to build AI Scientists. In this work, w","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-21T07:00:23Z","category_id":"agent","cover_url":"/covers/2610_29303.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.CL","stat.ML"],"summary":{"core_problem":"在开放式“进化式编码发现”（不断生成/变异程序、用外部评估器打分）的循环里，经验很难被有效复用：LLM 往往在低层细节（超参、实现微调）上做局部搜索，缺少能跨代累积的“想法/机制”级控制变量，导致探索效率低、容易陷入重复试错。论文要解决的是：如何从已评估的程序与分数中抽取可复用的高层 idea factors，并用它们来驱动后续探索-利用的决策。","importance":"对预训练本身（数据/优化/recipe）不是直接贡献，更像是面向“AI scientist / 开放式发现”的 agent 搜索方法。但它给预训练研究的启发点在于：1）把“经验复用”从文本记忆/总结提升到可操作的因子化控制变量，类似把训练动态中的有效改动抽象成可复用的 recipe 片段；2）用可检验的判别任务（预测程序对的优劣）来筛选可用的高层因子，避免纯主观总结；3）用 bandit 做 idea 级调度，提供一种比启发式 mutation 更稳定的探索策略。整体属于可泛化的方法论启发，量级中等，偏 skim。","technical_approach":"核心设计是“idea 抽取 + idea 控制”。(1) 形式化：把进化式发现写成 POMDP，隐藏状态是问题机制 θ_sci，动作是提出程序 p_t，观测是评估分数 y_t。定义 idea 为决定分数差异的高层机制属性（如构造家族、优化策略）。文中用定理刻画：不识别 idea 时，程序之间不可比较，搜索退化为近似穷举；而识别 idea 需要足够的对比性探索（有/无某 idea 的程序都要出现）。(2) idea factors 发现：在已评估程序档案上，寻找能解释“哪个程序更好”的高层因素。实现上基于 COAT（文中引用的框架），把候选因素当作可解释特征，目标是对任意程序对 (p_i, p_j) 预测哪一个分数更高；能稳定提升对比预测的因素被“admit”为 idea factor。这里的关键取舍是：用 pairwise ranking 作为训练/筛选信号，比直接回归分数更鲁棒，也更贴合“差异解释”。(3) idea-level 控制：把每个已发现的 idea factor 当作一个 bandit arm，在生成新候选程序时选择要强调/变异的 idea（利用），同时保留随机探索槽位；当搜索进入平台期时触发 idea-guided exploration，强制 LLM 去探索在当前 top 程序中不常见的 idea，以满足前述“对比性探索”的条件。整体上，它把进化搜索的控制粒度从 token/patch 级提升到“可复用的机制因子”级。","experiments":"实验设置强调“小预算开放式发现”：在 11 个开放式数学发现任务上，总评估预算 200 个程序；两种代际划分：20 代×每代 10 个、10 代×每代 20 个。以 SCS 作为 backbone，并与 SCS、ShinkaEvolve 等对比。结果描述为：在多数设置下，ExpEvolve 在更小预算时取得更高最终 best-so-far，最高可到 5.0× 的提升（文中表述为 up to 5.0×，但给出的全文片段未包含逐任务绝对分数/方差，建议查 Sec.5 的主表）。分析实验包括：发现的 idea factors 在“对档案程序排序”上优于用代码长度等简单 proxy（11/11 任务）；把 ExpEvolve 集成进 OpenRSI，在 MLS-Bench 的 6 个任务上，mean final best-so-far 全部提升。若要判断稳健性，需要重点看：不同 seed 方差、bandit/探索策略消融、以及 idea factor 数量随时间的增长曲线（这些通常在分析小节/附录）。","reading_guide":"泛读建议（约 25–35 分钟）：1）先看 Sec.3 的 控制”还是“额外探索预算/plateau 触发”。如果只想拿走可迁移的工程做法：重点抄 bandit 调度 + plateau 触发的探索策略，以及用 pairwise ranking 来筛选可复用经验因子。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Open-ended evolutionary coding agents accumulate many scored programs, but they rarely reuse experience at the right abstraction level. LLM-guided mutations drift toward low-level tweaks and lack a persistent control unit comparable to human “ideas”. The paper targets a concrete gap: extract high-level idea factors from the archive of (program, score) pairs and use them as explicit control variables to steer exploration vs. exploitation in subsequent generations.","importance":"This is not a pretraining-recipe paper; it sits in the AI-scientist / open-ended search space. Realizing the benefits of these harnesses depends on how effectively the underlying model can make use of them, motivating reinforcement learning directly through the harness. However, existing approaches typically train through a single fixed harness, which can lead to specialization and limited transfer across harness configurations. runs."},"why_scales":{"zh":"这个现象不是小模型特有的超参偶然性，而是优化动力学里的分布切换问题。MT 引入后，梯度噪声尺度、最优步长和参数所在盆地都会变；若 LR 已经提前衰减，模型对新分布的可塑性不足，只会把 MT 当作低步长微调，容易欠适配。反过来，若在 MT 引入后维持较高 LR，再在末段衰减，模型先完成分布重定位，再做收敛压噪。这个机制依赖的是 SGD/Adam 类优化在非平稳数据分布下的通用行为，不依赖某个具体模型宽度，因此放大 100 倍算力后更可能增强而不是消失。论文已在 dense 与 MoE 上都观察到同向结果，也降低了架构特异性导致秩反转的风险。","en":"This is not a proxy-scale hyperparameter accident but a distribution-shift optimization effect. Once MT data is introduced, the gradient noise scale, effective step size, and basin location all change. If LR has already decayed, the model lacks plasticity and treats MT as low-step-size polishing, leading to under-adaptation. If LR stays high through MT introduction and decays only near the end, the model first relocates to the new distribution and only then contracts noise for convergence. This mechanism is a generic property of SGD/Adam-like training under non-stationary data, not a width-specific artifact, so it should strengthen rather than wash out at 100x scale. The paper also shows the same directional effect in both dense and MoE settings, reducing architecture-specific rank-reversal risk."},"plan":{"goal":{"zh":"验证“数据调度必须与 LR 调度联合设计”是否在 300M→1.3B proxy ladder 上保持同序，并找出一个可迁移到大基座的 mid-train 退火模板。","en":"Validate that data schedules must be co-designed with LR schedules across a 300M→1.3B proxy ladder, and extract a transferable mid-train annealing template for larger base models."},"data_sources":{"zh":"使用同一 PT 语料作为底座，构造一个明确更高质量或更目标化的 MT 子集；模型做 300M、700M、1.3B 三档 Iso-FLOPs。总 token 固定，MT token 占比固定为后段 10%–20%。","en":"Use a fixed PT corpus as the base distribution and construct a clearly higher-quality or more target-aligned MT subset; train 300M, 700M, and 1.3B models under an Iso-FLOPs ladder. PPT changes the conditional structure: when predicting the latter half of a window, the model can directly read semantic counterparts in another language, inducing stronger cross-lingual alignment and retrieval-like use. But most downstream inputs are monolingual documents; if training ends in the PPT distribution, capacity remains allocated to a conditioning structure absent at deployment. PPT→SPT is therefore a representation-then-rematching recipe: first learn coupling, then restore the deployment distribution. This mechanism is only weakly tied to model size and often becomes more stable at larger scale, because larger models can preserve alignment while re-adapting monolingual LM behavior without as much objective interference."},"plan":{"goal":{"zh":"验证“PPT 的价值在阶段顺序，而非单阶段替代 SPT”这一点，并测量该顺序在低资源与高资源语言对上是否都稳定。","en":"Validate that the value of PPT lies in stage ordering rather than replacing SPT outright, and test whether the ordering is stable in both low-resource and high-resource language pairs."},"data_sources":{"zh":"选两组语言对：一组高资源（如 En-Fr），一组低资源（如 En-Sw）。每组都从同一语料池构造 SPT 与 PPT 两种视图。模型做 350M 与 1.3B 两档，context 固定 2K，总 token 预算固定；第二阶段占总预算约 10%–20%。","en":"Use two language pairs: one high-resource (e.g., En-Fr) and one low-resource (e.g., En-Sw). For each pair, derive both SPT and PPT views from the same corpus pool. Train 350M and 1.3B models with fixed 2K context and fixed total token budget; allocate roughly 10%–20% of tokens to stage two."},"steps":[{"zh":"步骤 1：四组主对照：SPT-only、PPT-only、PPT→SPT、SPT→PPT。严格匹配总 token、batch、optimizer、语言配比与平行语料覆盖率。","en":"Step 1: Run four main conditions: SPT-only, PPT-only, PPT→SPT, and SPT→PPT. Strictly match total tokens, batch size, optimizer, language mix, and parallel-data coverage."},{"zh":"步骤 2：加入一个关键机制消融：固定两阶段总预算，只改变第二阶段长度（如 5%、10%、20%），看 PPT→SPT 的收益是否来自“分布回配”而非单纯多看一遍数据；同时测一个 cross-lingual context utilization probe。","en":"Step 2: Add a key mechanism ablation: keep total two-stage budget fixed and vary only stage-two length (e.g., 5%, 10%, 20%) to test whether PPT→SPT gains come from distribution rematching rather than simply seeing data twice; also include a cross-lingual context utilization probe."}],"eval_signal":{"zh":"主信号看单语 held-out PPL、跨语言条件 PPL 差值、翻译集 BLEU/ChrF，以及多语理解平均分；机制信号看在“给定另一语言前缀”时的 loss 降幅，与去掉该前缀后的性能回落。","en":"Primary signals: monolingual held-out PPL, cross-lingual conditional PPL gap, translation BLEU/ChrF, and average multilingual understanding score; mechanism signal: loss reduction when the paired-language prefix is provided, and the drop when that prefix is removed."},"success_criteria":{"zh":"若 PPT-only 在 cross-lingual probe 上更强但单语理解不稳，而 PPT→SPT 在两档模型、两组语言对上都优于 SPT-only，且平均多语理解与翻译至少各提升一个统计显著幅度（如 >1% 相对或超过 95% bootstrap CI），则支持假设。若 SPT→PPT 与 PPT→SPT 无稳定差异，或 PPT-only 已全面最好，则核心机制不成立。","en":"Support the hypothesis if PPT-only is stronger on the cross-lingual probe but unstable on monolingual understanding, while PPT→SPT beats SPT-only on both model scales and both language pairs with statistically reliable gains in both multilingual understanding and translation (e.g., >1% relative or beyond 95% bootstrap CI). If SPT→PPT matches PPT→SPT without stable differences, or PPT-only dominates everywhere, the core mechanism is not supported."},"compute_tier":"small"},"risk":{"zh":"最大边界风险是平行语料质量与覆盖率。若对齐噪声高，PPT 可能只是在学坏的检索捷径；若语言对过近或评测过度偏翻译，可能高估 PPT 的普适价值。","en":"The main boundary risk is parallel-data quality and coverage. If alignment noise is high, PPT may learn brittle retrieval shortcuts; if the language pair is too easy or evaluation is too translation-heavy, the generality of the effect may be overstated."},"source_paper_ids":["2610.28838"],"scaling_confidence":0.81}],"feishu_doc_url":"https://bytedance.larkoffice.com/docx/UPDxd3Pwnofe8JxiJgRcXRaen5H","feishu_doc_id":"UPDxd3Pwnofe8JxiJgRcXRaen5H"},"dates":"$0:f:0:1:1:children:0:props:children:0:0:props:dates","currentDate":"2026-10-23"}]}]}]
